# II Kings

מלכים ב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings)

סיפורי הנביא אלישע וניסיו, דעיכתן של ממלכות ישראל ויהודה וחורבן בית המקדש.

Stories and miracles of the prophet Elisha, the decline of Israel’s kingdoms, and the Temple’s destruction.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 719, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Metsudah Tanach series, Lakewood, N.J (Segments: 719, License: unknown)


## Chapter 1

###### II Kings 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:1)


וַיִּפְשַׁ֤ע מוֹאָב֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַחֲרֵ֖י מ֥וֹת אַחְאָֽב׃


Moav rebelled against Yisroel after Achov’s death.


###### II Kings 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:2)


וַיִּפֹּ֨ל אֲחַזְיָ֜ה בְּעַ֣ד הַשְּׂבָכָ֗ה בַּעֲלִיָּת֛וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּשֹׁמְר֖וֹן וַיָּ֑חַל וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח מַלְאָכִ֗ים וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲלֵהֶם֙ לְכ֣וּ דִרְשׁ֗וּ בְּבַ֤עַל זְבוּב֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י עֶקְר֔וֹן אִם־אֶחְיֶ֖ה מֵחֳלִ֥י זֶֽה׃{ס}


[Then] Achazyohu fell through the lattice in his loft that was in Shomron, and he became ill. He sent messengers [to Ekron] and said to them, “Go inquire of Baal-Zevuv, the god of Ekron, if I will live [recover] from this illness.”


###### II Kings 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:3)


וּמַלְאַ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֗ה דִּבֶּר֙ אֶל־אֵלִיָּ֣ה הַתִּשְׁבִּ֔י ק֣וּם עֲלֵ֔ה לִקְרַ֖את מַלְאֲכֵ֣י מֶֽלֶךְ־שֹׁמְר֑וֹן וְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם הֲֽמִבְּלִ֤י אֵין־אֱלֹהִים֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַתֶּם֙ הֹֽלְכִ֔ים לִדְרֹ֕שׁ בְּבַ֥עַל זְב֖וּב אֱלֹהֵ֥י עֶקְרֽוֹן׃


An angel of Adonoy then spoke to Eliyohu, the Tishbi, “Arise [and] go up to meet the messengers of the king of Shomron, and speak to them [saying], ‘Is there no God in Yisroel that you are going to inquire of Baal-Zevuv, the god of Ekron?


###### II Kings 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:4)


וְלָכֵן֙ כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הַמִּטָּ֞ה אֲשֶׁר־עָלִ֥יתָ שָּׁ֛ם לֹא־תֵרֵ֥ד מִמֶּ֖נָּה כִּ֣י מ֣וֹת תָּמ֑וּת וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ אֵלִיָּֽה׃


Therefore thus said Adonoy, “[From] the bed you have gone up onto, you will not go down from it for you will definitely die.’” And so Eliyohu departed.


###### II Kings 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:5)


וַיָּשׁ֥וּבוּ הַמַּלְאָכִ֖ים אֵלָ֑יו וַיֹּ֥אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מַה־זֶּ֥ה שַׁבְתֶּֽם׃


The messengers returned to him and he said to them, “Why have you returned?”


###### II Kings 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:6)


וַיֹּאמְר֨וּ אֵלָ֜יו אִ֣ישׁ**׀** עָלָ֣ה לִקְרָאתֵ֗נוּ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלֵ֘ינוּ֮ לְכ֣וּ שׁ֘וּבוּ֮ אֶל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אֲשֶׁר־שָׁלַ֣ח אֶתְכֶם֒ וְדִבַּרְתֶּ֣ם אֵלָ֗יו כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הֲֽמִבְּלִ֤י אֵין־אֱלֹהִים֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַתָּ֣ה שֹׁלֵ֔חַ לִדְרֹ֕שׁ בְּבַ֥עַל זְב֖וּב אֱלֹהֵ֣י עֶקְר֑וֹן לָ֠כֵ֠ן הַמִּטָּ֞ה אֲשֶׁר־עָלִ֥יתָ שָּׁ֛ם לֹא־תֵרֵ֥ד מִמֶּ֖נָּה כִּי־מ֥וֹת תָּמֽוּת׃


And they said to him, “Adonoy man came up to [meet] us and said to us, ‘Go’ return to the king who sent you and speak to him [saying], this is what Adonoy has said, ‘Is there no God in Yisroel that you send to inquire of Baal-Zevuv’ the god of Ekron? Therefore the bed on which you have gone up you will not go down from it for you will definitely die.’”


###### II Kings 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:7)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם מֶ֚ה מִשְׁפַּ֣ט הָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָלָ֖ה לִקְרַאתְכֶ֑ם וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵיכֶ֔ם אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


And he [the king] spoke to them, “What is the description of this man who came up to meet you and spoke to you these words?”


###### II Kings 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:8)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֗יו אִ֚ישׁ בַּ֣עַל שֵׂעָ֔ר וְאֵז֥וֹר ע֖וֹר אָז֣וּר בְּמׇתְנָ֑יו וַיֹּאמַ֕ר אֵלִיָּ֥ה הַתִּשְׁבִּ֖י הֽוּא׃


And they said to him, “He was a hairy man and was girded with a leather belt around his waist.” He said, “He is Eliyohu, the Tishbi


###### II Kings 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:9)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח אֵלָ֛יו שַׂר־חֲמִשִּׁ֖ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֑יו וַיַּ֣עַל אֵלָ֗יו וְהִנֵּה֙ יֹשֵׁב֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הָהָ֔ר וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלָ֔יו אִ֚ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ דִּבֶּ֖ר רֵֽדָה׃


And he sent to him an officer over fifty [men] with his fifty [men]. He [the officer] went up to him, and there he [Eliyohu] was sitting at the top of a mountain. He said to him, “Man of God, the king said, ‘Come down.’”


###### II Kings 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:10)


וַיַּעֲנֶ֣ה אֵלִיָּ֗הוּ וַיְדַבֵּר֮ אֶל־שַׂ֣ר הַחֲמִשִּׁים֒ וְאִם־אִ֤ישׁ אֱלֹהִים֙ אָ֔נִי תֵּ֤רֶד אֵשׁ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְתֹאכַ֥ל אֹתְךָ֖ וְאֶת־חֲמִשֶּׁ֑יךָ וַתֵּ֤רֶד אֵשׁ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַתֹּ֥אכַל אֹת֖וֹ וְאֶת־חֲמִשָּֽׁיו׃


Eliyohu replied and spoke to the officer of the fifty [men]. “If I am really a man of God, let fire come down from heaven and consume you and your fifty men.” A fire came down from heaven and consumed him and his fifty [men].


###### II Kings 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:11)


וַיָּ֜שׇׁב וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח אֵלָ֛יו שַׂר־חֲמִשִּׁ֥ים אַחֵ֖ר וַחֲמִשָּׁ֑יו וַיַּ֙עַן֙ וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלָ֔יו אִ֚ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ מְהֵרָ֥ה רֵֽדָה׃


Once again he sent to him another officer of fifty [men] with his fifty [men], and he raised his voice and spoke to him, “Man of God, thus has the king spoken ‘Make it fast [and] come down.’”


###### II Kings 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:12)


וַיַּ֣עַן אֵלִיָּה֮ וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶם֒ אִם־אִ֤ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים֙ אָ֔נִי תֵּ֤רֶד אֵשׁ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְתֹאכַ֥ל אֹתְךָ֖ וְאֶת־חֲמִשֶּׁ֑יךָ וַתֵּ֤רֶד אֵשׁ־אֱלֹהִים֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַתֹּ֥אכַל אֹת֖וֹ וְאֶת־חֲמִשָּֽׁיו׃


And Eliyohu replied and said to them, “If I am really a man of God, let fire come down from heaven and consume you and your fifty [men].” A fire of God came down from heaven and consumed him and his fifty men.


###### II Kings 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:13)


וַיָּ֗שׇׁב וַיִּשְׁלַ֛ח שַׂר־חֲמִשִּׁ֥ים שְׁלִשִׁ֖ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֑יו וַיַּ֡עַל וַיָּבֹא֩ שַׂר־הַחֲמִשִּׁ֨ים הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֜י וַיִּכְרַ֥ע עַל־בִּרְכָּ֣יו**׀** לְנֶ֣גֶד אֵלִיָּ֗הוּ וַיִּתְחַנֵּ֤ן אֵלָיו֙ וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלָ֔יו אִ֚ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים תִּיקַר־נָ֣א נַפְשִׁ֗י וְנֶ֨פֶשׁ עֲבָדֶ֥יךָֽ אֵ֛לֶּה חֲמִשִּׁ֖ים בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ׃


Once again, he sent a third officer of fifty [men] with his fifty [men]. The third officer went up and came, and bowed on his knees opposite Eliyohu and begged him and said to him, “Man of God, let my life be precious, and the lives of your servants, these fifty men, be [precious] in your eyes.


###### II Kings 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:14)


הִ֠נֵּ֠ה יָ֤רְדָה אֵשׁ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַ֠תֹּ֠אכַל אֶת־שְׁנֵ֞י שָׂרֵ֧י הַחֲמִשִּׁ֛ים הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים וְאֶת־חֲמִשֵּׁיהֶ֑ם וְעַתָּ֕ה תִּיקַ֥ר נַפְשִׁ֖י בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


Behold, a fire has come down from heaven, and consumed the first two officers of the fifty [men], and their fifty [men]. And now, let my life be precious in your eyes.”


###### II Kings 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:15)


וַיְדַבֵּ֞ר מַלְאַ֤ךְ יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־אֵ֣לִיָּ֔הוּ רֵ֣ד אוֹת֔וֹ אַל־תִּירָ֖א מִפָּנָ֑יו וַיָּ֛קׇם וַיֵּ֥רֶד אוֹת֖וֹ אֶל־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


And the angel of Adonoy said to Eliyohu, “Go down with him, do not be afraid of him.” He then got up and went down with him to the king.


###### II Kings 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:16)


וַיְדַבֵּ֨ר אֵלָ֜יו כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה יַ֜עַן אֲשֶׁר־שָׁלַ֣חְתָּ מַלְאָכִים֮ לִדְרֹשׁ֮ בְּבַ֣עַל זְבוּב֮ אֱלֹהֵ֣י עֶקְרוֹן֒ הֲֽמִבְּלִ֤י אֵין־אֱלֹהִים֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לִדְרֹ֖שׁ בִּדְבָר֑וֹ לָ֠כֵ֠ן הַמִּטָּ֞ה אֲשֶׁר־עָלִ֥יתָ שָּׁ֛ם לֹא־תֵרֵ֥ד מִמֶּ֖נָּה כִּי־מ֥וֹת תָּמֽוּת׃


And he spoke to him [the king], “Thus Adonoy has said, ‘Because you sent messengers to inquire of Baal-Zevuv, god of Ekron, is there no God in Yisroel to inquire of His word? Therefore, from the bed you have gone up onto, you will not get down from it, because you will definitely die.’”


###### II Kings 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:17)


וַיָּ֜מׇת כִּדְבַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֣ה**׀** אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֣ר אֵלִיָּ֗הוּ וַיִּמְלֹ֤ךְ יְהוֹרָם֙ תַּחְתָּ֔יו{פ}
בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שְׁתַּ֔יִם לִיהוֹרָ֥ם בֶּן־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה כִּ֛י לֹא־הָ֥יָה ל֖וֹ בֵּֽן׃{ס}


And he died in accordance with the word of Adonoy that Eliyohu had spoken, and Yehorom reigned in his place, in the second year of Yehorom, son of Yehoshophot, king of Yehudah, for he had no son.


###### II Kings 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/1:18)


וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י אֲחַזְיָ֖הוּ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה הֲלוֹא־הֵ֣מָּה כְתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


The rest of the events of Achazyohu [and] what he did are recorded in the Book of Chronicles of the kings of Yisroel.



## Chapter 2

###### II Kings 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:1)


וַיְהִ֗י בְּהַעֲל֤וֹת יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־אֵ֣לִיָּ֔הוּ בַּֽסְעָרָ֖ה הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַיֵּ֧לֶךְ אֵלִיָּ֛הוּ וֶאֱלִישָׁ֖ע מִן־הַגִּלְגָּֽל׃


And it happened when Adonoy was about to bring up Eliyohu to heaven in a mighty wind, Eliyohu and Elisha traveled from Gilgol.


###### II Kings 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:2)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ אֵלִיָּ֨הוּ אֶל־אֱלִישָׁ֜ע שֵֽׁב־נָ֣א פֹ֗ה כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ שְׁלָחַ֣נִי עַד־בֵּֽית־אֵ֔ל וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֱלִישָׁ֔ע חַי־יְהֹוָ֥ה וְחֵֽי־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ אִם־אֶעֶזְבֶ֑ךָּ וַיֵּרְד֖וּ בֵּֽית־אֵֽל׃


Eliyohu said to Elisha, “Please remain here for Adonoy has sent me to Beis Eil.” And Elisha said, “As Adonoy lives, and as your soul lives, I will not leave you.” And they went down to Beis Eil.


###### II Kings 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:3)


וַיֵּצְא֨וּ בְנֵי־הַנְּבִיאִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־בֵּֽית־אֵל֮ אֶל־אֱלִישָׁע֒ וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֔יו הֲיָדַ֕עְתָּ כִּ֣י הַיּ֗וֹם יְהֹוָ֛ה לֹקֵ֥חַ אֶת־אֲדֹנֶ֖יךָ מֵעַ֣ל רֹאשֶׁ֑ךָ וַיֹּ֛אמֶר גַּם־אֲנִ֥י יָדַ֖עְתִּי הֶחֱשֽׁוּ׃


They came out — the disciples of the prophets — who were in Beis Eil [came out] to Elisha, and said to him, “Do you know that today Adonoy is taking your master from upon your head?” He said, “I also know it! Be quiet.”


###### II Kings 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:4)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ ל֨וֹ אֵלִיָּ֜הוּ אֱלִישָׁ֣ע**׀** שֵֽׁב־נָ֣א פֹ֗ה כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ שְׁלָחַ֣נִי יְרִיח֔וֹ וַיֹּ֕אמֶר חַי־יְהֹוָ֥ה וְחֵֽי־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ אִם־אֶעֶזְבֶ֑ךָּ וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ יְרִיחֽוֹ׃


And Eliyohu said to him, “Elisha, please remain here, for Adonoy has sent me to Yericho.” And he said, “As Adonoy lives, and as your soul lives, I will not leave you.” And they came to Yericho.


###### II Kings 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:5)


וַיִּגְּשׁ֨וּ בְנֵי־הַנְּבִיאִ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־בִּֽירִיחוֹ֮ אֶל־אֱלִישָׁע֒ וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֔יו הֲיָדַ֕עְתָּ כִּ֣י הַיּ֗וֹם יְהֹוָ֛ה לֹקֵ֥חַ אֶת־אֲדֹנֶ֖יךָ מֵעַ֣ל רֹאשֶׁ֑ךָ וַיֹּ֛אמֶר גַּם־אֲנִ֥י יָדַ֖עְתִּי הֶחֱשֽׁוּ׃


They approached — the disciples of the prophets who were in Yericho—Elisha and they said to him, “Do you know that today Adonoy is taking you master from upon your head?” He said, “I also know it! Be quiet.”


###### II Kings 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:6)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ ל֨וֹ אֵלִיָּ֜הוּ שֵֽׁב־נָ֣א פֹ֗ה כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ שְׁלָחַ֣נִי הַיַּרְדֵּ֔נָה וַיֹּ֕אמֶר חַי־יְהֹוָ֥ה וְחֵֽי־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ אִם־אֶעֶזְבֶ֑ךָּ וַיֵּלְכ֖וּ שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃


And Eliyohu said to him, “Please remain here, for Adonoy has sent me to the Yardein.” And he said, “As Adonoy lives, and as your soul lives, I will not leave you.” And so the two of them went.


###### II Kings 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:7)


וַחֲמִשִּׁ֨ים אִ֜ישׁ מִבְּנֵ֤י הַנְּבִיאִים֙ הָֽלְכ֔וּ וַיַּעַמְד֥וּ מִנֶּ֖גֶד מֵרָח֑וֹק וּשְׁנֵיהֶ֖ם עָמְד֥וּ עַל־הַיַּרְדֵּֽן׃


And fifty men of the disciples of the prophets went and stood opposite them at a distance; and the two of them stood at the Yardein.


###### II Kings 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:8)


וַיִּקַּח֩ אֵלִיָּ֨הוּ אֶת־אַדַּרְתּ֤וֹ וַיִּגְלֹם֙ וַיַּכֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַמַּ֔יִם וַיֵּחָצ֖וּ הֵ֣נָּה וָהֵ֑נָּה וַיַּעַבְר֥וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֖ם בֶּחָרָבָֽה׃


Eliyohu took his cloak and rolled it up, and struck the water, and it split to this side and that side; then the two of them crossed over on dry land.


###### II Kings 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:9)


וַיְהִ֣י כְעׇבְרָ֗ם וְאֵ֨לִיָּ֜הוּ אָמַ֤ר אֶל־אֱלִישָׁע֙ שְׁאַל֙ מָ֣ה אֶעֱשֶׂה־לָּ֔ךְ בְּטֶ֖רֶם אֶלָּקַ֣ח מֵעִמָּ֑ךְ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֱלִישָׁ֔ע וִ֥יהִי נָ֛א פִּֽי־שְׁנַ֥יִם בְּרוּחֲךָ֖ אֵלָֽי׃


As they were crossing Eliyohu said to Elisha, “Ask what I shall do for you before I am taken from you.” Elisha said, “Please let there be a double amount of your spirit upon me.”


###### II Kings 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:10)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר הִקְשִׁ֣יתָ לִשְׁא֑וֹל אִם־תִּרְאֶ֨ה אֹתִ֜י לֻקָּ֤ח מֵֽאִתָּךְ֙ יְהִי־לְךָ֣ כֵ֔ן וְאִם־אַ֖יִן לֹ֥א יִהְיֶֽה׃


And he said, “You have made a difficult request. If you witness my departure from you, it will be so for you, and if not, it will not be.”


###### II Kings 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:11)


וַיְהִ֗י הֵ֣מָּה הֹלְכִ֤ים הָלוֹךְ֙ וְדַבֵּ֔ר וְהִנֵּ֤ה רֶֽכֶב־אֵשׁ֙ וְס֣וּסֵי אֵ֔שׁ וַיַּפְרִ֖דוּ בֵּ֣ין שְׁנֵיהֶ֑ם וַיַּ֙עַל֙ אֵ֣לִיָּ֔הוּ בַּֽסְעָרָ֖ה הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


As they were walking and conversing, behold a fiery chariot and fiery horses [appeared] and separated the two of them. And Eliyohu ascended to heaven in a mighty wind.


###### II Kings 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:12)


וֶאֱלִישָׁ֣ע רֹאֶ֗ה וְה֤וּא מְצַעֵק֙ אָבִ֣י**׀** אָבִ֗י רֶ֤כֶב יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וּפָ֣רָשָׁ֔יו וְלֹ֥א רָאָ֖הוּ ע֑וֹד וַֽיַּחֲזֵק֙ בִּבְגָדָ֔יו וַיִּקְרָעֵ֖ם לִשְׁנַ֥יִם קְרָעִֽים׃


Elisha saw and was crying, “My father, my father! Chariot of Yisroel and its riders!” And he did not see him any more. He took hold of his clothes and tore them into two pieces.


###### II Kings 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:13)


וַיָּ֙רֶם֙ אֶת־אַדֶּ֣רֶת אֵלִיָּ֔הוּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָפְלָ֖ה מֵעָלָ֑יו וַיָּ֥שׇׁב וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֖ד עַל־שְׂפַ֥ת הַיַּרְדֵּֽן׃


He picked up Eliyohu’s cloak that had fallen off of him, and he went back and stood on the bank of the Yardein.


###### II Kings 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:14)


וַיִּקַּח֩ אֶת־אַדֶּ֨רֶת אֵלִיָּ֜הוּ אֲשֶׁר־נָפְלָ֤ה מֵֽעָלָיו֙ וַיַּכֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַמַּ֔יִם וַיֹּאמַ֕ר אַיֵּ֕ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֵלִיָּ֑הוּ אַף־ה֣וּא**׀** וַיַּכֶּ֣ה אֶת־הַמַּ֗יִם וַיֵּֽחָצוּ֙ הֵ֣נָּה וָהֵ֔נָּה וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֖ר אֱלִישָֽׁע׃


And he took the cloak of Eliyohu that had fallen off of him, and struck the water, and said, “Where is Adonoy, the God of Eliyohu?” Then he also struck the water, and it split to this side and that side, and Elisha crossed over.


###### II Kings 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:15)


וַיִּרְאֻ֨הוּ בְנֵי־הַנְּבִיאִ֤ים אֲשֶׁר־בִּֽירִיחוֹ֙ מִנֶּ֔גֶד וַיֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ נָ֛חָה ר֥וּחַ אֵלִיָּ֖הוּ עַל־אֱלִישָׁ֑ע וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ לִקְרָאת֔וֹ וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲווּ־ל֖וֹ אָֽרְצָה׃


They saw him— the disciples of the prophets who were in Yericho — from afar and said, “The spirit of Eliyohu has rested on Elisha.” They came to greet him and prostrated themselves before him to the ground.


###### II Kings 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:16)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֡יו הִנֵּה־נָ֣א יֵֽשׁ־אֶת־עֲבָדֶ֩יךָ֩ חֲמִשִּׁ֨ים אֲנָשִׁ֜ים בְּנֵי־חַ֗יִל יֵ֣לְכוּ נָא֮ וִיבַקְשׁ֣וּ אֶת־אֲדֹנֶ֒יךָ֒ פֶּן־נְשָׂאוֹ֙ ר֣וּחַ יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיַּשְׁלִכֵ֙הוּ֙ בְּאַחַ֣ד הֶהָרִ֔ים א֖וֹ בְּאַחַ֣ת (הגיאות) [הַגֵּיאָי֑וֹת] וַיֹּ֖אמֶר לֹ֥א תִשְׁלָֽחוּ׃


They said to him, “Behold, please, Your servants have fifty able bodied men; please let them go and search for your master, perhaps the wind of Adonoy carried him up and threw him on one of the mountains, or in one of the valleys.” And he said, “Do not send.”


###### II Kings 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:17)


וַיִּפְצְרוּ־ב֥וֹ עַד־בֹּ֖שׁ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר שְׁלָ֑חוּ וַֽיִּשְׁלְחוּ֙ חֲמִשִּׁ֣ים אִ֔ישׁ וַיְבַקְשׁ֥וּ שְׁלֹשָֽׁה־יָמִ֖ים וְלֹ֥א מְצָאֻֽהוּ׃


They pressured him until he was ashamed, and he said, “Send.” They sent fifty men and they searched for three days but did not find him.


###### II Kings 2:18

וַיָּשֻׁ֣בוּ אֵלָ֔יו וְה֖וּא יֹשֵׁ֣ב בִּירִיח֑וֹ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם הֲלֹֽא־אָמַ֥רְתִּי אֲלֵיכֶ֖ם אַל־תֵּלֵֽכוּ׃{ס}


They returned to him as he was staying in Yericho, and he said to them, “Did I not say to you, ‘Do not go’?”


###### II Kings 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:19)


וַיֹּ֨אמְר֜וּ אַנְשֵׁ֤י הָעִיר֙ אֶל־אֱלִישָׁ֔ע הִנֵּה־נָ֞א מוֹשַׁ֤ב הָעִיר֙ ט֔וֹב כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲדֹנִ֖י רֹאֶ֑ה וְהַמַּ֥יִם רָעִ֖ים וְהָאָ֥רֶץ מְשַׁכָּֽלֶת׃


The people of the city said to Elisha, “Behold, please the city is a good place to live, as my master sees, but the water is bad, and the land causes bereavement.”


###### II Kings 2:20

וַיֹּ֗אמֶר קְחוּ־לִי֙ צְלֹחִ֣ית חֲדָשָׁ֔ה וְשִׂ֥ימוּ שָׁ֖ם מֶ֑לַח וַיִּקְח֖וּ אֵלָֽיו׃


And he said, “Bring me a new jar and put salt in it,” and they brought it to him.


###### II Kings 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:21)


וַיֵּצֵא֙ אֶל־מוֹצָ֣א הַמַּ֔יִם וַיַּשְׁלֶךְ־שָׁ֖ם מֶ֑לַח וַיֹּ֜אמֶר כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה רִפִּ֙אתִי֙ לַמַּ֣יִם הָאֵ֔לֶּה לֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה מִשָּׁ֛ם ע֖וֹד מָ֥וֶת וּמְשַׁכָּֽלֶת׃


He went out to the source of the water and threw salt there, and said, “Thus Adonoy has said, ‘I have cured these waters, there will no longer be from there a cause of death and bereavement.’”


###### II Kings 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:22)


וַיֵּרָפ֣וּ הַמַּ֔יִם עַ֖ד הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה כִּדְבַ֥ר אֱלִישָׁ֖ע אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


The waters were cured until this day, like the word that Elisha had spoken.


###### II Kings 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:23)


וַיַּ֥עַל מִשָּׁ֖ם בֵּֽית־אֵ֑ל וְה֣וּא**׀** עֹלֶ֣ה בַדֶּ֗רֶךְ וּנְעָרִ֤ים קְטַנִּים֙ יָצְא֣וּ מִן־הָעִ֔יר וַיִּתְקַלְּסוּ־בוֹ֙ וַיֹּ֣אמְרוּ ל֔וֹ עֲלֵ֥ה קֵרֵ֖חַ עֲלֵ֥ה קֵרֵֽחַ׃


He went up from there to Beis Eil, and as he was going up on the way, little boys came out from the city and taunted him and said to him, “Go away, baldy! Go away, baldy!”


###### II Kings 2:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:24)


וַיִּ֤פֶן אַֽחֲרָיו֙ וַיִּרְאֵ֔ם וַֽיְקַלְלֵ֖ם בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֑ה וַתֵּצֶ֜אנָה שְׁתַּ֤יִם דֻּבִּים֙ מִן־הַיַּ֔עַר וַתְּבַקַּ֣עְנָה מֵהֶ֔ם אַרְבָּעִ֥ים וּשְׁנֵ֖י יְלָדִֽים׃


He turned around and saw them. He cursed them in the name of Adonoy. Two bears came out of the woods and tore apart forty-two of the boys.


###### II Kings 2:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/2:25)


וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ מִשָּׁ֖ם אֶל־הַ֣ר הַכַּרְמֶ֑ל וּמִשָּׁ֖ם שָׁ֥ב שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃{פ}


He went from there to Mount Carmel, and from there he returned to Shomron.



## Chapter 3

###### II Kings 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:1)


וִיהוֹרָ֣ם בֶּן־אַחְאָ֗ב מָלַ֤ךְ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ בְּשֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן בִּשְׁנַת֙ שְׁמֹנֶ֣ה עֶשְׂרֵ֔ה לִיהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיִּמְלֹ֖ךְ שְׁתֵּים־עֶשְׂרֵ֥ה שָׁנָֽה׃


Yehorom, son of Achov, became king of Yisroel in Shomron in the eighteenth year of Yehoshophot, king of Yehudah, and he reigned for twelve years.


###### II Kings 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:2)


וַיַּעֲשֶׂ֤ה הָרַע֙ בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה רַ֕ק לֹ֥א כְאָבִ֖יו וּכְאִמּ֑וֹ וַיָּ֙סַר֙ אֶת־מַצְּבַ֣ת הַבַּ֔עַל אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה אָבִֽיו׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, but not [as bad] as his father and mother. He removed the monument of Baal that his father had made.


###### II Kings 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/3:3)


רַ֠ק בְּחַטֹּ֞אות יָרׇבְעָ֧ם בֶּן־נְבָ֛ט אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱטִ֥יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל דָּבֵ֑ק לֹא־סָ֖ר מִמֶּֽנָּה׃{פ}


However, to the sins of Yorovom, son of Nevot, who caused Yisroel to sin, did he cling. He did not reject them.

Meisha, king of Moav, owned many flocks. He used to send to the king of Yisroel one hundred thousand fattened sheep and one hundred thousand wooly rams.

And when Achov died, the king of Moav rebelled against the king of Yisroel.

King Yehoram went out on that day from Shomron and took a census of all Yisroel.

He went and sent to Yehoshophot, king of Yehudah, saying, “the king of Moav has rebelled against me. Will you go with me to war against Moav?” And he said, “I will go up. I am like you, my people are like your people, my horses are like your horses.”

וַיִּקַּח֩ אֶת־בְּנ֨וֹ הַבְּכ֜וֹר אֲשֶׁר־יִמְלֹ֣ךְ תַּחְתָּ֗יו וַיַּעֲלֵ֤הוּ עֹלָה֙ עַל־הַ֣חֹמָ֔ה וַיְהִ֥י קֶצֶף־גָּד֖וֹל עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּסְעוּ֙ מֵעָלָ֔יו וַיָּשֻׁ֖בוּ לָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


He then took his firstborn son, who was to rule in his place, and offered him as a burnt offering on the wall; this caused a terrible anger against Yisroel. They withdrew and returned to the land.



## Chapter 4

###### II Kings 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:1)


וְאִשָּׁ֣ה אַחַ֣ת מִנְּשֵׁ֣י בְנֵֽי־הַ֠נְּבִיאִ֠ים צָעֲקָ֨ה אֶל־אֱלִישָׁ֜ע לֵאמֹ֗ר עַבְדְּךָ֤ אִישִׁי֙ מֵ֔ת וְאַתָּ֣ה יָדַ֔עְתָּ כִּ֣י עַבְדְּךָ֔ הָיָ֥ה יָרֵ֖א אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהַ֨נֹּשֶׁ֔ה בָּ֗א לָקַ֜חַת אֶת־שְׁנֵ֧י יְלָדַ֛י ל֖וֹ לַעֲבָדִֽים׃


A woman, a wife of one of the disciples of the prophets cried out to Elisha, saying, “Your servant, my husband died, and you know that your servant was one who feared Adonoy. Now the creditor has come to take my two sons for himself as slaves.”


###### II Kings 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלֶ֤יהָ אֱלִישָׁע֙ מָ֣ה אֶֽעֱשֶׂה־לָּ֔ךְ הַגִּ֣ידִי לִ֔י מַה־יֶּשׁ־[לָ֖ךְ] (לכי) בַּבָּ֑יִת וַתֹּ֗אמֶר אֵ֣ין לְשִׁפְחָתְךָ֥ כֹל֙ בַּבַּ֔יִת כִּ֖י אִם־אָס֥וּךְ שָֽׁמֶן׃


Elisha said to her, “What can I do for you? Tell me what do you have in the house?” She said, “Your maidservant has nothing in the house except a jar of oil.”


###### II Kings 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:3)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר לְכִ֨י שַׁאֲלִי־לָ֤ךְ כֵּלִים֙ מִן־הַח֔וּץ מֵאֵ֖ת כׇּל־[שְׁכֵנָ֑יִךְ] (שכנכי) כֵּלִ֥ים רֵקִ֖ים אַל־תַּמְעִֽיטִי׃


And he said, “Go borrow containers from the outside, from all of your neighbors, empty containers, do not skimp.


###### II Kings 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:4)


וּבָ֗את וְסָגַ֤רְתְּ הַדֶּ֙לֶת֙ בַּעֲדֵ֣ךְ וּבְעַד־בָּנַ֔יִךְ וְיָצַ֕קְתְּ עַ֥ל כׇּל־הַכֵּלִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְהַמָּלֵ֖א תַּסִּֽיעִי׃


And then come in and close the door behind yourself and behind your sons; pour into all these containers, and the full one[s] you shall remove.”

וַתֵּ֙לֶךְ֙ מֵֽאִתּ֔וֹ וַתִּסְגֹּ֣ר הַדֶּ֔לֶת בַּעֲדָ֖הּ וּבְעַ֣ד בָּנֶ֑יהָ הֵ֛ם מַגִּישִׁ֥ים אֵלֶ֖יהָ וְהִ֥יא (מיצקת) [מוֹצָֽקֶת]׃


She went from him, and closed the door behind herself and behind her sons. They would bring [the containers] to her and she would pour.

When the containers were full she said to her son “Bring me another container,” and he said to her, “there is no other container.” Then the oil stopped.

She said to her husband, “Behold, now I know that he is a holy man of God who passes by us frequently.

נַעֲשֶׂה־נָּ֤א עֲלִיַּת־קִיר֙ קְטַנָּ֔ה וְנָשִׂ֨ים ל֥וֹ שָׁ֛ם מִטָּ֥ה וְשֻׁלְחָ֖ן וְכִסֵּ֣א וּמְנוֹרָ֑ה וְהָיָ֛ה בְּבֹא֥וֹ אֵלֵ֖ינוּ יָס֥וּר שָֽׁמָּה׃

And he said to him [Geichazi], “Please say to her, ‘Behold, you have busied yourself with us with all this attention. What can be done for you? Should we speak to the king on your behalf, or to the general of the army?’” She said, “I live in the midst of my people.”

וַתָּבֹ֞א אֶל־אִ֤ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים֙ אֶל־הָהָ֔ר וַֽתַּחֲזֵ֖ק בְּרַגְלָ֑יו וַיִּגַּ֨שׁ גֵּיחֲזִ֜י לְהׇדְפָ֗הּ וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ אִ֨ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֤ים הַרְפֵּה־לָהּ֙ כִּֽי־נַפְשָׁ֣הּ מָֽרָה־לָ֔הּ וַֽיהֹוָה֙ הֶעְלִ֣ים מִמֶּ֔נִּי וְלֹ֥א הִגִּ֖יד לִֽי׃


She came to the man of God, to the mountains, and took hold of his feet. Geichazi approached to push her away, but the man of God, said, “Leave her, for her soul is bitter, and Adonoy has concealed it from me and did not tell me.”


###### II Kings 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:28)


וַתֹּ֕אמֶר הֲשָׁאַ֥לְתִּי בֵ֖ן מֵאֵ֣ת אֲדֹנִ֑י הֲלֹ֣א אָמַ֔רְתִּי לֹ֥א תַשְׁלֶ֖ה אֹתִֽי׃


She said, “Did I ask for a son from my master? Did I not say, ‘Do not mislead me.’”


###### II Kings 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:29)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר לְגֵיחֲזִ֜י חֲגֹ֣ר מׇתְנֶ֗יךָ וְקַ֨ח מִשְׁעַנְתִּ֣י בְיָדְךָ֮ וָלֵךְ֒ כִּי־תִמְצָ֥א אִישׁ֙ לֹ֣א תְבָרְכֶ֔נּוּ וְכִֽי־יְבָרֶכְךָ֥ אִ֖ישׁ לֹ֣א תַעֲנֶ֑נּוּ וְשַׂמְתָּ֥ מִשְׁעַנְתִּ֖י עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הַנָּֽעַר׃


And he said to Geichazi, “Gird your loins [strengthen yourself], and take my staff in your hand and go. If you meet anyone, do not greet him, and if anyone greets you, do not answer him; and place my staff on the boy’s face.”


###### II Kings 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:30)


וַתֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֵ֣ם הַנַּ֔עַר חַי־יְהֹוָ֥ה וְחֵֽי־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ אִם־אֶעֶזְבֶ֑ךָּ וַיָּ֖קׇם וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ אַחֲרֶֽיהָ׃


The boy’s mother said, “As Adonoy lives and by your life, I will not leave you.” He got up and followed her.


###### II Kings 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/4:31)


וְגֵחֲזִ֞י עָבַ֣ר לִפְנֵיהֶ֗ם וַיָּ֤שֶׂם אֶת־הַמִּשְׁעֶ֙נֶת֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַנַּ֔עַר וְאֵ֥ין ק֖וֹל וְאֵ֣ין קָ֑שֶׁב וַיָּ֤שׇׁב לִקְרָאתוֹ֙ וַיַּגֶּד־ל֣וֹ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֥א הֵקִ֖יץ הַנָּֽעַר׃


Geichazi had gone on ahead of them and placed the staff on the boy’s face but there was no sound or awareness. He went back to meet him [Elisha], and told him saying, “the boy did not wake up.”

וַיַּ֜עַל וַיִּשְׁכַּ֣ב עַל־הַיֶּ֗לֶד וַיָּ֩שֶׂם֩ פִּ֨יו עַל־פִּ֜יו וְעֵינָ֤יו עַל־עֵינָיו֙ וְכַפָּ֣יו עַל־כַּפָּ֔ו וַיִּגְהַ֖ר עָלָ֑יו וַיָּ֖חׇם בְּשַׂ֥ר הַיָּֽלֶד׃


He got up and lay down on the boy. He put his mouth on his mouth, his eyes upon his eyes, his palms on his palms, and he spread himself on him; and boy’s flesh became warm.

וַיֵּצֵ֨א אֶחָ֣ד אֶל־הַשָּׂדֶה֮ לְלַקֵּ֣ט אֹרֹת֒ וַיִּמְצָא֙ גֶּ֣פֶן שָׂדֶ֔ה וַיְלַקֵּ֥ט מִמֶּ֛נּוּ פַּקֻּעֹ֥ת שָׂדֶ֖ה מְלֹ֣א בִגְד֑וֹ וַיָּבֹ֗א וַיְפַלַּ֛ח אֶל־סִ֥יר הַנָּזִ֖יד כִּי־לֹ֥א יָדָֽעוּ׃


One of them went out to the field to gather greens [herbs] and he found a vine in the field and gathered wild gourds from it, enough to fill his garment. He came and sliced [them] into the pot of porridge for they did not know [that it was poisonous].

## Chapter 5

###### II Kings 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:1)


וְ֠נַעֲמָ֠ן שַׂר־צְבָ֨א מֶלֶךְ־אֲרָ֜ם הָיָ֣ה אִישׁ֩ גָּד֨וֹל לִפְנֵ֤י אֲדֹנָיו֙ וּנְשֻׂ֣א פָנִ֔ים כִּי־ב֛וֹ נָתַן־יְהֹוָ֥ה תְּשׁוּעָ֖ה לַאֲרָ֑ם וְהָאִ֗ישׁ הָיָ֛ה גִּבּ֥וֹר חַ֖יִל מְצֹרָֽע׃


Na’amon, the general of the king of Arom’s army, was a prominent man before his master, and was highly respected, for through him Adonoy has given victory to Arom. This man was a mighty warrior, but he was a metzora.


###### II Kings 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:2)


וַֽאֲרָם֙ יָצְא֣וּ גְדוּדִ֔ים וַיִּשְׁבּ֛וּ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נַעֲרָ֣ה קְטַנָּ֑ה וַתְּהִ֕י לִפְנֵ֖י אֵ֥שֶׁת נַעֲמָֽן׃


Bands of marauders from Arom went out and kidnaped a little girl from Yisroel, and she become [a servant] to Na’amon’s wife.


###### II Kings 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:3)


וַתֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֶל־גְּבִרְתָּ֔הּ אַחֲלֵ֣י אֲדֹנִ֔י לִפְנֵ֥י הַנָּבִ֖יא אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּשֹׁמְר֑וֹן אָ֛ז יֶאֱסֹ֥ף אֹת֖וֹ מִצָּרַעְתּֽוֹ׃


She said to her mistress, “My master should plead [for curing his illness] before the prophet who is in Shomron; and then he will cure him of his tzora’as.”


###### II Kings 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:4)


וַיָּבֹ֕א וַיַּגֵּ֥ד לַאדֹנָ֖יו לֵאמֹ֑ר כָּזֹ֤את וְכָזֹאת֙ דִּבְּרָ֣ה הַֽנַּעֲרָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר מֵאֶ֥רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


He [Na’amon] came and told his master, saying, “Such and such spoke the girl who is from the land of Yisroel.”


###### II Kings 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:5)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲרָם֙ לֶךְ־בֹּ֔א וְאֶשְׁלְחָ֥ה סֵ֖פֶר אֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֵּ֩לֶךְ֩ וַיִּקַּ֨ח בְּיָד֜וֹ עֶ֣שֶׂר כִּכְּרֵי־כֶ֗סֶף וְשֵׁ֤שֶׁת אֲלָפִים֙ זָהָ֔ב וְעֶ֖שֶׂר חֲלִיפ֥וֹת בְּגָדִֽים׃


And the king of Arom said, “Come, go and I will send a letter to the king of Yisroel.” He went and took in his hand ten kikar of silver, six thousand gold [pieces] and ten changes of clothes.


###### II Kings 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:6)


וַיָּבֵ֣א הַסֵּ֔פֶר אֶל־מֶ֥לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר וְעַתָּ֗ה כְּב֨וֹא הַסֵּ֤פֶר הַזֶּה֙ אֵלֶ֔יךָ הִנֵּ֨ה שָׁלַ֤חְתִּי אֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶת־נַעֲמָ֣ן עַבְדִּ֔י וַאֲסַפְתּ֖וֹ מִצָּרַעְתּֽוֹ׃


He brought the letter to the king of Yisroel, saying, “Now when this letter reaches you, I will have sent to you my servant, Na’amon; arrange to cure him of his tzora’as.”


###### II Kings 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:7)


וַיְהִ֡י כִּקְרֹא֩ מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֨ל אֶת־הַסֵּ֜פֶר וַיִּקְרַ֣ע בְּגָדָ֗יו וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ הַאֱלֹהִ֥ים אָ֙נִי֙ לְהָמִ֣ית וּֽלְהַחֲי֔וֹת כִּי־זֶה֙ שֹׁלֵ֣חַ אֵלַ֔י לֶאֱסֹ֥ף אִ֖ישׁ מִצָּרַעְתּ֑וֹ כִּ֤י אַךְ־דְּעוּ־נָא֙ וּרְא֔וּ כִּֽי־מִתְאַנֶּ֥ה ה֖וּא לִֽי׃


When the king of Yisroel read the letter, he tore his clothes and said, “Am I God, Who causes death and life, that this one sends to me to cure a man of his tzora’as? I want it to be known and seen that he is looking for an excuse [to start a war] against me.”


###### II Kings 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/5:8)


וַיְהִ֞י כִּשְׁמֹ֣עַ**׀** אֱלִישָׁ֣ע אִישׁ־הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים כִּֽי־קָרַ֤ע מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֶת־בְּגָדָ֔יו וַיִּשְׁלַח֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ לֵאמֹ֔ר לָ֥מָּה קָרַ֖עְתָּ בְּגָדֶ֑יךָ יָבֹא־נָ֣א אֵלַ֔י וְיֵדַ֕ע כִּ֛י יֵ֥שׁ נָבִ֖יא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


When Elisha, the man of God heard that the king of Yisroel had torn his clothes, he sent [a message] to the king, saying, “Why have you torn your clothes? Let him come to me now and he will know that there is a prophet in Yisroel.”

And Na’amon came with his horses and chariot and stood at the doorway of Elisha’s house.

And Elisha sent a messenger to him saying, “Go and wash seven times in the Yardein, and your flesh will be restored to you and you will be pure.”

Na’amon was enraged and left. He said, “Behold, I thought he would come out to me and stand and call in the name, Adonoy, his God, and wave his hand toward the spot and the tzora’as would be cured.

וַיֹּ֕אמֶר כֹּֽה־יַעֲשֶׂה־לִּ֥י אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְכֹ֣ה יוֹסִ֑ף אִֽם־יַעֲמֹ֞ד רֹ֣אשׁ אֱלִישָׁ֧ע בֶּן־שָׁפָ֛ט עָלָ֖יו הַיּֽוֹם׃


And he said, “So should God do to me, and even more if the head of Elisha son of Shophot stands [remains] on him today.”


###### II Kings 6:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/6:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/6:32)


וֶאֱלִישָׁע֙ יֹשֵׁ֣ב בְּבֵית֔וֹ וְהַזְּקֵנִ֖ים יֹשְׁבִ֣ים אִתּ֑וֹ וַיִּשְׁלַ֨ח אִ֜ישׁ מִלְּפָנָ֗יו בְּטֶ֣רֶם יָבֹא֩ הַמַּלְאָ֨ךְ אֵלָ֜יו וְה֣וּא**׀** אָמַ֣ר אֶל־הַזְּקֵנִ֗ים הַרְּאִיתֶם֙ כִּֽי־שָׁלַ֞ח בֶּן־הַֽמְרַצֵּ֤חַ הַזֶּה֙ לְהָסִ֣יר אֶת־רֹאשִׁ֔י רְא֣וּ**׀** כְּבֹ֣א הַמַּלְאָ֗ךְ סִגְר֤וּ הַדֶּ֙לֶת֙ וּלְחַצְתֶּ֤ם אֹתוֹ֙ בַּדֶּ֔לֶת הֲל֗וֹא ק֛וֹל רַגְלֵ֥י אֲדֹנָ֖יו אַחֲרָֽיו׃


Elisha was sitting in his house and the elders were sitting with him. He [the king] sent a man from his presence. Before the messenger came to him, he said to the elders, “Are you aware that this son of a murderer has sent [a man] to remove my head. Look, when the messenger comes, close the door, and push him out with the door; the sound of his master’s footsteps will surely follow after him.”


###### II Kings 6:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/6:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/6:33)


עוֹדֶ֙נּוּ֙ מְדַבֵּ֣ר עִמָּ֔ם וְהִנֵּ֥ה הַמַּלְאָ֖ךְ יֹרֵ֣ד אֵלָ֑יו וַיֹּ֗אמֶר הִנֵּה־זֹ֤את הָרָעָה֙ מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה מָה־אוֹחִ֥יל לַיהֹוָ֖ה עֽוֹד׃{פ}


He was still speaking with them, and behold, the messenger was coming down to him. He [the king] said, “This tragedy is from Adonoy; What can I hope for from Adonoy?”



## Chapter 7

###### II Kings 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:1)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֱלִישָׁ֔ע שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֑ה כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה כָּעֵ֤ת׀מָחָר֙ סְאָה־סֹ֣לֶת בְּשֶׁ֗קֶל וְסָאתַ֧יִם שְׂעֹרִ֛ים בְּשֶׁ֖קֶל בְּשַׁ֥עַר שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃


And Elisha said, “Listen to the word of Adonoy, thus has Adonoy said, ‘At this time tomorrow a measure of fine flour will cost a shekel, and two measures of barley will cost a shekel at the gate of Shomron.’”


###### II Kings 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:2)


וַיַּ֣עַן הַשָּׁלִ֡ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־לַמֶּ֩לֶךְ֩ נִשְׁעָ֨ן עַל־יָד֜וֹ אֶת־אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים֮ וַיֹּאמַר֒ הִנֵּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֗ה עֹשֶׂ֤ה אֲרֻבּוֹת֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֔יִם הֲיִֽהְיֶ֖ה הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וַיֹּ֗אמֶר הִנְּכָ֤ה רֹאֶה֙ בְּעֵינֶ֔יךָ וּמִשָּׁ֖ם לֹ֥א תֹאכֵֽל׃{פ}


And the king’s aide answered — he was the one upon whose hand he leaned — the man of God, and he said, “Behold, if Adonoy makes windows in the sky will such a thing happen?” He [Elisha] said, “Behold, you will see this with your eyes, but you will not eat from it.”


###### II Kings 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:3)


וְאַרְבָּעָ֧ה אֲנָשִׁ֛ים הָי֥וּ מְצֹרָעִ֖ים פֶּ֣תַח הַשָּׁ֑עַר וַיֹּֽאמְרוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶל־רֵעֵ֔הוּ מָ֗ה אֲנַ֛חְנוּ יֹשְׁבִ֥ים פֹּ֖ה עַד־מָֽתְנוּ׃


There were four men, suffering with tzora’as at the entrance of the gate, and they said to each other, “Why are we sitting here until we die?


###### II Kings 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:4)


אִם־אָמַ֩רְנוּ֩ נָב֨וֹא הָעִ֜יר וְהָרָעָ֤ב בָּעִיר֙ וָמַ֣תְנוּ שָׁ֔ם וְאִם־יָשַׁ֥בְנוּ פֹ֖ה וָמָ֑תְנוּ וְעַתָּ֗ה לְכוּ֙ וְנִפְּלָה֙ אֶל־מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֔ם אִם־יְחַיֻּ֣נוּ נִֽחְיֶ֔ה וְאִם־יְמִיתֻ֖נוּ וָמָֽתְנוּ׃


If we say [decide] to come into the city, there is a famine in the city, and we will die there, and if we sit here we will die. So now let us go and fall [defect] to the camp of Arom. If they let us live we will live, and if they kill us we will die.”


###### II Kings 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:5)


וַיָּקֻ֣מוּ בַנֶּ֔שֶׁף לָב֖וֹא אֶל־מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֑ם וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ עַד־קְצֵה֙ מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֔ם וְהִנֵּ֥ה אֵֽין־שָׁ֖ם אִֽישׁ׃


They got up at dusk to come to the camp of Arom, and they came to the edge of Arom’s camp, and behold there was no one there.


###### II Kings 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:6)


וַאדֹנָ֞י הִשְׁמִ֣יעַ**׀** אֶת־מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֗ם ק֥וֹל רֶ֙כֶב֙ ק֣וֹל ס֔וּס ק֖וֹל חַ֣יִל גָּד֑וֹל וַיֹּאמְר֞וּ אִ֣ישׁ אֶל־אָחִ֗יו הִנֵּ֣ה שָֽׂכַר־עָלֵ֩ינוּ֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל אֶת־מַלְכֵ֧י הַחִתִּ֛ים וְאֶת־מַלְכֵ֥י מִצְרַ֖יִם לָב֥וֹא עָלֵֽינוּ׃


For Adonoy had caused the camp of Arom to hear the sound of chariots, the sound of horses, and the sound of a great army. They said to one another, “Behold, the king of Yisroel has hired the kings of the Chittim and the kings of the Egyptians at attack us.”


###### II Kings 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:7)


וַיָּק֘וּמוּ֮ וַיָּנ֣וּסוּ בַנֶּ֒שֶׁף֒ וַיַּעַזְב֣וּ אֶת־אׇהֳלֵיהֶ֗ם וְאֶת־סֽוּסֵיהֶם֙ וְאֶת־חֲמֹ֣רֵיהֶ֔ם הַֽמַּחֲנֶ֖ה כַּאֲשֶׁר־הִ֑יא וַיָּנֻ֖סוּ אֶל־נַפְשָֽׁם׃


They got up and fled at dusk, leaving behind their tents, their horses and their donkeys, the camp as it was, and they fled for their lives.


###### II Kings 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:8)


וַיָּבֹ֩אוּ֩ הַֽמְצֹרָעִ֨ים הָאֵ֜לֶּה עַד־קְצֵ֣ה הַֽמַּחֲנֶ֗ה וַיָּבֹ֜אוּ אֶל־אֹ֤הֶל אֶחָד֙ וַיֹּאכְל֣וּ וַיִּשְׁתּ֔וּ וַיִּשְׂא֣וּ מִשָּׁ֗ם כֶּ֤סֶף וְזָהָב֙ וּבְגָדִ֔ים וַיֵּלְכ֖וּ וַיַּטְמִ֑נוּ וַיָּשֻׁ֗בוּ וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ אֶל־אֹ֣הֶל אַחֵ֔ר וַיִּשְׂא֣וּ מִשָּׁ֔ם וַיֵּלְכ֖וּ וַיַּטְמִֽנוּ׃


These metzora’im came to the edge of the camp, and they entered one tent and ate and drank. They carried from there silver, gold and clothing, and they went and hid them; they returned and entered another tent, and carried from there, and then they went and hid them.


###### II Kings 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:9)


וַיֹּאמְרוּ֩ אִ֨ישׁ אֶל־רֵעֵ֜הוּ לֹא־כֵ֣ן**׀** אֲנַ֣חְנוּ עֹשִׂ֗ים הַיּ֤וֹם הַזֶּה֙ יוֹם־בְּשֹׂרָ֣ה ה֔וּא וַאֲנַ֣חְנוּ מַחְשִׁ֗ים וְחִכִּ֛ינוּ עַד־א֥וֹר הַבֹּ֖קֶר וּמְצָאָ֣נוּ עָו֑וֹן וְעַתָּה֙ לְכ֣וּ וְנָבֹ֔אָה וְנַגִּ֖ידָה בֵּ֥ית הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


They said to one another, “It is not proper, what we are doing, this day is a day of good news, and we are keeping quiet. If we wait until daylight, we will be considered guilty. So now let us go and come, and tell [this] to the royal household.”


###### II Kings 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:10)


וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ וַֽיִּקְרְאוּ֮ אֶל־שֹׁעֵ֣ר הָעִיר֒ וַיַּגִּ֤ידוּ לָהֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר בָּ֚אנוּ אֶל־מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֔ם וְהִנֵּ֧ה אֵֽין־שָׁ֛ם אִ֖ישׁ וְק֣וֹל אָדָ֑ם כִּ֣י אִם־הַסּ֤וּס אָסוּר֙ וְהַחֲמ֣וֹר אָס֔וּר וְאֹהָלִ֖ים כַּאֲשֶׁר־הֵֽמָּה׃


They came and called out to the gatekeeper[s] of the city and told them, saying, “We came to the camp of Arom and behold there is no man there nor the sound of a human voice. Only, the horses are tied and the donkeys are tied, and the tents are as they were.”


###### II Kings 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:11)


וַיִּקְרָ֖א הַשֹּׁעֲרִ֑ים וַיַּגִּ֕ידוּ בֵּ֥ית הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ פְּנִֽימָה׃


The gate keepers called and related [the news] to the royal household within.


###### II Kings 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:12)


וַיָּ֨קׇם הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ לַ֗יְלָה וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אֶל־עֲבָדָ֔יו אַגִּֽידָה־נָּ֣א לָכֶ֔ם אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־עָ֥שׂוּ לָ֖נוּ אֲרָ֑ם יָדְע֞וּ כִּֽי־רְעֵבִ֣ים אֲנַ֗חְנוּ וַיֵּֽצְא֤וּ מִן־הַֽמַּחֲנֶה֙ לְהֵחָבֵ֤ה (בהשדה) [בַשָּׂדֶה֙] לֵאמֹ֔ר כִּֽי־יֵצְא֤וּ מִן־הָעִיר֙ וְנִתְפְּשֵׂ֣ם חַיִּ֔ים וְאֶל־הָעִ֖יר נָבֹֽא׃


The king got up at night and said to his servants, “I will tell you what Arom has done to us. They know we are starving, so they left the camp to hide in the field, saying, ‘When they go out of the city, we will capture them alive and come into the city.’”


###### II Kings 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:13)


וַיַּ֩עַן֩ אֶחָ֨ד מֵעֲבָדָ֜יו וַיֹּ֗אמֶר וְיִקְחוּ־נָ֞א חֲמִשָּׁ֣ה מִן־הַסּוּסִים֮ הַֽנִּשְׁאָרִים֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁאֲרוּ־בָהּ֒ הִנָּ֗ם כְּכׇל־[הֲמ֤וֹן] (ההמון) יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁאֲרוּ־בָ֔הּ הִנָּ֕ם כְּכׇל־הֲמ֥וֹן יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אֲשֶׁר־תָּ֑מּוּ וְנִשְׁלְחָ֖ה וְנִרְאֶֽה׃


And one of his servants answered and said, “Let them take five of the remaining horses that are left in it [the city]. Behold, they are like all the multitude of Yisroel that are left in [the city], behold they are like all the multitude of Yisroel that have perished; let us send [them out] and we will see.”


###### II Kings 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:14)


וַיִּקְח֕וּ שְׁנֵ֖י רֶ֣כֶב סוּסִ֑ים וַיִּשְׁלַ֨ח הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אַחֲרֵ֧י מַֽחֲנֵה־אֲרָ֛ם לֵאמֹ֖ר לְכ֥וּ וּרְאֽוּ׃


So they took two horsemen, and the king sent them after the camp of Arom, saying, “Go and see.”


###### II Kings 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:15)


וַיֵּלְכ֣וּ אַחֲרֵיהֶם֮ עַד־הַיַּרְדֵּן֒ וְהִנֵּ֣ה כׇל־הַדֶּ֗רֶךְ מְלֵאָ֤ה בְגָדִים֙ וְכֵלִ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־הִשְׁלִ֥יכוּ אֲרָ֖ם (בהחפזם) [בְּחׇפְזָ֑ם] וַיָּשֻׁ֙בוּ֙ הַמַּלְאָכִ֔ים וַיַּגִּ֖דוּ לַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


They went after them up to the Yardein, and behold, the entire way was filled with clothes and utensils that Arom threw away in their haste. The messengers returned and told the king.


###### II Kings 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:16)


וַיֵּצֵ֣א הָעָ֔ם וַיָּבֹ֕זּוּ אֵ֖ת מַחֲנֵ֣ה אֲרָ֑ם וַיְהִ֨י סְאָה־סֹ֜לֶת בְּשֶׁ֗קֶל וְסָאתַ֧יִם שְׂעֹרִ֛ים בְּשֶׁ֖קֶל כִּדְבַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


And the people went out and looted the camp of Arom, and it was then that a measure of fine flour cost a shekel, and two measures of barley cost a shekel, as Adonoy had said.


###### II Kings 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:17)


וְהַמֶּ֩לֶךְ֩ הִפְקִ֨יד אֶת־הַשָּׁלִ֜ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁעָ֤ן עַל־יָדוֹ֙ עַל־הַשַּׁ֔עַר וַיִּרְמְסֻ֧הוּ הָעָ֛ם בַּשַּׁ֖עַר וַיָּמֹ֑ת כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר דִּבֶּר֙ אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּ֔ר בְּרֶ֥דֶת הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֵלָֽיו׃


The king appointed his aide upon whose hand he leaned to be in charge of the gate, and the people trampled him at the gate and he died; as the man of God had spoken, which he had spoken when the king came down to him.


###### II Kings 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:18)


וַיְהִ֗י כְּדַבֵּר֙ אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֶל־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לֵאמֹ֑ר סָאתַ֨יִם שְׂעֹרִ֜ים בְּשֶׁ֗קֶל וּֽסְאָה־סֹ֙לֶת֙ בְּשֶׁ֔קֶל יִֽהְיֶה֙ כָּעֵ֣ת מָחָ֔ר בְּשַׁ֖עַר שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃


And it was when the man of God spoke to the king, saying, “Two measures of barley will cost a shekel and one measure of fine flour will cost a shekel at this time tomorrow in the gate of Shomron.”


###### II Kings 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:19)


וַיַּ֨עַן הַשָּׁלִ֜ישׁ אֶת־אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים֮ וַיֹּאמַר֒ וְהִנֵּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֗ה עֹשֶׂ֤ה אֲרֻבּוֹת֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֔יִם הֲיִֽהְיֶ֖ה כַּדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וַיֹּ֗אמֶר הִנְּךָ֤ רֹאֶה֙ בְּעֵינֶ֔יךָ וּמִשָּׁ֖ם לֹ֥א תֹאכֵֽל׃


And the [king’s] aide had answered the man of God and said, “Behold, if Adonoy makes windows in the sky will such a thing happen?” He [Elisha] replied, “Behold, you ill see this with your eyes but you will not eat from it.”


###### II Kings 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/7:20)


וַֽיְהִי־ל֖וֹ כֵּ֑ן וַיִּרְמְס֨וּ אֹת֥וֹ הָעָ֛ם בַּשַּׁ֖עַר וַיָּמֹֽת׃{ס}


And so it happened to him, that the people trampled him at the gate and he died.



## Chapter 8

###### II Kings 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:1)


וֶאֱלִישָׁ֡ע דִּבֶּ֣ר אֶל־הָאִשָּׁה֩ אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱיָ֨ה אֶת־בְּנָ֜הּ לֵאמֹ֗ר ק֤וּמִי וּלְכִי֙ (אתי) [אַ֣תְּ] וּבֵיתֵ֔ךְ וְג֖וּרִי בַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר תָּג֑וּרִי כִּֽי־קָרָ֤א יְהֹוָה֙ לָרָעָ֔ב וְגַם־בָּ֥א אֶל־הָאָ֖רֶץ שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִֽים׃


Elisha spoke to the woman whose son he had revived, saying, “Get up and go, you and your household, and live wherever you want to live, for Adonoy has proclaimed a famine and it is coming upon the land for seven years.”


###### II Kings 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:2)


וַתָּ֙קׇם֙ הָאִשָּׁ֔ה וַתַּ֕עַשׂ כִּדְבַ֖ר אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וַתֵּ֤לֶךְ הִיא֙ וּבֵיתָ֔הּ וַתָּ֥גׇר בְּאֶרֶץ־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִֽים׃


The woman got up and did according to the word of the man of God. She and her household went and lived in the land of the Pelishtim for seven years.


###### II Kings 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:3)


וַיְהִ֗י מִקְצֵה֙ שֶׁ֣בַע שָׁנִ֔ים וַתָּ֥שׇׁב הָאִשָּׁ֖ה מֵאֶ֣רֶץ פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַתֵּצֵא֙ לִצְעֹ֣ק אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אֶל־בֵּיתָ֖הּ וְאֶל־שָׂדָֽהּ׃


At the end of seven years the woman returned from the land of Pelishtim, and she went out to cry to the king about her house and her field.


###### II Kings 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:4)


וְהַמֶּ֗לֶךְ מְדַבֵּר֙ אֶל־גֵּ֣חֲזִ֔י נַ֥עַר אִישׁ־הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים לֵאמֹ֑ר סַפְּרָה־נָּ֣א לִ֔י אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַגְּדֹל֖וֹת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֥ה אֱלִישָֽׁע׃{ס}


The king was speaking to Geichazi, the [former] servant of the man of God, saying, “Please tell me about all the great deeds that Elisha performed.”


###### II Kings 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:5)


וַ֠יְהִ֠י ה֥וּא מְסַפֵּ֣ר לַמֶּ֘לֶךְ֮ אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱיָ֣ה אֶת־הַמֵּת֒ וְהִנֵּ֨ה הָאִשָּׁ֜ה אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱיָ֤ה אֶת־בְּנָהּ֙ צֹעֶ֣קֶת אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַל־בֵּיתָ֖הּ וְעַל־שָׂדָ֑הּ וַיֹּ֤אמֶר גֵּֽחֲזִי֙ אֲדֹנִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ זֹ֚את הָֽאִשָּׁ֔ה וְזֶה־בְּנָ֖הּ אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱיָ֥ה אֱלִישָֽׁע׃


Just as he was telling the king how he [Elisha] revived the dead, behold there was the woman whose son he had revived, crying out to the king about her house and her field. Geichazi said, “My master, the king, this is the woman and this is her son whom Elisha revived.”


###### II Kings 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:6)


וַיִּשְׁאַ֥ל הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ לָאִשָּׁ֖ה וַתְּסַפֶּר־ל֑וֹ וַיִּתֶּן־לָ֣הּ הַמֶּ֩לֶךְ֩ סָרִ֨יס אֶחָ֜ד לֵאמֹ֗ר הָשֵׁ֤יב אֶת־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־לָהּ֙ וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־תְּבוּאֹ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה מִיּ֛וֹם עׇזְבָ֥הֿ אֶת־הָאָ֖רֶץ וְעַד־עָֽתָּה׃{פ}


The king asked the woman, and she told him; the king then appointed for her, a certain officer, saying, “Return all that belongs to her, and [the value of] all the produce of the field from the day she left the land until now.”


###### II Kings 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:7)


וַיָּבֹ֤א אֱלִישָׁע֙ דַּמֶּ֔שֶׂק וּבֶן־הֲדַ֥ד מֶלֶךְ־אֲרָ֖ם חֹלֶ֑ה וַיֻּגַּד־ל֣וֹ לֵאמֹ֔ר בָּ֛א אִ֥ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים עַד־הֵֽנָּה׃


Elisha came to Damesek and Ben-Hadad, king of Arom, was sick. It was told to him, saying, “The man of God has come here.”


###### II Kings 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:8)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ אֶל־חֲזָהאֵ֗ל קַ֤ח בְּיָדְךָ֙ מִנְחָ֔ה וְלֵ֕ךְ לִקְרַ֖את אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְדָרַשְׁתָּ֨ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֤ה מֵאוֹתוֹ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הַאֶחְיֶ֖ה מֵחֳלִ֥י זֶֽה׃


The king said to Chazoeil, “Take a gift in your hand, and go greet the man of God. Ask of Adonoy through him, saying, ‘Will I recover from this sickness?’”


###### II Kings 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:9)


וַיֵּ֣לֶךְ חֲזָאֵל֮ לִקְרָאתוֹ֒ וַיִּקַּ֨ח מִנְחָ֤ה בְיָדוֹ֙ וְכׇל־ט֣וּב דַּמֶּ֔שֶׂק מַשָּׂ֖א אַרְבָּעִ֣ים גָּמָ֑ל וַיָּבֹא֙ וַיַּעֲמֹ֣ד לְפָנָ֔יו וַיֹּ֗אמֶר בִּנְךָ֨ בֶן־הֲדַ֤ד מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲרָם֙ שְׁלָחַ֤נִי אֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הַאֶחְיֶ֖ה מֵחֳלִ֥י זֶֽה׃


Chazoeil went to [greet] him and took a gift in his hand, and all the best of Damesek, forty camel loads. He came and stood before him, and said, “Your son, Ben-Hadad king of Arom, sent me to you, saying, ‘Will I recover from this sickness?’”


###### II Kings 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:10)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֵלָיו֙ אֱלִישָׁ֔ע לֵ֥ךְ אֱמׇר־[ל֖וֹ] (לא) חָיֹ֣ה תִֽחְיֶ֑ה וְהִרְאַ֥נִי יְהֹוָ֖ה כִּי־מ֥וֹת יָמֽוּת׃


And Elisha said to him, “Go say to him, ‘You will surely live,’ but Adonoy has informed me that he will surely die.”


###### II Kings 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:11)


וַיַּעֲמֵ֥ד אֶת־פָּנָ֖יו וַיָּ֣שֶׂם עַד־בֹּ֑שׁ וַיֵּ֖בְךְּ אִ֥ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


He [Elisha] kept his face emotionless and held it so for a long time, and then the man of God wept.


###### II Kings 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:12)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חֲזָאֵ֔ל מַדּ֖וּעַ אֲדֹנִ֣י בֹכֶ֑ה וַיֹּ֡אמֶר כִּֽי־יָדַ֡עְתִּי אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁר־תַּעֲשֶׂה֩ לִבְנֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל רָעָ֗ה מִבְצְרֵיהֶ֞ם תְּשַׁלַּ֤ח בָּאֵשׁ֙ וּבַחֻֽרֵיהֶם֙ בַּחֶ֣רֶב תַּהֲרֹ֔ג וְעֹלְלֵיהֶ֣ם תְּרַטֵּ֔שׁ וְהָרֹתֵיהֶ֖ם תְּבַקֵּֽעַ׃


Chazoeil said, “Why is my master weeping?” He said, “Because I know that you will do evil to the Bnei Yisroel; their strongholds you will set on fire, their youths you will kill with the sword, their infants you will dash to pieces, and their pregnant women you will split open.”


###### II Kings 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:13)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חֲזָהאֵ֔ל כִּ֣י מָ֤ה עַבְדְּךָ֙ הַכֶּ֔לֶב כִּ֣י יַעֲשֶׂ֔ה הַדָּבָ֥ר הַגָּד֖וֹל הַזֶּ֑ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֱלִישָׁ֔ע הִרְאַ֧נִי יְהֹוָ֛ה אֹתְךָ֖ מֶ֥לֶךְ עַל־אֲרָֽם׃


And Chazoeil said, “What is your servant but a [mere] dog that he can do this terrible thing?” And Elisha said, “Adonoy has revealed to me that you are to be the king of Arom.”


###### II Kings 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:14)


וַיֵּ֣לֶךְ**׀** מֵאֵ֣ת אֱלִישָׁ֗ע וַיָּבֹא֙ אֶל־אֲדֹנָ֔יו וַיֹּ֣אמֶר ל֔וֹ מָה־אָמַ֥ר לְךָ֖ אֱלִישָׁ֑ע וַיֹּ֕אמֶר אָ֥מַר לִ֖י חָיֹ֥ה תִֽחְיֶֽה׃


He left Elisha and came to his master, and he said to him, “What did Elisha tell you?” He said, “He said to me, ‘You will surely live.’”


###### II Kings 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:15)


וַיְהִ֣י מִֽמׇּחֳרָ֗ת וַיִּקַּ֤ח הַמַּכְבֵּר֙ וַיִּטְבֹּ֣ל בַּמַּ֔יִם וַיִּפְרֹ֥שׂ עַל־פָּנָ֖יו וַיָּמֹ֑ת וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ חֲזָהאֵ֖ל תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


The next day he [Chazoeil] took a blanket and dipped it in water; he spread it over his [Ben-Hadad’s] face and he died. Chazoeil became king in his place.


###### II Kings 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:16)


וּבִשְׁנַ֣ת חָמֵ֗שׁ לְיוֹרָ֤ם בֶּן־אַחְאָב֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וִיהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה מָלַ֛ךְ יְהוֹרָ֥ם בֶּן־יְהוֹשָׁפָ֖ט מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


In the fifth year of Yorom son of Achov king of Yisroel, and Yehoshophot king of Yehudah, Yehorom, son of Yehoshophot became king. king of Yisroel.


###### II Kings 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:17)


בֶּן־שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים וּשְׁתַּ֛יִם שָׁנָ֖ה הָיָ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וּשְׁמֹנֶ֣ה (שנה) [שָׁנִ֔ים] מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


He was thirty-two years old when he became king, and he reigned for eight years in Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:18)


וַיֵּ֜לֶךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ**׀** מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשׂוּ֙ בֵּ֣ית אַחְאָ֔ב כִּ֚י בַּת־אַחְאָ֔ב הָ֥יְתָה לּ֖וֹ לְאִשָּׁ֑ה וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


He followed in the way of the kings of Yisroel, as the house of Achov had done, for Achov’s daughter became his wife. He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:19)


וְלֹא־אָבָ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ לְהַשְׁחִ֣ית אֶת־יְהוּדָ֔ה לְמַ֖עַן דָּוִ֣ד עַבְדּ֑וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר אָמַר־ל֗וֹ לָתֵ֨ת ל֥וֹ נִ֛יר לְבָנָ֖יו כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃


[However] Adonoy did not want to destroy Yehudah for the sake of His servant, Dovid, as He had said to him, to give him a kingdom for his descendants all the days.


###### II Kings 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:20)


בְּיָמָיו֙ פָּשַׁ֣ע אֱד֔וֹם מִתַּ֖חַת יַד־יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיַּמְלִ֥כוּ עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מֶֽלֶךְ׃


In his days, Edom rebelled against Yehudah’s rule, and appointed a king over themselves.


###### II Kings 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/8:21)


וַיַּעֲבֹ֤ר יוֹרָם֙ צָעִ֔ירָה וְכׇל־הָרֶ֖כֶב עִמּ֑וֹ וַֽיְהִי־ה֞וּא קָ֣ם לַ֗יְלָה וַיַּכֶּ֨ה אֶת־אֱד֜וֹם הַסֹּבֵ֤יב אֵלָיו֙ וְאֵת֙ שָׂרֵ֣י הָרֶ֔כֶב וַיָּ֥נׇס הָעָ֖ם לְאֹהָלָֽיו׃


So Yorom went over to [attack] Tzo’ir, and all his chariots were with him. He got up at night and struck Edom that were surrounding him, and the officers of the chariots; then the people fled to their tents.

And king Yorom returned to recuperate in Yizre’el from the wounds that were inflicted upon him by the Aromeans in Romoh when he fought Cagainst hazoeil king of Arom. Achazyohu, son of Yehorom, king of Yehudah, went down to see Yorom, son of Achov, in Yizre’el, for he was sick.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:28)


וַיַּשְׁמֵ֥ד יֵה֛וּא אֶת־הַבַּ֖עַל מִיִּשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And thus Yeihu obliterated Baal from Yisroel.


###### II Kings 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:29)


רַ֠ק חֲטָאֵ֞י יָרׇבְעָ֤ם בֶּן־נְבָט֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֶחֱטִ֣יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לֹא־סָ֥ר יֵה֖וּא מֵאַֽחֲרֵיהֶ֑ם עֶגְלֵי֙ הַזָּהָ֔ב אֲשֶׁ֥ר בֵּֽית־אֵ֖ל וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּדָֽן׃{פ}


However, from the sins of Yorovom, son of Nevot, who caused Yisroel to sin, Yeihu did not turn away from them, the Golden calves that were in Beis Eil and in Don.


###### II Kings 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:30)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶל־יֵה֗וּא יַ֤עַן אֲשֶׁר־הֱטִיבֹ֙תָ֙ לַעֲשׂ֤וֹת הַיָּשָׁר֙ בְּעֵינַ֔י כְּכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּלְבָבִ֔י עָשִׂ֖יתָ לְבֵ֣ית אַחְאָ֑ב בְּנֵ֣י רְבִעִ֔ים יֵשְׁב֥וּ לְךָ֖ עַל־כִּסֵּ֥א יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Adonoy said to Yeihu, “Because you have done well, doing that which is upright in My eyes; and all that is in My heart you have done to the house of Achov, four generations of your sons will sit on the throne of Yisroel.


###### II Kings 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:31)


וְיֵה֗וּא לֹ֥א שָׁמַ֛ר לָלֶ֛כֶת בְּתֽוֹרַת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּכׇל־לְבָב֑וֹ לֹ֣א סָ֗ר מֵעַל֙ חַטֹּ֣אות יָרׇבְעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶחֱטִ֖יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


But Yeihu was not observant in following the Torah of Adonoy, God of Yisroel, with all his heart. He did not reject the sins of Yorovom who caused Yisroel to sin.


###### II Kings 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:32)


בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֔ם הֵחֵ֣ל יְהֹוָ֔ה לְקַצּ֖וֹת בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיַּכֵּ֥ם חֲזָאֵ֖ל בְּכׇל־גְּב֥וּל יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


In those days Adonoy began to show His disgust with Yisroel, and Chazoeil struck them along the entire border of Yisroel.


###### II Kings 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:33)


מִן־הַיַּרְדֵּן֙ מִזְרַ֣ח הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ אֵ֚ת כׇּל־אֶ֣רֶץ הַגִּלְעָ֔ד הַגָּדִ֥י וְהָראוּבֵנִ֖י וְהַֽמְנַשִּׁ֑י מֵֽעֲרֹעֵר֙ אֲשֶׁר־עַל־נַ֣חַל אַרְנֹ֔ן וְהַגִּלְעָ֖ד וְהַבָּשָֽׁן׃


From the Yardein toward the rising sun, the entire land of Gilod, the [territories of] Gad, Reuvein, and Menashe; from Aroeir by the Arnon River, and the Gilod and the Boshon.


###### II Kings 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/10:34)


וְיֶ֨תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יֵה֛וּא וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה וְכׇל־גְּבוּרָת֑וֹ הֲלוֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


The rest of Yeihu’s events, and all that he did, and all of his might, they are indeed written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Yisroel.

וַיִּשְׁכַּ֤ב יֵהוּא֙ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֔יו וַיִּקְבְּר֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ בְּשֹׁמְר֑וֹן וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ יְהוֹאָחָ֥ז בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


And Yeihu lay with his forefathers and they buried him in Shomron. His son, Yehoachaz, reigned in his place.

The days that Yeihu reigned over Yisroel were twenty-eight years in Shomron.



## Chapter 11

###### II Kings 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:1)


וַֽעֲתַלְיָה֙ אֵ֣ם אֲחַזְיָ֔הוּ (וראתה) [רָאֲתָ֖ה] כִּ֣י מֵ֣ת בְּנָ֑הּ וַתָּ֙קׇם֙ וַתְּאַבֵּ֔ד אֵ֖ת כׇּל־זֶ֥רַע הַמַּמְלָכָֽה׃


When Asalyoh, Achazyohu’s mother, saw that her son was dead, she got up and destroyed all the descendants of the royal family.


###### II Kings 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:2)


וַתִּקַּ֣ח יְהוֹשֶׁ֣בַע בַּת־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ־י֠וֹרָ֠ם אֲח֨וֹת אֲחַזְיָ֜הוּ אֶת־יוֹאָ֣שׁ בֶּן־אֲחַזְיָ֗ה וַתִּגְנֹ֤ב אֹתוֹ֙ מִתּ֤וֹךְ בְּנֵֽי־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ (הממותתים) [הַמּ֣וּמָתִ֔ים] אֹת֥וֹ וְאֶת־מֵינִקְתּ֖וֹ בַּחֲדַ֣ר הַמִּטּ֑וֹת וַיַּסְתִּ֧רוּ אֹת֛וֹ מִפְּנֵ֥י עֲתַלְיָ֖הוּ וְלֹ֥א הוּמָֽת׃


But Yehosheva, daughter of King Arom, took she was the sister of Achazyohu— Yoash son of Achazyoh, and she stole him away from among the children of the king who were being killed. [She smuggled] him and his nurse into the bed-chamber, and she concealed him from Asalyohu, and he was not killed.


###### II Kings 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:3)


וַיְהִ֤י אִתָּהּ֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה מִתְחַבֵּ֖א שֵׁ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֑ים וַעֲתַלְיָ֖ה מֹלֶ֥כֶת עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


And he remained with her in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, hidden for six years, and Asalyoh ruled over the land.


###### II Kings 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:4)


וּבַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַ֠שְּׁבִיעִ֠ית שָׁלַ֨ח יְהוֹיָדָ֜ע וַיִּקַּ֣ח**׀** אֶת־שָׂרֵ֣י (המאיות) [הַמֵּא֗וֹת] לַכָּרִי֙ וְלָ֣רָצִ֔ים וַיָּבֵ֥א אֹתָ֛ם אֵלָ֖יו בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּכְרֹת֩ לָהֶ֨ם בְּרִ֜ית וַיַּשְׁבַּ֤ע אֹתָם֙ בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיַּ֥רְא אֹתָ֖ם אֶת־בֶּן־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


In the seventh year, Yehoyoda summoned, and he took the officers of the hundreds [who were over] the mighty warriors and the runners, and he brought them to him in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy. He made a covenant with them and beswore them in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and then he showed them the king’s son.


###### II Kings 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:5)


וַיְצַוֵּ֣ם לֵאמֹ֔ר זֶ֥ה הַדָּבָ֖ר אֲשֶׁ֣ר תַּעֲשׂ֑וּן הַשְּׁלִשִׁ֤ית מִכֶּם֙ בָּאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת וְשֹׁ֣מְרֵ֔י מִשְׁמֶ֖רֶת בֵּ֥ית הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


He commanded them saying, “This is what you are to do: one third of you, of those who come on Shabbos, will keep the watch of the king’s palace.


###### II Kings 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:6)


וְהַשְּׁלִשִׁית֙ בְּשַׁ֣עַר ס֔וּר וְהַשְּׁלִשִׁ֥ית בַּשַּׁ֖עַר אַחַ֣ר הָרָצִ֑ים וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֛ם אֶת־מִשְׁמֶ֥רֶת הַבַּ֖יִת מַסָּֽח׃


One third [will be] at the Sur Gate, and one third [will be] at the gate behind the runners; and you will keep the watch of the palace, without taking your mind off it.


###### II Kings 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:7)


וּשְׁתֵּ֤י הַיָּדוֹת֙ בָּכֶ֔ם כֹּ֖ל יֹצְאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת וְשָׁ֥מְר֛וּ אֶת־מִשְׁמֶ֥רֶת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


The two groups of you, [with] all who depart on Shabbos, will guard the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, for the king.


###### II Kings 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:8)


וְהִקַּפְתֶּ֨ם עַל־הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ סָבִ֗יב אִ֚ישׁ וְכֵלָ֣יו בְּיָד֔וֹ וְהַבָּ֥א אֶל־הַשְּׂדֵר֖וֹת יוּמָ֑ת וִֽהְי֥וּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בְּצֵאת֥וֹ וּבְבֹאֽוֹ׃


You will encircle the king all around, each man with his weapons in his hand, and whoever breaks the ranks will be killed. You will be with the king when he goes out and when he comes in.”


###### II Kings 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:9)


וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֞וּ שָׂרֵ֣י (המאיות) [הַמֵּא֗וֹת] כְּכֹ֣ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּה֮ יְהוֹיָדָ֣ע הַכֹּהֵן֒ וַיִּקְחוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־אֲנָשָׁ֔יו בָּאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת עִ֖ם יֹצְאֵ֣י הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ אֶל־יְהוֹיָדָ֥ע הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


The officers of hundreds did everything that Yehoyoda, the *kohein* commanded. Each one took his men, those that were coming in for Shabbos, and those that were departing on Shabbos; and they came to Yehoyoda, the *kohein*.


###### II Kings 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:10)


וַיִּתֵּ֨ן הַכֹּהֵ֜ן לְשָׂרֵ֣י (המאיות) [הַמֵּא֗וֹת] אֶֽת־הַחֲנִית֙ וְאֶת־הַשְּׁלָטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ דָּוִ֑ד אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And the *kohein*gave the officers of the hundreds the spear[s] and the shields of King Dovid, that were in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:11)


וַיַּעַמְד֨וּ הָרָצִ֜ים אִ֣ישׁ**׀** וְכֵלָ֣יו בְּיָד֗וֹ מִכֶּ֨תֶף הַבַּ֤יִת הַיְמָנִית֙ עַד־כֶּ֤תֶף הַבַּ֙יִת֙ הַשְּׂמָאלִ֔ית לַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ וְלַבָּ֑יִת עַל־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ סָבִֽיב׃


The runners stood, each one with his weapons in his hand, from the right side of the Beis [Hamikdosh] to the left side of the Beis [Hamikdosh] at the Altar and the Sanctuary, surrounding the king.


###### II Kings 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:12)


וַיּוֹצִ֣א אֶת־בֶּן־הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וַיִּתֵּ֤ן עָלָיו֙ אֶת־הַנֵּ֙זֶר֙ וְאֶת־הָ֣עֵד֔וּת וַיַּמְלִ֥כוּ אֹת֖וֹ וַיִּמְשָׁחֻ֑הוּ וַיַּ֨כּוּ־כָ֔ף וַיֹּאמְר֖וּ יְחִ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃{ס}


He [Yehoyoda] brought out the king’s son and placed the crown on him, and [gave him] the Testimony [the *Seifer Torah*]. They proclaimed him king and anointed him. They applauded and declared, “[Long] live the king!”


###### II Kings 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:13)


וַתִּשְׁמַ֣ע עֲתַלְיָ֔ה אֶת־ק֥וֹל הָרָצִ֖ין הָעָ֑ם וַתָּבֹ֥א אֶל־הָעָ֖ם בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Asalyoh heard the sound of running people, and she came to the people in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:14)


וַתֵּ֡רֶא וְהִנֵּ֣ה הַמֶּ֩לֶךְ֩ עֹמֵ֨ד עַֽל־הָעַמּ֜וּד כַּמִּשְׁפָּ֗ט וְהַשָּׂרִ֤ים וְהַחֲצֹֽצְרוֹת֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְכׇל־עַ֤ם הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ שָׂמֵ֔חַ וְתֹקֵ֖עַ בַּחֲצֹֽצְר֑וֹת וַתִּקְרַ֤ע עֲתַלְיָה֙ אֶת־בְּגָדֶ֔יהָ וַתִּקְרָ֖א קֶ֥שֶׁר קָֽשֶׁר׃


She looked and behold, the king was standing on the pedestal as was the custom, and the officers and the trumpets were next to the king, and all the people of the land were rejoicing and blowing the trumpets. Asalyoh tore her clothing, and shouted, “A revolt! A revolt!”


###### II Kings 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:15)


וַיְצַו֩ יְהוֹיָדָ֨ע הַכֹּהֵ֜ן אֶת־שָׂרֵ֥י (המאיות) [הַמֵּא֣וֹת**׀**] פְּקֻדֵ֣י הַחַ֗יִל וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶם֙ הוֹצִ֤יאוּ אֹתָהּ֙ אֶל־מִבֵּ֣ית לַשְּׂדֵרֹ֔ת וְהַבָּ֥א אַחֲרֶ֖יהָ הָמֵ֣ת בֶּחָ֑רֶב כִּ֚י אָמַ֣ר הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אַל־תּוּמַ֖ת בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Yehoyoda the *kohein*commanded the officers of the hundreds, who were appointed over the army and he said to them, “Remove her by way of the ranks, and anyone who follows her kill with the sword.” For the *kohein* had said, “Let her not be killed in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.”


###### II Kings 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:16)


וַיָּשִׂ֤מוּ לָהּ֙ יָדַ֔יִם וַתָּב֛וֹא דֶּרֶךְ־מְב֥וֹא הַסּוּסִ֖ים בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַתּוּמַ֖ת שָֽׁם׃{ס}


They made place for her and she came by way of the horses’ entrance to the king’s palace, and she was executed there.


###### II Kings 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:17)


וַיִּכְרֹ֨ת יְהוֹיָדָ֜ע אֶֽת־הַבְּרִ֗ית בֵּ֤ין יְהֹוָה֙ וּבֵ֤ין הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וּבֵ֣ין הָעָ֔ם לִהְי֥וֹת לְעָ֖ם לַיהֹוָ֑ה וּבֵ֥ין הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וּבֵ֥ין הָעָֽם׃


Yehoyoda made a covenant between Adonoy and the king, and the people to be a people of Adonoy; and between the king and the people.


###### II Kings 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:18)


וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ כׇל־עַם֩ הָאָ֨רֶץ בֵּית־הַבַּ֜עַל וַֽיִּתְּצֻ֗הוּ אֶת־מִזְבְּחֹתָ֤ו וְאֶת־צְלָמָיו֙ שִׁבְּר֣וּ הֵיטֵ֔ב וְאֵ֗ת מַתָּן֙ כֹּהֵ֣ן הַבַּ֔עַל הָרְג֖וּ לִפְנֵ֣י הַֽמִּזְבְּח֑וֹת וַיָּ֧שֶׂם הַכֹּהֵ֛ן פְּקֻדֹּ֖ת עַל־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And all the people of the land came to the temple of the Baal and tore it down; its altars and its statues they broke completely. Matan, the priest of the Baal, they killed in front of the altars. Then [Yehoyoda] the *kohein* set up assignments for the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:19)


וַיִּקַּ֣ח אֶת־שָׂרֵ֣י הַ֠מֵּא֠וֹת וְאֶת־הַכָּרִ֨י וְאֶת־הָרָצִ֜ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־עַ֣ם הָאָ֗רֶץ וַיֹּרִ֤ידוּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיָּב֛וֹאוּ דֶּרֶךְ־שַׁ֥עַר הָרָצִ֖ים בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֵּ֖שֶׁב עַל־כִּסֵּ֥א הַמְּלָכִֽים׃


He took the officers of the hundreds, the mighty warriors and the runners, and all the people of the land, and they escorted the king down from the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy. They came by way of the Gate of the Runners to the king’s palace, and he sat on the royal throne.


###### II Kings 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/11:20)


וַיִּשְׂמַ֥ח כׇּל־עַם־הָאָ֖רֶץ וְהָעִ֣יר שָׁקָ֑טָה וְאֶת־עֲתַלְיָ֛הוּ הֵמִ֥יתוּ בַחֶ֖רֶב בֵּ֥ית (מלך) [הַמֶּֽלֶךְ]׃{ס}


All the people of the land rejoiced and the city was tranquil. They killed Asalyohu with the sword in the king’s palace.



## Chapter 12

###### II Kings 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:1)


בֶּן־שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִ֖ים יְהוֹאָ֥שׁ בְּמׇלְכֽוֹ׃{פ}


Yehoash was seven years old when he became king.


###### II Kings 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:2)


בִּשְׁנַת־שֶׁ֤בַע לְיֵהוּא֙ מָלַ֣ךְ יְהוֹאָ֔שׁ וְאַרְבָּעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ צִבְיָ֖ה מִבְּאֵ֥ר שָֽׁבַע׃


In the seventh year of Yeihu, Yehoash became king, and he reigned forty years in Yerusholayim. His mother’s name was Tzivia from B’eir Sheva.


###### II Kings 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:3)


וַיַּ֨עַשׂ יְהוֹאָ֧שׁ הַיָּשָׁ֛ר בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה כׇּל־יָמָ֑יו אֲשֶׁ֣ר הוֹרָ֔הוּ יְהוֹיָדָ֖ע הַכֹּהֵֽן׃


Yehoash did what was proper in the eyes of Adonoy all his days, as Yehoyoda, the *kohein*, instructed him.


###### II Kings 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:4)


רַ֥ק הַבָּמ֖וֹת לֹא־סָ֑רוּ ע֥וֹד הָעָ֛ם מְזַבְּחִ֥ים וּֽמְקַטְּרִ֖ים בַּבָּמֽוֹת׃


Except that the individual altars were not removed; the people were still sacrificing and burning incense on [their] individual altars.


###### II Kings 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:5)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהוֹאָ֜שׁ אֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִ֗ים כֹּל֩ כֶּ֨סֶף הַקֳּדָשִׁ֜ים אֲשֶׁר־יוּבָ֤א בֵית־יְהֹוָה֙ כֶּ֣סֶף עוֹבֵ֔ר אִ֕ישׁ כֶּ֥סֶף נַפְשׁ֖וֹת עֶרְכּ֑וֹ כׇּל־כֶּ֗סֶף אֲשֶׁ֤ר יַֽעֲלֶה֙ עַ֣ל לֶב־אִ֔ישׁ לְהָבִ֖יא בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And Yehoash said to the *kohanim*, “All the sacred money that is brought to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, the half-shekel [given by every male over twenty, and] donations representing the value of one’s age, all monies that a person’s heart inspires him to bring to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:6)


יִקְח֤וּ לָהֶם֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים אִ֖ישׁ מֵאֵ֣ת מַכָּר֑וֹ וְהֵ֗ם יְחַזְּקוּ֙ אֶת־בֶּ֣דֶק הַבַּ֔יִת לְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יִמָּצֵ֥א שָׁ֖ם בָּֽדֶק׃{פ}


Let them take [the money] for themselves— the *kohanim*— each one from his friend; and they will support the restoration of the Beis [Hamikdosh] wherever the need for restoration is found.”


###### II Kings 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:7)


וַיְהִ֗י בִּשְׁנַ֨ת עֶשְׂרִ֧ים וְשָׁלֹ֛שׁ שָׁנָ֖ה לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יְהוֹאָ֑שׁ לֹא־חִזְּק֥וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים אֶת־בֶּ֥דֶק הַבָּֽיִת׃


In the twenty-third year of king Yehoash, the *kohanim*had [still] not supported the restoration of the Beis [Hamikdosh].


###### II Kings 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:8)


וַיִּקְרָא֩ הַמֶּ֨לֶךְ יְהוֹאָ֜שׁ לִיהוֹיָדָ֤ע הַכֹּהֵן֙ וְלַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֔ם מַדּ֛וּעַ אֵינְכֶ֥ם מְחַזְּקִ֖ים אֶת־בֶּ֣דֶק הַבָּ֑יִת וְעַתָּ֗ה אַל־תִּקְחֽוּ־כֶ֙סֶף֙ מֵאֵ֣ת מַכָּרֵיכֶ֔ם כִּֽי־לְבֶ֥דֶק הַבַּ֖יִת תִּתְּנֻֽהוּ׃


King Yehoash summoned Yehoyoda the *kohein*, and the [other] *kohanim*, and said to them, “Why are you not supporting the restoration of the Beis [Hamikdosh]? And now, do not take the money from your friends [for yourselves], but for the restoration of the Beis [Hamikdosh]— you should give it over


###### II Kings 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:9)


וַיֵּאֹ֖תוּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֑ים לְבִלְתִּ֤י קְחַת־כֶּ֙סֶף֙ מֵאֵ֣ת הָעָ֔ם וּלְבִלְתִּ֥י חַזֵּ֖ק אֶת־בֶּ֥דֶק הַבָּֽיִת׃


The *kohanim* agreed not to take money from the people, and not to support [from their own funds] the restoration of the Beis [Hamikdosh].


###### II Kings 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:10)


וַיִּקַּ֞ח יְהוֹיָדָ֤ע הַכֹּהֵן֙ אֲר֣וֹן אֶחָ֔ד וַיִּקֹּ֥ב חֹ֖ר בְּדַלְתּ֑וֹ וַיִּתֵּ֣ן אֹתוֹ֩ אֵ֨צֶל הַמִּזְבֵּ֜חַ (בימין) [מִיָּמִ֗ין] בְּבוֹא־אִישׁ֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנָֽתְנוּ־שָׁ֤מָּה הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ שֹׁמְרֵ֣י הַסַּ֔ף אֶת־כׇּל־הַכֶּ֖סֶף הַמּוּבָ֥א בֵית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And Yehoyoda the *kohein* took one box and bored a hole in its lid; he placed it near the altar to the right, as a person enters the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy. And they put in there— the guards of the threshold— all the money that was brought to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:11)


וַֽיְהִי֙ כִּרְאוֹתָ֔ם כִּי־רַ֥ב הַכֶּ֖סֶף בָּאָר֑וֹן וַיַּ֨עַל סֹפֵ֤ר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְהַכֹּהֵ֣ן הַגָּד֔וֹל וַיָּצֻ֙רוּ֙ וַיִּמְנ֔וּ אֶת־הַכֶּ֖סֶף הַנִּמְצָ֥א בֵית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Whenever they saw that there was much money in the box, the king’s scribe would go up— and the *Kohein Gadol*— and bag and count the money that was found in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:12)


וְנָֽתְנוּ֙ אֶת־הַכֶּ֣סֶף הַֽמְתֻכָּ֔ן עַל־[יְדֵי֙] (יד) עֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה (הפקדים) [הַמֻּפְקָדִ֖ים] בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיּוֹצִיאֻ֜הוּ לְחָרָשֵׁ֤י הָעֵץ֙ וְלַבֹּנִ֔ים הָעֹשִׂ֖ים בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


They gave the counted money to those who oversaw the work, who were [thus] appointed over the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy. They paid the carpenters and the builders who worked in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:13)


וְלַגֹּֽדְרִים֙ וּלְחֹצְבֵ֣י הָאֶ֔בֶן וְלִקְנ֤וֹת עֵצִים֙ וְאַבְנֵ֣י מַחְצֵ֔ב לְחַזֵּ֖ק אֶת־בֶּ֣דֶק בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּלְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־יֵצֵ֥א עַל־הַבַּ֖יִת לְחׇזְקָֽה׃


And [they] also [paid] the masons and the stone cutters, and [also used the money] to buy lumber and quarried stones [that were needed] to restore the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and for whatever expenditures [were necessary] to restore the Beis [Hamikdosh].


###### II Kings 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:14)


אַךְ֩ לֹ֨א יֵעָשֶׂ֜ה בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֗ה סִפּ֥וֹת כֶּ֙סֶף֙ מְזַמְּר֤וֹת מִזְרָקוֹת֙ חֲצֹ֣צְר֔וֹת כׇּל־כְּלִ֥י זָהָ֖ב וּכְלִי־כָ֑סֶף מִן־הַכֶּ֖סֶף הַמּוּבָ֥א בֵית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


However, there would not be made for the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, silver pitchers, musical instruments, basins, trumpets, or any gold or silver utensils, from the money brought to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:15)


כִּֽי־לְעֹשֵׂ֥י הַמְּלָאכָ֖ה יִתְּנֻ֑הוּ וְחִזְּקוּ־ב֖וֹ אֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


For to those who supervised the work, did they gave it and with it they supported [the restoration of] the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:16)


וְלֹ֧א יְחַשְּׁב֣וּ אֶת־הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִתְּנ֤וּ אֶת־הַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ עַל־יָדָ֔ם לָתֵ֖ת לְעֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֑ה כִּ֥י בֶאֱמֻנָ֖ה הֵ֥ם עֹשִֽׂים׃


They did not demand an accounting from the men into whose hands they gave the money to pay those who supervised the work; for they worked on trust.


###### II Kings 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:17)


כֶּ֤סֶף אָשָׁם֙ וְכֶ֣סֶף חַטָּא֔וֹת לֹ֥א יוּבָ֖א בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה לַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים יִהְיֽוּ׃{פ}


Money for guilt-offerings and money for sin-offerings was not brought to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy; it was for the *kohanim*.


###### II Kings 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:18)


אָ֣ז יַעֲלֶ֗ה חֲזָאֵל֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָ֔ם וַיִּלָּ֥חֶם עַל־גַּ֖ת וַֽיִּלְכְּדָ֑הּ וַיָּ֤שֶׂם חֲזָאֵל֙ פָּנָ֔יו לַעֲל֖וֹת עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Then Chazoeil king of Arom went up, and fought with Gas and conquered it; then Chazoeil set his face to go up against Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:19)


וַיִּקַּ֞ח יְהוֹאָ֣שׁ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֗ה אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַקֳּדָשִׁ֡ים אֲשֶׁר־הִקְדִּ֣ישׁוּ יְהוֹשָׁפָ֣ט וִיהוֹרָם֩ וַאֲחַזְיָ֨הוּ אֲבֹתָ֜יו מַלְכֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ וְאֶת־קֳדָשָׁ֔יו וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַזָּהָ֗ב הַנִּמְצָ֛א בְּאֹצְר֥וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה וּבֵ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיִּשְׁלַ֗ח לַֽחֲזָאֵל֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָ֔ם וַיַּ֖עַל מֵעַ֥ל יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Yehoash king of Yehudah, took all the sacred things that were sanctified by Yehoshophot, Yehorom and Achazyohu, his forefathers the kings of Yehudah, and his own sacred things, and all the gold that was found in the treasuries of the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy and in the king’s palace; and he sent it to Chazoeil, king of Arom, and he turned back from upon Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 12:20

וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יוֹאָ֖שׁ וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה הֲלוֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


And the rest of Yoash’s events and all that he did, are indeed written in the book of the chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.


###### II Kings 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:21)


וַיָּקֻ֥מוּ עֲבָדָ֖יו וַיִּקְשְׁרוּ־קָ֑שֶׁר וַיַּכּוּ֙ אֶת־יוֹאָ֔שׁ בֵּ֥ית מִלֹּ֖א הַיֹּרֵ֥ד סִלָּֽא׃


His servants rose up and revolted, and they killed Yoash at Beis Millo which leads down to Selo.


###### II Kings 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/12:22)


וְיוֹזָכָ֣ר בֶּן־שִׁ֠מְעָ֠ת וִיהוֹזָבָ֨ד בֶּן־שֹׁמֵ֤ר׀עֲבָדָיו֙ הִכֻּ֣הוּ וַיָּמֹ֔ת וַֽיִּקְבְּר֥וּ אֹת֛וֹ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֖יו בְּעִ֣יר דָּוִ֑ד וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אֲמַצְיָ֥ה בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


Yozochor, son of Shimos, and Yehozovod, son of Shomeir, his servants, struck him and he died. They buried him with his forefathers in the City of Dovid. His son Amatzyoh reigned in his place.

In the twenty-third year of Yoash, son of Achazyohu king of Yehudah, Yehoachaz son of Yeihu, reigned over Yisroel in Shomron for seventeen years.

###### II Kings 13:21
