וַיְהִ֞י הֵ֣ם**׀** קֹבְרִ֣ים אִ֗ישׁ וְהִנֵּה֙ רָא֣וּ אֶֽת־הַגְּד֔וּד וַיַּשְׁלִ֥יכוּ אֶת־הָאִ֖ישׁ בְּקֶ֣בֶר אֱלִישָׁ֑ע וַיֵּ֜לֶךְ וַיִּגַּ֤ע הָאִישׁ֙ בְּעַצְמ֣וֹת אֱלִישָׁ֔ע וַיְחִ֖י וַיָּ֥קׇם עַל־רַגְלָֽיו׃{פ}


They were in the midst of burying a man and behold, they saw a band [approaching] so they threw the corpse into Elisha’s grave; the corpse rolled and touched Elisha’s bones, and it came back to life and stood up.


###### II Kings 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:22)


וַֽחֲזָאֵל֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אֲרָ֔ם לָחַ֖ץ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֥י יְהוֹאָחָֽז׃


Chazoeil king of Arom oppressed Yisroel all the days of Yehoachaz[’s reign].


###### II Kings 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:23)


וַיָּ֩חׇן֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֹתָ֤ם וַֽיְרַחֲמֵם֙ וַיִּ֣פֶן אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם לְמַ֣עַן בְּרִית֔וֹ אֶת־אַבְרָהָ֖ם יִצְחָ֣ק וְיַעֲקֹ֑ב וְלֹ֤א אָבָה֙ הַשְׁחִיתָ֔ם וְלֹא־הִשְׁלִיכָ֥ם מֵעַל־פָּנָ֖יו עַד־עָֽתָּה׃


But Adonoy was gracious and had pity on them, and He turned back to them because of His covenant with Avrohom, Yitzchok and Yaakov; he did not want to destroy them, and He did not cast them off from His Presence until now.


###### II Kings 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:24)


וַיָּ֖מׇת חֲזָאֵ֣ל מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲרָ֑ם וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ בֶּן־הֲדַ֥ד בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


Chazoeil, Arom’s king, died; his son, Ben-Hadad, reigned in his place.


###### II Kings 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/13:25)


וַיָּ֜שׇׁב יְהוֹאָ֣שׁ בֶּן־יְהוֹאָחָ֗ז וַיִּקַּ֤ח אֶת־הֶעָרִים֙ מִיַּד֙ בֶּן־הֲדַ֣ד בֶּן־חֲזָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָקַ֗ח מִיַּ֛ד יְהוֹאָחָ֥ז אָבִ֖יו בַּמִּלְחָמָ֑ה שָׁלֹ֤שׁ פְּעָמִים֙ הִכָּ֣הוּ יוֹאָ֔שׁ וַיָּ֖שֶׁב אֶת־עָרֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


Yehoash, son of Yehoachaz, returned and took back the cities from the hand of Ben-Hadad, son of Chazoeil, who had taken [them] from the hand of his father Yehoachaz in battle; Yoash struck him three times and recovered the cities of Yisroel.



## Chapter 14

###### II Kings 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שְׁתַּ֔יִם לְיוֹאָ֥שׁ בֶּן־יוֹאָחָ֖ז מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל מָלַ֛ךְ אֲמַצְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־יוֹאָ֖שׁ מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


In the second year of Yoash, son of Yoachaz king of Yisroel, Amatzyoh, son of Yoash became king— king of Yehudah.


###### II Kings 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:2)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ הָיָ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְעֶשְׂרִ֤ים וָתֵ֙שַׁע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ (יהועדין) [יְהוֹעַדָּ֖ן] מִן־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


He was twenty-five years old when he became king, and for twenty-nine years; he reigned in Yerusholayim his mother’s name was Yehoadon of Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:3)


וַיַּ֤עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁר֙ בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה רַ֕ק לֹ֖א כְּדָוִ֣ד אָבִ֑יו כְּכֹ֧ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֛ה יוֹאָ֥שׁ אָבִ֖יו עָשָֽׂה׃


He did what was proper in the eyes of Adonoy, but not like his forefather Dovid; he did everything as Yoash his father had done.


###### II Kings 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:4)


רַ֥ק הַבָּמ֖וֹת לֹא־סָ֑רוּ ע֥וֹד הָעָ֛ם מְזַבְּחִ֥ים וּֽמְקַטְּרִ֖ים בַּבָּמֽוֹת׃


However, the private altars were not removed, the people continued slaughtering and burning incense on the private altars.


###### II Kings 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:5)


וַיְהִ֕י כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר חָזְקָ֥ה הַמַּמְלָכָ֖ה בְּיָד֑וֹ וַיַּךְ֙ אֶת־עֲבָדָ֔יו הַמַּכִּ֖ים אֶת־הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ אָבִֽיו׃


And when the kingdom became firmly established, he killed his servants who had murdered the king, his father.


###### II Kings 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:6)


וְאֶת־בְּנֵ֥י הַמַּכִּ֖ים לֹ֣א הֵמִ֑ית כַּכָּת֣וּב בְּסֵֽפֶר־תּֽוֹרַת־מֹ֠שֶׁ֠ה אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֨ה יְהֹוָ֜ה לֵאמֹ֗ר לֹא־יוּמְת֨וּ אָב֤וֹת עַל־בָּנִים֙ וּבָנִים֙ לֹא־יוּמְת֣וּ עַל־אָב֔וֹת כִּ֛י אִם־אִ֥ישׁ בְּחֶטְא֖וֹ (ימות) [יוּמָֽת]׃


But he did not kill the murderers’ children, for it is written in the Book of the Torah of Moshe, which Adonoy had commanded, saying, “Fathers shall not be put to death because of sons, and sons shall not be put to death because of fathers, rather, a person shall be put to death for his own sin.


###### II Kings 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:7)


הוּא־הִכָּ֨ה אֶת־אֱד֤וֹם בְּגֵי־[מֶ֙לַח֙] (המלח) עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת אֲלָפִ֔ים וְתָפַ֥שׂ אֶת־הַסֶּ֖לַע בַּמִּלְחָמָ֑ה וַיִּקְרָ֤א אֶת־שְׁמָהּ֙ יׇקְתְאֵ֔ל עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


He struck of Edom in the Salt Valley— ten thousand and he captured the Rock in battle, and named it Yokseil [as it is known] to this day.


###### II Kings 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:8)


אָ֣ז שָׁלַ֤ח אֲמַצְיָה֙ מַלְאָכִ֔ים אֶל־יְהוֹאָ֨שׁ בֶּן־יְהוֹאָחָ֧ז בֶּן־יֵה֛וּא מֶ֥לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר לְכָ֖ה נִתְרָאֶ֥ה פָנִֽים׃


Amatzyoh then sent messengers to Yehoash, son of Yehoachaz, son of Yeihu, king of Yisroel saying, “Come, let us battle each other.”


###### II Kings 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:9)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֞ח יְהוֹאָ֣שׁ מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אֶל־אֲמַצְיָ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֮ לֵאמֹר֒ הַח֜וֹחַ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּלְּבָנ֗וֹן שָׁ֠לַ֠ח אֶל־הָאֶ֜רֶז אֲשֶׁ֤ר בַּלְּבָנוֹן֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר תְּנָה־אֶת־בִּתְּךָ֥ לִבְנִ֖י לְאִשָּׁ֑ה וַֽתַּעֲבֹ֞ר חַיַּ֤ת הַשָּׂדֶה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּלְּבָנ֔וֹן וַתִּרְמֹ֖ס אֶת־הַחֽוֹחַ׃


And Yehoash sent [back a message]— king of Yisroel. to Amatzyohu, king of Yehudah, saying, “The thistle that was in Levanon sent [a message] to the cedar [tree] that was in Levanon, saying, ‘Give your daughter to my son for a wife,’ and the wild beast that was in Levanon passed by and trampled the thistle.


###### II Kings 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:10)


הַכֵּ֤ה הִכִּ֙יתָ֙ אֶת־אֱד֔וֹם וּֽנְשָׂאֲךָ֖ לִבֶּ֑ךָ הִכָּבֵד֙ וְשֵׁ֣ב בְּבֵיתֶ֔ךָ וְלָ֤מָּה תִתְגָּרֶה֙ בְּרָעָ֔ה וְנָ֣פַלְתָּ֔ה אַתָּ֖ה וִיהוּדָ֥ה עִמָּֽךְ׃


You have soundly defeated Edom so you have become arrogant; keep your honor and stay home. Why should you provoke evil and be defeated, you, and Yehudah together?”


###### II Kings 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:11)


וְלֹא־שָׁמַ֣ע אֲמַצְיָ֔הוּ וַיַּ֨עַל יְהוֹאָ֤שׁ מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וַיִּתְרָא֣וּ פָנִ֔ים ה֖וּא וַאֲמַצְיָ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֑ה בְּבֵ֥ית שֶׁ֖מֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִיהוּדָֽה׃


But Amatzyohu did not listen, so Yehoash, king of Yisroel, went up, and they confronted each other, he and Amatzyoh[u] king of Yehudah, in Beis Shemesh which [belongs] to Yehudah.


###### II Kings 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:12)


וַיִּנָּ֥גֶף יְהוּדָ֖ה לִפְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּנֻ֖סוּ אִ֥ישׁ לְאֹהָלָֽו׃


Yehudah was soundly defeated before Yisroel, and each man fled to his dwelling.


###### II Kings 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:13)


וְאֵת֩ אֲמַצְיָ֨הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֜ה בֶּן־יְהוֹאָ֣שׁ בֶּן־אֲחַזְיָ֗הוּ תָּפַ֛שׂ יְהוֹאָ֥שׁ מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּבֵ֣ית שָׁ֑מֶשׁ (ויבאו) [וַיָּבֹא֙] יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיִּפְרֹץ֩ בְּחוֹמַ֨ת יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם בְּשַׁ֤עַר אֶפְרַ֙יִם֙ עַד־שַׁ֣עַר הַפִּנָּ֔ה אַרְבַּ֥ע מֵא֖וֹת אַמָּֽה׃


And Amatzyohu king of Yehudah, son of Yehoash, son of Achazyahu, was captured by Yehoash king of Yisroel, in Beis Shemesh; he then came to Yerusholayim and breached the wall of Yerusholayim [from] the Ephrayim Gate to the Corner Gate, [a distance of] four hundred *amohs*.


###### II Kings 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:14)


וְלָקַ֣ח אֶת־כׇּל־הַזָּֽהָב־וְ֠הַכֶּ֠סֶף וְאֵ֨ת כׇּל־הַכֵּלִ֜ים הַנִּמְצְאִ֣ים בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֗ה וּבְאֹֽצְרוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְאֵ֖ת בְּנֵ֣י הַתַּעֲרֻב֑וֹת וַיָּ֖שׇׁב שֹׁמְרֽוֹנָה׃


And he took all the gold and silver, and all the vessels that were found in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy and in the palace treasuries, and the children [held] hostage; and he returned to Shomron.


###### II Kings 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:15)


וְיֶ֩תֶר֩ דִּבְרֵ֨י יְהוֹאָ֜שׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ וּגְב֣וּרָת֔וֹ וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר נִלְחַ֔ם עִ֖ם אֲמַצְיָ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֑ה הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


The rest of Yehoash’s deeds which he did, and his might, and how he fought against Amatzyohu king of Yehudah, are indeed written in the book of the chronicles of Yisroel’s kings.


###### II Kings 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:16)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֤ב יְהוֹאָשׁ֙ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֔יו וַיִּקָּבֵר֙ בְּשֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן עִ֖ם מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ יָרׇבְעָ֥ם בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


And Yehuoash lay with his forefathers, and he was buried in Shomron with the kings of Yisroel; and Yorovom his son reigned in his place.


###### II Kings 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:17)


וַיְחִ֨י אֲמַצְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־יוֹאָשׁ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה אַחֲרֵ֣י מ֔וֹת יְהוֹאָ֥שׁ בֶּן־יְהוֹאָחָ֖ז מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל חֲמֵ֥שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה שָׁנָֽה׃


Amatzyohu, son of Yehoash, king of Yehudah lived, after the death of Yehoash son of Yehoachaz king of Yisroel fifteen years.


###### II Kings 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:18)


וְיֶ֖תֶר דִּבְרֵ֣י אֲמַצְיָ֑הוּ הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתֻבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


And the rest of Amatzyoh’s deeds are indeed written in the book of chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.


###### II Kings 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:19)


וַיִּקְשְׁר֨וּ עָלָ֥יו קֶ֛שֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וַיָּ֣נׇס לָכִ֑ישָׁה וַיִּשְׁלְח֤וּ אַחֲרָיו֙ לָכִ֔ישָׁה וַיְמִתֻ֖הוּ שָֽׁם׃


They conspired a revolt against him in Yerusholayim, and he fled to Lakish; and they sent after him to Lakish and assassinated him there.


###### II Kings 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:20)


וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ עַל־הַסּוּסִ֑ים וַיִּקָּבֵ֧ר בִּירוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם עִם־אֲבֹתָ֖יו בְּעִ֥יר דָּוִֽד׃


They carried him [back] on the horses, and he was buried in Yerusholayim with his forefathers, in the City of Dovid.

וַיִּקְח֞וּ כׇּל־עַ֤ם יְהוּדָה֙ אֶת־עֲזַרְיָ֔ה וְה֕וּא בֶּן־שֵׁ֥שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה שָׁנָ֑ה וַיַּמְלִ֣כוּ אֹת֔וֹ תַּ֖חַת אָבִ֥יו אֲמַצְיָֽהוּ׃


The entire people of Yehudah took Azaryoh, who was sixteen years old, and made him king in place of his father Amatzyohu.

He built up Eilas and restored it to Yehudah, after the king lay with his forefathers.

בִּשְׁנַת֙ חֲמֵשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לַאֲמַצְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־יוֹאָ֖שׁ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה מָ֠לַ֠ךְ יָרׇבְעָ֨ם בֶּן־יוֹאָ֤שׁ מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ בְּשֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן אַרְבָּעִ֥ים וְאַחַ֖ת שָׁנָֽה׃


In the fifteenth year of Amatzyohu, son of Yehoash, king of Yehudah, Yerovom, son of Yoash became king, he ruled Yisroel in Shomron forty-one years.


###### II Kings 14:24

He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy; he did not turn away from all the sins which Yerovom, son of Nevot, had caused Yisroel to commit.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:25)


ה֗וּא הֵשִׁיב֙ אֶת־גְּב֣וּל יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִלְּב֥וֹא חֲמָ֖ת עַד־יָ֣ם הָעֲרָבָ֑ה כִּדְבַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּ֗ר בְּיַד־עַבְדּ֞וֹ יוֹנָ֤ה בֶן־אֲמִתַּי֙ הַנָּבִ֔יא אֲשֶׁ֖ר מִגַּ֥ת הַחֵֽפֶר׃


He restored the boundary of Yisroel from Levo Chamos until the Aravah Sea, according to the word of Adonoy, God of Yisroel, which He spoke through His servant, Yonah son of Amitai, the prophet, who was from Gas Hachepher.


###### II Kings 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:26)


כִּי־רָאָ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־עֳנִ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מֹרֶ֣ה מְאֹ֑ד וְאֶ֤פֶס עָצוּר֙ וְאֶ֣פֶס עָז֔וּב וְאֵ֥ין עֹזֵ֖ר לְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For Adonoy saw the suffering of Yisroel intensifying increasingly, with no one surviving and no one remaining, and no one helping Yisroel.

וְלֹא־דִבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה לִמְחוֹת֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִתַּ֖חַת הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַיּ֣וֹשִׁיעֵ֔ם בְּיַ֖ד יָרׇבְעָ֥ם בֶּן־יוֹאָֽשׁ׃


But Adonoy did not say to eradicate Yisroel’s name from under the heavens, and He saved them through Yerovom, the son of Yoash.


###### II Kings 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/14:28)


וְיֶ֩תֶר֩ דִּבְרֵ֨י יָרׇבְעָ֜ם וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ וּגְבוּרָת֣וֹ אֲשֶׁר־נִלְחָ֔ם וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵשִׁ֜יב אֶת־דַּמֶּ֧שֶׂק וְאֶת־חֲמָ֛ת לִיהוּדָ֖ה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And the rest of Yerovom’s deeds and all that he did, and of might, how he fought, and how he restored Damesek and Chamos to Yehudah through Yisroel, are indeed written in the book of the chronicles of Yisroel’s kings.

And Yerovom lay with his forefathers, with the kings of Yisroel, and his son Zecharyoh reigned in his place.

###### II Kings 15:1

In the twenty-seventh year of Yorovom king of Yisroel, Azaryoh son of Amatzyoh the king of Yehudah, became king.

He did what was right in the eyes of Adonoy; like all that Amatzyoh his father had done.

However, the high places were not removed; the people were still offering sacrifices and burning incense on the high places.

וַיְנַגַּ֨ע יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶת־הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ וַיְהִ֤י מְצֹרָע֙ עַד־י֣וֹם מֹת֔וֹ וַיֵּ֖שֶׁב בְּבֵ֣ית הַחׇפְשִׁ֑ית וְיוֹתָ֤ם בֶּן־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ עַל־הַבַּ֔יִת שֹׁפֵ֖ט אֶת־עַ֥ם הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Adonoy brought a plague upon the king and he was stricken with *tzora’as* until the day of his death, and he lived in a retirement house. Yosum, the king’s son, who was [appointed] over the palace judging the people of the land.

He did what was evil the eyes of Adonoy as his forefathers had done; he did not turn away from the sins of Yorovom son of Nevot, who had caused Yisroel to sin.

וַיִּקְשֹׁ֤ר עָלָיו֙ שַׁלֻּ֣ם בֶּן־יָבֵ֔שׁ וַיַּכֵּ֥הוּ קׇֽבׇל־עָ֖ם וַיְמִיתֵ֑הוּ וַיִּמְלֹ֖ךְ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


Shalum son of Yoveish revolted against him, and struck him before the people and killed him; and he reigned in his place.

The rest of Zecharyoh’s deeds are written in the book of chronicles of Yisroel’s kings.

וַיַּ֩עַל֩ מְנַחֵ֨ם בֶּן־גָּדִ֜י מִתִּרְצָ֗ה וַיָּבֹא֙ שֹׁמְר֔וֹן וַיַּ֛ךְ אֶת־שַׁלּ֥וּם בֶּן־יָבֵ֖ישׁ בְּשֹׁמְר֑וֹן וַיְמִיתֵ֖הוּ וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃

בִּשְׁנַ֨ת שְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וָתֵ֙שַׁע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה לַעֲזַרְיָ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה מָ֠לַ֠ךְ מְנַחֵ֨ם בֶּן־גָּדִ֧י עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל עֶ֥שֶׂר שָׁנִ֖ים בְּשֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃


In the thirty-ninth year of Azaryoh king of Yehudah[’s reign], Menachem, son of Gadi became king over Yisroel ten years in Shomron.


###### II Kings 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/15:18)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹ֣א סָ֠ר מֵעַ֨ל חַטֹּ֜אות יָרׇבְעָ֧ם בֶּן־נְבָ֛ט אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱטִ֥יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כׇּל־יָמָֽיו׃


He did what evil in the eyes of Adonoy; he did not turn away from the sins of Yorovom son of Nevot, who had caused Yisroel to sin, all his days.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/15:19)


בָּ֣א פ֤וּל מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ וַיִּתֵּ֤ן מְנַחֵם֙ לְפ֔וּל אֶ֖לֶף כִּכַּר־כָּ֑סֶף לִהְי֤וֹת יָדָיו֙ אִתּ֔וֹ לְהַחֲזִ֥יק הַמַּמְלָכָ֖ה בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


Pul, the king of Ashur invaded the land, and Menachem gave Pul one thousand silver talents that he be his ally, to strengthen the kingdom in his hand.

וַיֹּצֵא֩ מְנַחֵ֨ם אֶת־הַכֶּ֜סֶף עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עַ֚ל כׇּל־גִּבּוֹרֵ֣י הַחַ֔יִל לָתֵת֙ לְמֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר חֲמִשִּׁ֧ים שְׁקָלִ֛ים כֶּ֖סֶף לְאִ֣ישׁ אֶחָ֑ד וַיָּ֙שׇׁב֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר וְלֹא־עָ֥מַד שָׁ֖ם בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


Menachem exacted the money from Yisroel from all the mighty warriors to give to the king of Ashur, fifty silver shekel per person. The king of Ashur returned [to his country] and did not remain there in the land.

In the days of Pekach king of Yisroel, Tiglas Pileser king of Ashur invaded and took Iyon, Aveil Beis Ma’acho, Yano’ach, Kedesh, Chatzor, Gilod, Goliloh, the entire land of Naftali, and he exiled them to Ashur.

Yosum lay with his forefathers, and he was buried with his forefathers, in the City of Dovid, his forefather. His son Achaz reigned in his place.



## Chapter 16

###### II Kings 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:1)


בִּשְׁנַת֙ שְׁבַֽע־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לְפֶ֖קַח בֶּן־רְמַלְיָ֑הוּ מָלַ֛ךְ אָחָ֥ז בֶּן־יוֹתָ֖ם מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


In the seventeenth year of Pekach son of Remalyohu, Achaz son of Yosum king of Yehudah, became king.


###### II Kings 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:2)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֤ים שָׁנָה֙ אָחָ֣ז בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְשֵׁשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְלֹא־עָשָׂ֣ה הַיָּשָׁ֗ר בְּעֵינֵ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהָ֖יו כְּדָוִ֥ד אָבִֽיו׃


Achaz was twenty years old when he became king and he reigned sixteen years in Yerusholayim; he did not do what is right in the eyes of Adonoy his God, like his forefather, Dovid.


###### II Kings 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:3)


וַיֵּ֕לֶךְ בְּדֶ֖רֶךְ מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְגַ֤ם אֶת־בְּנוֹ֙ הֶעֱבִ֣יר בָּאֵ֔שׁ כְּתֹֽעֲבוֹת֙ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר הוֹרִ֤ישׁ יְהֹוָה֙ אֹתָ֔ם מִפְּנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


He followed in the way of the kings of Yisroel, even his son he passed through the fire, like the abominations of the nations whom Adonoy had driven out before the Bnei Yisroel.


###### II Kings 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:4)


וַיְזַבֵּ֧חַ וַיְקַטֵּ֛ר בַּבָּמ֖וֹת וְעַל־הַגְּבָע֑וֹת וְתַ֖חַת כׇּל־עֵ֥ץ רַעֲנָֽן׃


He offered sacrifices and burned incense on the high places and on the hills, and under every leafy tree.


###### II Kings 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:5)


אָ֣ז יַעֲלֶ֣ה רְצִ֣ין מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲ֠רָ֠ם וּפֶ֨קַח בֶּן־רְמַלְיָ֧הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה וַיָּצֻ֙רוּ֙ עַל־אָחָ֔ז וְלֹ֥א יָכְל֖וּ לְהִלָּחֵֽם׃


Then Retzin king of Arom went up with Pekach son of Remalyohu king of Yisroel, to Yerusholayim to wage war; they besieged Achaz, but were unable to overcome him.


###### II Kings 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:6)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗יא הֵ֠שִׁ֠יב רְצִ֨ין מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲרָ֤ם אֶת־אֵילַת֙ לַֽאֲרָ֔ם וַיְנַשֵּׁ֥ל אֶת־הַיְּהוּדִ֖ים מֵֽאֵיל֑וֹת (וארמים) [וַֽאֲדֹמִים֙] בָּ֣אוּ אֵילַ֔ת וַיֵּ֣שְׁבוּ שָׁ֔ם עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


At that time Retzin king of Arom restored Eilas to Arom, and he drove out the *Yehudim* from Eilos, and the people of Edom then came to Eilas and settled there until this day.


###### II Kings 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:7)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֨ח אָחָ֜ז מַלְאָכִ֗ים אֶל־תִּ֠גְלַ֠ת פְּלֶ֤סֶר מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר עַבְדְּךָ֥ וּבִנְךָ֖ אָ֑נִי עֲלֵ֨ה וְהוֹשִׁעֵ֜נִי מִכַּ֣ף מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲרָ֗ם וּמִכַּף֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הַקּוֹמִ֖ים עָלָֽי׃


Achaz sent messengers to Tiglas Pileser, king of Ashur, saying, “I am your servant and your son; come up and save me from the grasp of Arom’s king and from the grasp of Yisroel’s king who have risen against me.”


###### II Kings 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:8)


וַיִּקַּ֨ח אָחָ֜ז אֶת־הַכֶּ֣סֶף וְאֶת־הַזָּהָ֗ב הַנִּמְצָא֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וּבְאֹֽצְר֖וֹת בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח לְמֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר שֹֽׁחַד׃


Achaz took the silver and gold that was found in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and in the treasuries of the king’s palace, and sent [them] as a bribe to Ashur’s king.

וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע אֵלָיו֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר וַיַּ֩עַל֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ אַשּׁ֤וּר אֶל־דַּמֶּ֙שֶׂק֙ וַֽיִּתְפְּשֶׂ֔הָ וַיַּגְלֶ֖הָ קִ֑ירָה וְאֶת־רְצִ֖ין הֵמִֽית׃


The king of Ashur listened and went to Damesek and seized it; he exiled [its people] to Kir, and he killed Retzin.


###### II Kings 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/16:10)


וַיֵּ֣לֶךְ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אָחָ֡ז לִ֠קְרַ֠את תִּגְלַ֨ת פִּלְאֶ֤סֶר מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ דּוּמֶּ֔שֶׂק וַיַּ֥רְא אֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּדַמָּ֑שֶׂק וַיִּשְׁלַח֩ הַמֶּ֨לֶךְ אָחָ֜ז אֶל־אוּרִיָּ֣ה הַכֹּהֵ֗ן אֶת־דְּמ֧וּת הַמִּזְבֵּ֛חַ וְאֶת־תַּבְנִית֖וֹ לְכׇֽל־מַעֲשֵֽׂהוּ׃


King Achaz went to greet Tiglas Pileser, king of Ashur in Damesek and he saw the altar that was in Damesek. King Achaz sent to Uriyoh the *kohein* a model of the altar and its design detailing to all its workmanship.

He burnt his burnt offering and his meal offering and he poured his libation; he sprinkled the blood of his peace offering upon the altar.

And the Copper Altar that was before Adonoy, —he brought [the new altar] closer to the entrance of the Sanctuary, [placing it] between the Altar and the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and he placed it on the northern side of the Altar.

(ויצוהו) [וַיְצַוֶּ֣ה] הַמֶּֽלֶךְ־אָ֠חָ֠ז אֶת־אוּרִיָּ֨ה הַכֹּהֵ֜ן לֵאמֹ֗ר עַ֣ל הַמִּזְבֵּ֣חַ הַגָּד֡וֹל הַקְטֵ֣ר אֶת־עֹֽלַת־הַבֹּ֩קֶר֩ וְאֶת־מִנְחַ֨ת הָעֶ֜רֶב וְֽאֶת־עֹלַ֧ת הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ וְאֶת־מִנְחָת֗וֹ וְ֠אֵ֠ת עֹלַ֞ת כׇּל־עַ֤ם הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ וּמִנְחָתָ֣ם וְנִסְכֵּיהֶ֔ם וְכׇל־דַּ֥ם עֹלָ֛ה וְכׇל־דַּם־זֶ֖בַח עָלָ֣יו תִּזְרֹ֑ק וּמִזְבַּ֧ח הַנְּחֹ֛שֶׁת יִֽהְיֶה־לִּ֖י לְבַקֵּֽר׃


King Achaz commanded Uriyoh the *kohein*, saying, “Upon the great altar burn the morning burnt offering and evening meal offering, and the king’s burnt offering and his meal offering, and the burnt offering of all the people of the land, and their meal offering and their libations, and the blood of every burnt offering and the blood of every offering you should sprinkle on it, and the Copper Altar will be for me to visit.”

King Achaz cut off the frames of the basins and he removed the basin from them, and he took down the Sea from the copper oxen that were under and put it on a stone floor.

The canopy for Shabbos that they built in the Beis Hamikdosh and the king’s outside entrance, he switched to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy because of the king of Ashur.

The rest of Achaz’s deeds that he did, behold, they are written in the book of chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.

## Chapter 17

###### II Kings 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:1)


בִּשְׁנַת֙ שְׁתֵּ֣ים עֶשְׂרֵ֔ה לְאָחָ֖ז מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה מָ֠לַ֠ךְ הוֹשֵׁ֨עַ בֶּן־אֵלָ֧ה בְשֹׁמְר֛וֹן עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תֵּ֥שַׁע שָׁנִֽים׃


In the twelfth year of Achaz king of Yehudah, Hoshei’a son of Eiloh became king over Yisroel in Shomron for nine years.


###### II Kings 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה רַ֗ק לֹ֚א כְּמַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָי֖וּ לְפָנָֽיו׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, but not like the kings of Yisroel [who ruled] before him.


###### II Kings 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:3)


עָלָ֣יו עָלָ֔ה שַׁלְמַנְאֶ֖סֶר מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וַֽיְהִי־ל֤וֹ הוֹשֵׁ֙עַ֙ עֶ֔בֶד וַיָּ֥שֶׁב ל֖וֹ מִנְחָֽה׃


Shalmaneser king of Ashur went up against him, and Hoshei’a became his vassal, and paid him tribute.


###### II Kings 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:4)


וַיִּמְצָא֩ מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֨וּר בְּהוֹשֵׁ֜עַ קֶ֗שֶׁר אֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁלַ֤ח מַלְאָכִים֙ אֶל־ס֣וֹא מֶֽלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֔יִם וְלֹא־הֶעֱלָ֥ה מִנְחָ֛ה לְמֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּׁ֖וּר כְּשָׁנָ֣ה בְשָׁנָ֑ה וַֽיַּעְצְרֵ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר וַיַּאַסְרֵ֖הוּ בֵּ֥ית כֶּֽלֶא׃


The king of Ashur discovered a conspiracy involving Hoshei’a, for he had sent messengers to So king of Egypt, and he did not pay tribute to the king of Ashur. Thus, the king of Ashur arrested him and confined him in prison.


###### II Kings 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:5)


וַיַּ֥עַל מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר בְּכׇל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַיַּ֙עַל֙ שֹׁמְר֔וֹן וַיָּ֥צַר עָלֶ֖יהָ שָׁלֹ֥שׁ שָׁנִֽים׃


The king of Ashur then invaded the entire land; he went up to Shomron and besieged it for three years.


###### II Kings 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:6)


בִּשְׁנַ֨ת הַתְּשִׁעִ֜ית לְהוֹשֵׁ֗עַ לָכַ֤ד מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ אֶת־שֹׁ֣מְר֔וֹן וַיֶּ֥גֶל אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אַשּׁ֑וּרָה וַיֹּ֨שֶׁב אוֹתָ֜ם בַּחְלַ֧ח וּבְחָב֛וֹר נְהַ֥ר גּוֹזָ֖ן וְעָרֵ֥י מָדָֽי׃{פ}


In the ninth year of Hoshei’a, the king of Ashur conquered Shomron and exiled Yisroel to Ashur; he settled them in Chelach, Chavor, [by] the Gozan River, and [in] the towns of Mudie.


###### II Kings 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:7)


וַיְהִ֗י כִּֽי־חָטְא֤וּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֔ם הַמַּעֲלֶ֤ה אֹתָם֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם מִתַּ֕חַת יַ֖ד פַּרְעֹ֣ה מֶֽלֶךְ־מִצְרָ֑יִם וַיִּֽירְא֖וּ אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִֽים׃


And it was, when the Bnei Yisroel sinned against Adonoy their God, Who brought them up from the land of Egypt, from under the hand of Paroh king of Egypt; and they feared gods of others.


###### II Kings 17:8

###### II Kings 17:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:31)


וְהָעַוִּ֛ים עָשׂ֥וּ נִבְחַ֖ז וְאֶת־תַּרְתָּ֑ק וְהַסְפַרְוִ֗ים שֹׂרְפִ֤ים אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶם֙ בָּאֵ֔שׁ לְאַדְרַמֶּ֥לֶךְ וַֽעֲנַמֶּ֖לֶךְ (אלה) [אֱלֹהֵ֥י] (ספרים) [סְפַרְוָֽיִם]׃


The Avim made Nivchaz and Tartok, and the Sepharvim burned their children in fire to Adramelech and Anamelech, the gods of Sepharvayim.


###### II Kings 17:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:32)


וַיִּהְי֥וּ יְרֵאִ֖ים אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיַּעֲשׂ֨וּ לָהֶ֤ם מִקְצוֹתָם֙ כֹּהֲנֵ֣י בָמ֔וֹת וַיִּהְי֛וּ עֹשִׂ֥ים לָהֶ֖ם בְּבֵ֥ית הַבָּמֽוֹת׃


They feared Adonoy, but they made some of them priests for the high places, and they practiced their [rites] in the high place temples.


###### II Kings 17:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:33)


אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה הָי֣וּ יְרֵאִ֑ים וְאֶת־אֱלֹֽהֵיהֶם֙ הָי֣וּ עֹֽבְדִ֔ים כְּמִשְׁפַּט֙ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הִגְל֥וּ אֹתָ֖ם מִשָּֽׁם׃


They feared Adonoy, but [also] worshiped their gods, as was the custom of the nations from which they had been exiled.


###### II Kings 17:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:34)


עַ֣ד הַיּ֤וֹם הַזֶּה֙ הֵ֣ם עֹשִׂ֔ים כַּמִּשְׁפָּטִ֖ים הָרִֽאשֹׁנִ֑ים אֵינָ֤ם יְרֵאִים֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאֵינָ֣ם עֹשִׂ֗ים כְּחֻקֹּתָם֙ וּכְמִשְׁפָּטָ֔ם וְכַתּוֹרָ֣ה וְכַמִּצְוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יַעֲקֹ֔ב אֲשֶׁר־שָׂ֥ם שְׁמ֖וֹ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


To this day, they act according to their earlier practices; they do not fear Adonoy, and they do not act according to their statutes, laws, or according to the Torah and the commandment[s] that Adonoy commanded the Bnei Yaakov, whose name He changed to Yisroel.


###### II Kings 17:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:35)


וַיִּכְרֹ֨ת יְהֹוָ֤ה אִתָּם֙ בְּרִ֔ית וַיְצַוֵּ֣ם לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֥א תִֽירְא֖וּ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים וְלֹא־תִשְׁתַּחֲו֣וּ לָהֶ֔ם וְלֹ֣א תַעַבְד֔וּם וְלֹ֥א תִזְבְּח֖וּ לָהֶֽם׃


And Adonoy enacted a covenant with them, and commanded them, saying, “Do not fear gods of others, do not prostrate yourselves to them, you do not worship them, and do not slaughter sacrifices to them.


###### II Kings 17:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:36)


כִּ֣י אִֽם־אֶת־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁר֩ הֶעֱלָ֨ה אֶתְכֶ֜ם מֵאֶ֧רֶץ מִצְרַ֛יִם בְּכֹ֧חַ גָּד֛וֹל וּבִזְר֥וֹעַ נְטוּיָ֖ה אֹת֣וֹ תִירָ֑אוּ וְל֥וֹ תִֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ וְל֥וֹ תִזְבָּֽחוּ׃


Only Adonoy Who brought you up from the land of Egypt with great might and with an outstretched arm, Him shall you fear, and to Him shall you prostrate yourselves and to Him shall you slaughter sacrifices.


###### II Kings 17:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:37)


וְאֶת־הַחֻקִּ֨ים וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֜ים וְהַתּוֹרָ֤ה וְהַמִּצְוָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר כָּתַ֣ב לָכֶ֔ם תִּשְׁמְר֥וּן לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת כׇּל־הַיָּמִ֑ים וְלֹ֥א תִֽירְא֖וּ אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִֽים׃


And the statutes, laws, the Torah and the commandment[s] that He wrote for you, you shall observe to perform all times, and you shall not fear gods of others.


###### II Kings 17:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:38)


וְהַבְּרִ֛ית אֲשֶׁר־כָּרַ֥תִּי אִתְּכֶ֖ם לֹ֣א תִשְׁכָּ֑חוּ וְלֹ֥א תִֽירְא֖וּ אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִֽים׃


And the covenant that I enacted with you, you shall not forget, and you must not fear gods of others.


###### II Kings 17:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:39)


כִּ֛י אִֽם־אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם תִּירָ֑אוּ וְהוּא֙ יַצִּ֣יל אֶתְכֶ֔ם מִיַּ֖ד כׇּל־אֹיְבֵיכֶֽם׃


But, only Adonoy your God you shall fear, and He will save you from the hand of all your enemies.”


###### II Kings 17:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:40)


וְלֹ֖א שָׁמֵ֑עוּ כִּ֛י אִֽם־כְּמִשְׁפָּטָ֥ם הָֽרִאשׁ֖וֹן הֵ֥ם עֹשִֽׂים׃


[But] they did not listen, rather, they observed their original practices.


###### II Kings 17:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/17:41)


וַיִּהְי֣וּ**׀** הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָאֵ֗לֶּה יְרֵאִים֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאֶת־פְּסִֽילֵיהֶ֖ם הָי֣וּ עֹֽבְדִ֑ים גַּם־בְּנֵיהֶ֣ם**׀** וּבְנֵ֣י בְנֵיהֶ֗ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֤וּ אֲבֹתָם֙ הֵ֣ם עֹשִׂ֔ים עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


These nations feared Adonoy, but worshiped their graven images; also their children and grandchildren, as their forefathers had done, so they do to this day.



## Chapter 18

###### II Kings 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:1)


וַֽיְהִי֙ בִּשְׁנַ֣ת שָׁלֹ֔שׁ לְהוֹשֵׁ֥עַ בֶּן־אֵלָ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל מָלַ֛ךְ חִזְקִיָּ֥ה בֶן־אָחָ֖ז מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


In the third year of Hoshei’a son of Eiloh king of Yisroel, that Chizkiyoh son of Achaz king of Yehudah, became king.


###### II Kings 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:2)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ הָיָ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְעֶשְׂרִ֤ים וָתֵ֙שַׁע֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ אֲבִ֖י בַּת־זְכַרְיָֽה׃


He was twenty-five years old when he became king, and he reigned twenty-nine years in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Avi daughter of Zecharyoh.


###### II Kings 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:3)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה דָּוִ֥ד אָבִֽיו׃


He did what was right in the eyes of Adonoy, like all that his forefather Dovid had done.


###### II Kings 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:4)


ה֣וּא**׀** הֵסִ֣יר אֶת־הַבָּמ֗וֹת וְשִׁבַּר֙ אֶת־הַמַּצֵּבֹ֔ת וְכָרַ֖ת אֶת־הָֽאֲשֵׁרָ֑ה וְכִתַּת֩ נְחַ֨שׁ הַנְּחֹ֜שֶׁת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֣ה מֹשֶׁ֗ה כִּ֣י עַד־הַיָּמִ֤ים הָהֵ֙מָּה֙ הָי֤וּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מְקַטְּרִ֣ים ל֔וֹ וַיִּקְרָא־ל֖וֹ נְחֻשְׁתָּֽן׃


He removed the high places, smashed the monuments, and cut down the *asheiroh*; he crushed the copper serpent that Moshe had made, because until those days, the Bnei Yisroel were burning incense to it, and he called it *Nechushton*.


###### II Kings 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:5)


בַּיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בָּטָ֑ח וְאַחֲרָ֞יו לֹא־הָיָ֣ה כָמֹ֗הוּ בְּכֹל֙ מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר הָי֖וּ לְפָנָֽיו׃


He trusted in Adonoy the God of Yisroel; after him there was none like him among all Yehudah’s kings, nor were there [among those] before him.


###### II Kings 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:6)


וַיִּדְבַּק֙ בַּֽיהֹוָ֔ה לֹא־סָ֖ר מֵאַֽחֲרָ֑יו וַיִּשְׁמֹר֙ מִצְוֺתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מֹשֶֽׁה׃


He clung to Adonoy, and did not turn away from [following] Him; he kept His commandments, which Adonoy had commanded Moshe.


###### II Kings 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:7)


וְהָיָ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ עִמּ֔וֹ בְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־יֵצֵ֖א יַשְׂכִּ֑יל וַיִּמְרֹ֥ד בְּמֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר וְלֹ֥א עֲבָדֽוֹ׃


Adonoy was with him; he succeeded in all his endeavors. He rebelled against the king of Ashur and did not serve him.


###### II Kings 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:8)


הֽוּא־הִכָּ֧ה אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֛ים עַד־עַזָּ֖ה וְאֶת־גְּבוּלֶ֑יהָ מִמִּגְדַּ֥ל נוֹצְרִ֖ים עַד־עִ֥יר מִבְצָֽר׃{פ}


He attacked the Pelishtim up to Azoh and its borders, from watchtower to fortified city.


###### II Kings 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:9)


וַיְהִ֞י בַּשָּׁנָ֤ה הָֽרְבִיעִית֙ לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ הִ֚יא הַשָּׁנָ֣ה הַשְּׁבִיעִ֔ית לְהוֹשֵׁ֥עַ בֶּן־אֵלָ֖ה מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל עָלָ֞ה שַׁלְמַנְאֶ֧סֶר מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֛וּר עַל־שֹׁמְר֖וֹן וַיָּ֥צַר עָלֶֽיהָ׃


And it was in the fourth year of king Chizkiyohu, which was the seventh year of Hoshei’a son of Eiloh king of Yisroel, that Shalmaneser king of Ashur went up to Shomron and besieged it.


###### II Kings 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:10)


וַֽיִּלְכְּדֻ֗הָ מִקְצֵה֙ שָׁלֹ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֔ים בִּשְׁנַת־שֵׁ֖שׁ לְחִזְקִיָּ֑ה הִ֣יא שְׁנַת־תֵּ֗שַׁע לְהוֹשֵׁ֙עַ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל נִלְכְּדָ֖ה שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃


They conquered it at the end of three years; in Chizkiyoh’s sixth year, which is the ninth year of Hoshei’a king of Yisroel, Shomron was conquered.


###### II Kings 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:11)


וַיֶּ֧גֶל מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֛וּר אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל אַשּׁ֑וּרָה וַיַּנְחֵ֞ם בַּחְלַ֧ח וּבְחָב֛וֹר נְהַ֥ר גּוֹזָ֖ן וְעָרֵ֥י מָדָֽי׃


The king of Ashur exiled Yisroel to Ashur, and he settled them in Chelach, Chavor, [by] the Gozan River, and in the towns of Mudie.


###### II Kings 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:12)


עַ֣ל**׀** אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־שָׁמְע֗וּ בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֔ם וַיַּעַבְרוּ֙ אֶת־בְּרִית֔וֹ אֵ֚ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוָּ֔ה מֹשֶׁ֖ה עֶ֣בֶד יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹ֥א שָׁמְע֖וּ וְלֹ֥א עָשֽׂוּ׃{פ}


[This happened] because they did not obey Adonoy their God, and they transgressed His covenant, everything that Moshe, the servant of Adonoy had commanded; and they neither listened nor obeyed [His will].


###### II Kings 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:13)


וּבְאַרְבַּע֩ עֶשְׂרֵ֨ה שָׁנָ֜ה לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֗ה עָלָ֞ה סַנְחֵרִ֤יב מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ עַ֣ל כׇּל־עָרֵ֧י יְהוּדָ֛ה הַבְּצֻר֖וֹת וַֽיִּתְפְּשֵֽׂם׃


In the fourteenth year of king Chizkiyohu’s [rule], Sancheiriv king of Ashur attacked all the fortified cities of Yehudah and seized them.


###### II Kings 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:14)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח חִזְקִיָּ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֣ה אֶל־מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֩׀לָכִ֨ישָׁה׀לֵאמֹ֤ר׀חָטָ֙אתִי֙ שׁ֣וּב מֵֽעָלַ֔י אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־תִּתֵּ֥ן עָלַ֖י אֶשָּׂ֑א וַיָּ֨שֶׂם מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֜וּר עַל־חִזְקִיָּ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֗ה שְׁלֹ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ כִּכַּר־כֶּ֔סֶף וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֖ים כִּכַּ֥ר זָהָֽב׃


Chizkiyoh king of Yehudah sent [a message] to the king of Ashur to Lakish, saying, “I have sinned, retreat from me; I will bear whatever you impose upon me.” And the king of Ashur imposed upon Chizkiyoh king of Yehudah, [a levy of] three hundred talents of silver and thirty talents of gold.


###### II Kings 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/18:15)

Chizkiyoh gave all the silver that was found in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and in the treasuries of the king’s palace.

The king of Ashur sent Tarton, Rav Soris, and Ravshokei from Lakish to king Chizkiyohu with a great army to Yerusholayim; and they went up and came and stood near the channel of the upper pool, which is on the road of the washer’s field.

## Chapter 19

###### II Kings 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:1)


וַיְהִ֗י כִּשְׁמֹ֙עַ֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ וַיִּקְרַ֖ע אֶת־בְּגָדָ֑יו וַיִּתְכַּ֣ס בַּשָּׂ֔ק וַיָּבֹ֖א בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And it was when king Chizkiyohu heard [this], that he rent his garments and covered himself with sackcloth, and he came to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:2)


וַ֠יִּשְׁלַ֠ח אֶת־אֶלְיָקִ֨ים אֲשֶׁר־עַל־הַבַּ֜יִת וְשֶׁבְנָ֣א הַסֹּפֵ֗ר וְאֵת֙ זִקְנֵ֣י הַכֹּהֲנִ֔ים מִתְכַּסִּ֖ים בַּשַּׂקִּ֑ים אֶל־יְשַׁעְיָ֥הוּ הַנָּבִ֖יא בֶּן־אָמֽוֹץ׃


He sent Elyokim who was in charge of the palace, and Shevno the scribe, and the elders of the *kohanim*, covered with sackcloth, to Yeshayohu the prophet, the son of Amotz.


###### II Kings 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:3)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֗יו כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ יוֹם־צָרָ֧ה וְתוֹכֵחָ֛ה וּנְאָצָ֖ה הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה כִּ֣י בָ֤אוּ בָנִים֙ עַד־מַשְׁבֵּ֔ר וְכֹ֥חַ אַ֖יִן לְלֵדָֽה׃


They said to him, “So has Chizkiyohu said, ‘This is a day of distress, debate, and blasphemy; for the children have come as far as the birth stool but there is no strength to give birth.


###### II Kings 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:4)


אוּלַ֡י יִשְׁמַע֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ אֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֣י רַבְשָׁקֵ֗ה אֲשֶׁר֩ שְׁלָח֨וֹ מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֤וּר׀אֲדֹנָיו֙ לְחָרֵף֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים חַ֔י וְהוֹכִ֙יחַ֙ בַּדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁמַ֖ע יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְנָשָׂ֣אתָ תְפִלָּ֔ה בְּעַ֥ד הַשְּׁאֵרִ֖ית הַנִּמְצָאָֽה׃


Perhaps Adonoy your God will hear all the words of Ravshokei, whom the king of Ashur, his master, sent to blaspheme the living God, and he brought proof with the words that Adonoy your God heard; and may you offer a prayer for the remnant [of Yisroel] that is still here.’”


###### II Kings 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:5)


וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ עַבְדֵ֛י הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ אֶֽל־יְשַׁעְיָֽהוּ׃


King Chizkiyohu’s servants came to Yeshayohu.


###### II Kings 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:6)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לָהֶם֙ יְשַֽׁעְיָ֔הוּ כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְר֖וּן אֶל־אֲדֹנֵיכֶ֑ם כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אַל־תִּירָא֙ מִפְּנֵ֤י הַדְּבָרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁמַ֔עְתָּ אֲשֶׁ֧ר גִּדְּפ֛וּ נַעֲרֵ֥י מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר אֹתִֽי׃


Yeshayohu said to them, “So shall you say to your master, ‘Thus has Adonoy said, “Do not be frightened by the words that you have heard, that the servants of Ashur’s king blasphemed Me.


###### II Kings 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:7)


הִנְנִ֨י נֹתֵ֥ן בּוֹ֙ ר֔וּחַ וְשָׁמַ֥ע שְׁמוּעָ֖ה וְשָׁ֣ב לְאַרְצ֑וֹ וְהִפַּלְתִּ֥יו בַּחֶ֖רֶב בְּאַרְצֽוֹ׃


Behold, I will imbue him with a desire, and he will hear a rumor and return to his land, and I will strike him down by the sword in his land.”’”


###### II Kings 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:8)


וַיָּ֙שׇׁב֙ רַבְשָׁקֵ֔ה וַיִּמְצָא֙ אֶת־מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר נִלְחָ֖ם עַל־לִבְנָ֑ה כִּ֣י שָׁמַ֔ע כִּ֥י נָסַ֖ע מִלָּכִֽישׁ׃


Ravshokei returned and found Ashur’s king waging war against Livnoh, because he heard that he had left Lakish.


###### II Kings 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:9)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֗ע אֶל־תִּרְהָ֤קָה מֶֽלֶךְ־כּוּשׁ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הִנֵּ֥ה יָצָ֖א לְהִלָּחֵ֣ם אִתָּ֑ךְ וַיָּ֙שׇׁב֙ וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח מַלְאָכִ֔ים אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ לֵאמֹֽר׃


He heard about Tirhokoh king of Cush, saying, “He has gone out to wage war against you.” He again sent messengers to Chizkiyohu, saying,


###### II Kings 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:10)


כֹּ֣ה תֹאמְר֗וּן אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֤הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר אַל־יַשִּׁאֲךָ֣ אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתָּ֛ה בֹּטֵ֥חַ בּ֖וֹ לֵאמֹ֑ר לֹ֤א תִנָּתֵן֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בְּיַ֖ד מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


“So shall you say to Chizkiyohu king of Yehudah, saying, ‘Do not let your God in Whom you trust, delude you, saying, “Yerusholayim will not be given into the king of Ashur’s hand.


###### II Kings 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:11)


הִנֵּ֣ה**׀** אַתָּ֣ה שָׁמַ֗עְתָּ אֵת֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֜וּ מַלְכֵ֥י אַשּׁ֛וּר לְכׇל־הָאֲרָצ֖וֹת לְהַחֲרִימָ֑ם וְאַתָּ֖ה תִּנָּצֵֽל׃


Behold, you have heard what the kings of Ashur have done to all the lands, to destroy them. [Now] will you be saved?


###### II Kings 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:12)


הַהִצִּ֨ילוּ אֹתָ֜ם אֱלֹהֵ֤י הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שִׁחֲת֣וּ אֲבוֹתַ֔י אֶת־גּוֹזָ֖ן וְאֶת־חָרָ֑ן וְרֶ֥צֶף וּבְנֵי־עֶ֖דֶן אֲשֶׁ֥ר בִּתְלַאשָּֽׂר׃


Did they save them— the gods of the nations whom my forefathers destroyed, Gozan, Choron, Retzeph and the children of Eden which is in Selassor?


###### II Kings 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:13)


אַיּ֤וֹ מֶֽלֶךְ־חֲמָת֙ וּמֶ֣לֶךְ אַרְפָּ֔ד וּמֶ֖לֶךְ לָעִ֣יר סְפַרְוָ֑יִם הֵנַ֖ע וְעִוָּֽה׃


Where are the king of Chamos, the king of Arpod, and the king of the city of Sepharvayim? He exiled and twisted them.”’”


###### II Kings 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:14)


וַיִּקַּ֨ח חִזְקִיָּ֧הוּ אֶת־הַסְּפָרִ֛ים מִיַּ֥ד הַמַּלְאָכִ֖ים וַיִּקְרָאֵ֑ם וַיַּ֙עַל֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיִּפְרְשֵׂ֥הוּ חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


Chizkiyohu took the letters from the messengers and read them; he went up to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and Chizkiyohu spread them out before Adonoy.


###### II Kings 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:15)


וַיִּתְפַּלֵּ֨ל חִזְקִיָּ֜הוּ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָה֮ וַיֹּאמַר֒ יְהֹוָ֞ה אֱלֹהֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יֹשֵׁ֣ב הַכְּרֻבִ֔ים אַתָּה־ה֤וּא הָאֱלֹהִים֙ לְבַדְּךָ֔ לְכֹ֖ל מַמְלְכ֣וֹת הָאָ֑רֶץ אַתָּ֣ה עָשִׂ֔יתָ אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וְאֶת־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Chizkiyohu prayed before Adonoy and said, “Adonoy, God of Yisroel, Who dwells [between] the *Cheruvim,* You alone are the God of all the kingdoms of the earth. You created heaven and earth.


###### II Kings 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:16)


הַטֵּ֨ה יְהֹוָ֤ה׀אׇזְנְךָ֙ וּֽשְׁמָ֔ע פְּקַ֧ח יְהֹוָ֛ה עֵינֶ֖יךָ וּרְאֵ֑ה וּשְׁמַ֗ע אֵ֚ת דִּבְרֵ֣י סַנְחֵרִ֔יב אֲשֶׁ֣ר שְׁלָח֔וֹ לְחָרֵ֖ף אֱלֹהִ֥ים חָֽי׃


Adonoy, incline Your ear and listen, Adonoy, open Your eyes and see; hear the words of Sancheiriv, who sent him to blaspheme the living God.


###### II Kings 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:17)


אׇמְנָ֖ם יְהֹוָ֑ה הֶחֱרִ֜יבוּ מַלְכֵ֥י אַשּׁ֛וּר אֶת־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם וְאֶת־אַרְצָֽם׃


Indeed, Adonoy, the kings of Ashur have destroyed the nations and their land.


###### II Kings 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:18)


וְנָתְנ֥וּ אֶת־אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם בָּאֵ֑שׁ כִּי֩ לֹ֨א אֱלֹהִ֜ים הֵ֗מָּה כִּ֣י אִם־מַעֲשֵׂ֧ה יְדֵי־אָדָ֛ם עֵ֥ץ וָאֶ֖בֶן וַֽיְאַבְּדֽוּם׃


They have placed their gods into the fire, for they are not gods but the work of man’s hands wood and stone, and they destroyed them.


###### II Kings 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:19)


וְעַתָּה֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ הוֹשִׁיעֵ֥נוּ נָ֖א מִיָּד֑וֹ וְיֵֽדְעוּ֙ כׇּל־מַמְלְכ֣וֹת הָאָ֔רֶץ כִּ֥י אַתָּ֛ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהִ֖ים לְבַדֶּֽךָ׃{ס}


And now, Adonoy our God, please save us from his hand, so that all the kingdoms of the world will know that You alone are Adonoy, God.”


###### II Kings 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:20)


וַיִּשְׁלַח֙ יְשַׁעְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־אָמ֔וֹץ אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ לֵאמֹ֑ר כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר הִתְפַּלַּ֧לְתָּ אֵלַ֛י אֶל־סַנְחֵרִ֥ב מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר שָׁמָֽעְתִּי׃


Yeshayohu son of Amotz sent to Chizkiyohu, saying, “So has Adonoy, God of Yisroel said, ‘Regarding what you prayed to me concerning Sancheiriv, king of Ashur, I have heard.’


###### II Kings 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:21)


זֶ֣ה הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה עָלָ֑יו בָּזָ֨ה לְךָ֜ לָעֲגָ֣ה לְךָ֗ בְּתוּלַת֙ בַּת־צִיּ֔וֹן אַחֲרֶ֙יךָ֙ רֹ֣אשׁ הֵנִ֔יעָה בַּ֖ת יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


This is the word that Adonoy has spoken about him, ‘She has despised you and has mocked you the virgin daughter of Tzion. She has shaken her head at you the daughter of Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:22)


אֶת־מִ֤י חֵרַ֙פְתָּ֙ וְגִדַּ֔פְתָּ וְעַל־מִ֖י הֲרִימ֣וֹתָ קּ֑וֹל וַתִּשָּׂ֥א מָר֛וֹם עֵינֶ֖יךָ עַל־קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Whom have you insulted and blasphemed, and against Whom have you raised [your] voice? And you have lifted your eyes on high against the Holy One of Yisroel.


###### II Kings 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:23)


בְּיַ֣ד מַלְאָכֶ֘יךָ֮ חֵרַ֣פְתָּ׀אֲדֹנָי֒ וַתֹּ֗אמֶר (ברכב) [בְּרֹ֥ב] רִכְבִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י עָלִ֛יתִי מְר֥וֹם הָרִ֖ים יַרְכְּתֵ֣י לְבָנ֑וֹן וְאֶכְרֹ֞ת קוֹמַ֤ת אֲרָזָיו֙ מִבְח֣וֹר בְּרֹשָׁ֔יו וְאָב֙וֹאָה֙ מְל֣וֹן קִצֹּ֔ה יַ֖עַר כַּרְמִלּֽוֹ׃


Through your messengers you have insulted the LORD, and said, “With my multitude of chariots I have ascended the highest mountains, to the ends of the Levanon, and I will cut down its tallest cedar [trees], its choice cypress [trees], and I will come to its remotest lodge, to its fruitful forest.


###### II Kings 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:24)


אֲנִ֣י קַ֔רְתִּי וְשָׁתִ֖יתִי מַ֣יִם זָרִ֑ים וְאַחְרִב֙ בְּכַף־פְּעָמַ֔י כֹּ֖ל יְאֹרֵ֥י מָצֽוֹר׃


I dug and drank strange water, and I dry up with the soles of my feet all rivers of the siege.”


###### II Kings 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/19:25)


הֲלֹֽא־שָׁמַ֤עְתָּ לְמֵֽרָחוֹק֙ אֹתָ֣הּ עָשִׂ֔יתִי לְמִ֥ימֵי קֶ֖דֶם וִיצַרְתִּ֑יהָ עַתָּ֣ה הֲבֵיאתִ֔יהָ וּתְהִ֗י לַהְשׁ֛וֹת גַּלִּ֥ים נִצִּ֖ים עָרִ֥ים בְּצֻרֽוֹת׃


Have you not heard from afar what I did in days of yore, and what I formed? Now I have brought it about and it shall be to make desolate, blossoming hills, and fortified cities.


###### II Kings 19:26

וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ יְחִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ נָקֵ֣ל לַצֵּ֔ל לִנְט֖וֹת עֶ֣שֶׂר מַעֲל֑וֹת לֹ֣א כִ֔י יָשׁ֥וּב הַצֵּ֛ל אֲחֹרַנִּ֖ית עֶ֥שֶׂר מַעֲלֽוֹת׃

וַיִּשְׁמַ֣ע עֲלֵיהֶם֮ חִזְקִיָּ֒הוּ֒ וַיַּרְאֵ֣ם אֶת־כׇּל־בֵּ֣ית נְכֹתֹ֡ה אֶת־הַכֶּ֩סֶף֩ וְאֶת־הַזָּהָ֨ב וְאֶת־הַבְּשָׂמִ֜ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שֶׁ֣מֶן הַטּ֗וֹב וְאֵת֙ בֵּ֣ית כֵּלָ֔יו וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִמְצָ֖א בְּאוֹצְרֹתָ֑יו לֹא־הָיָ֣ה דָבָ֗ר אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹֽא־הֶרְאָ֧ם חִזְקִיָּ֛הוּ בְּבֵית֖וֹ וּבְכׇל־מֶמְשַׁלְתּֽוֹ׃


Chizkiyohu listened to them, and he showed them his entire treasure house, the silver, the gold, the spices, the fine oil, and the house in which he kept his vessels, and everything that was found in his treasuries; there was nothing that Chizkiyohu did not show them in his palace and in his entire kingdom.


###### II Kings 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:14)


וַיָּבֹא֙ יְשַׁעְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֔יא אֶל־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֜יו מָ֥ה אָמְר֣וּ**׀** הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה וּמֵאַ֙יִן֙ יָבֹ֣אוּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ מֵאֶ֧רֶץ רְחוֹקָ֛ה בָּ֖אוּ מִבָּבֶֽל׃


Yeshayohu the prophet came to king Chizkiyohu and said to him, “What did these men say, and from where did they come to you?” And Chizkiyohu said, “They came from a distant land, from Bavel.”


###### II Kings 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:15)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר מָ֥ה רָא֖וּ בְּבֵיתֶ֑ךָ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר בְּבֵיתִי֙ רָא֔וּ לֹא־הָיָ֥ה דָבָ֛ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־הִרְאִיתִ֖ם בְּאֹצְרֹתָֽי׃


He said, “What did they see in your palace?” And Chizkiyohu said, “They saw everything that is in my palace; there was nothing that I did not show them in my treasuries.”

Yeshayohu said to Chizkiyohu, “Hear the word of Adonoy.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/20:17)


הִנֵּה֮ יָמִ֣ים בָּאִים֒ וְנִשָּׂ֣א**׀** כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּבֵיתֶ֗ךָ וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר אָצְר֧וּ אֲבֹתֶ֛יךָ עַד־הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה בָּבֶ֑לָה לֹא־יִוָּתֵ֥ר דָּבָ֖ר אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Behold, days are coming when everything will be carried off— that is in your palace and that which your forefathers have accumulated until this day to Bavel; nothing will remain,” said Adonoy.

וּמִבָּנֶ֜יךָ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יֵצְא֧וּ מִמְּךָ֛ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תּוֹלִ֖יד (יקח) [יִקָּ֑חוּ] וְהָיוּ֙ סָרִיסִ֔ים בְּהֵיכַ֖ל מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


And some of your sons, who will issue from you, whom you will beget, they will take, and they will be officers in the king of Bavel’s palace.”

Chizkiyohu said to Yeshayohu, “The word of Adonoy that you have spoken is good,” for he said [to himself], “It is not so, that there will be peace and truth in my days?”

The rest of Chizkiyohu’s deeds and all his might, and how he made the pool and the conduit, and brought water into the city, they indeed are written in the book of the chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.

Chizkiyohu lay with his forefathers, and Menashe his son reigned in his place.



## Chapter 21

###### II Kings 21:1

בֶּן־שְׁתֵּ֨ים עֶשְׂרֵ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ מְנַשֶּׁ֣ה בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וַחֲמִשִּׁ֤ים וְחָמֵשׁ֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֥ם אִמּ֖וֹ חֶפְצִי־בָֽהּ׃


At the age of twelve years Menashe became king, and he reigned in Yerusholayim fifty-five years; his mother’s name was Chephtzi Boh.

He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy; like the abominations of the nations that Adonoy had driven out from before the Bnei Yisroel.

וַיָּ֗שׇׁב וַיִּ֙בֶן֙ אֶת־הַבָּמ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִבַּ֖ד חִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ אָבִ֑יו וַיָּ֨קֶם מִזְבְּחֹ֜ת לַבַּ֗עַל וַיַּ֤עַשׂ אֲשֵׁרָה֙ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֗ה אַחְאָב֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַיִּשְׁתַּ֙חוּ֙ לְכׇל־צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֖ד אֹתָֽם׃


He rebuilt the high places that Chizkiyohu, his father, had destroyed, and he erected altars to the Baal; he made an *asheiroh* as Achov king of Yisroel had made, and he prostrated himself to the entire host of the heavens and worshiped them.

וְהֶעֱבִ֤יר אֶת־בְּנוֹ֙ בָּאֵ֔שׁ וְעוֹנֵ֣ן וְנִחֵ֔שׁ וְעָ֥שָׂה א֖וֹב וְיִדְּעֹנִ֑ים הִרְבָּ֗ה לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהַכְעִֽיס׃


He passed his own son through fire; he practiced soothsaying and divination, and he consulted necromancers and those who divine by the Yidoa bone; he did much that was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, to provoke [Him].

And I will not continue to cause Yisroel’s feet to wander from the land that I have given to their forefathers; only if they will observe to act according to all that I have commanded them, and according to the entire Torah that Moshe, My servant, commanded them.”

Adonoy spoke through His servants, the prophets, saying.

יַ֩עַן֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה מְנַשֶּׁ֤ה מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֙ הַתֹּעֵב֣וֹת הָאֵ֔לֶּה הֵרַ֕ע מִכֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֥וּ הָאֱמֹרִ֖י אֲשֶׁ֣ר לְפָנָ֑יו וַיַּחֲטִ֥א גַֽם־אֶת־יְהוּדָ֖ה בְּגִלּוּלָֽיו׃{ס}


“Because Menashe king of Yehudah has committed these abominations, he has acted more wickedly than all the Amorite[s] who were before him, and he caused Yehudah also to sin with his idols.

לָכֵ֗ן כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הִנְנִי֙ מֵבִ֣יא רָעָ֔ה עַל־יְרֽוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וִֽיהוּדָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר֙ כׇּל־[שֹׁ֣מְעָ֔הּ] (שמעיו) תִּצַּ֖לְנָה שְׁתֵּ֥י אׇזְנָֽיו׃


Therefore, so has Adonoy God of Yisroel said, ‘Behold I am bringing calamity upon Yerusholayim and Yehudah, concerning which the two ears of all those who hear it will ring.

וְנָטִ֣יתִי עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם אֵ֚ת קָ֣ו שֹׁמְר֔וֹן וְאֶת־מִשְׁקֹ֖לֶת בֵּ֣ית אַחְאָ֑ב וּמָחִ֨יתִי אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם כַּאֲשֶׁר־יִמְחֶ֤ה אֶת־הַצַּלַּ֙חַת֙ מָחָ֔ה וְהָפַ֖ךְ עַל־פָּנֶֽיהָ׃

I will forsake the remnant of My heritage, and I will deliver them into the hands of their enemies, and they will become plunder and prey for all their enemies.

###### II Kings 21:17

וְיֶ֨תֶר דִּבְרֵ֤י מְנַשֶּׁה֙ וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה וְחַטָּאת֖וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֑א הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


The rest of Menashe’s deeds and all that he did, and his sin[s] that he committed, are written in the book of the chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.


###### II Kings 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:18)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֤ב מְנַשֶּׁה֙ עִם־אֲבֹתָ֔יו וַיִּקָּבֵ֥ר בְּגַן־בֵּית֖וֹ בְּגַן־עֻזָּ֑א וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אָמ֥וֹן בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{פ}


Menashe lay with his forefathers and was buried in the garden of his palace, in the garden of Uzza; his son Amon reigned in his place.


###### II Kings 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:19)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וּשְׁתַּ֤יִם שָׁנָה֙ אָמ֣וֹן בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁתַּ֣יִם שָׁנִ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ מְשֻׁלֶּ֥מֶת בַּת־חָר֖וּץ מִן־יׇטְבָֽה׃


Amon was twenty-two years old when he became king, and he reigned two years in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Meshulemes daughter of Charutz of Yotvoh.


###### II Kings 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:20)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה מְנַשֶּׁ֥ה אָבִֽיו׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, as Menashe his father had done.


###### II Kings 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:21)


וַיֵּ֕לֶךְ בְּכׇל־הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ אֲשֶׁר־הָלַ֣ךְ אָבִ֑יו וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֗ד אֶת־הַגִּלֻּלִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָבַ֣ד אָבִ֔יו וַיִּשְׁתַּ֖חוּ לָהֶֽם׃


He went in the same way as his father had gone, and he worshiped the pagan deities that his father had worshiped, and he prostrated himself to them.


###### II Kings 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:22)


וַיַּעֲזֹ֕ב אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתָ֑יו וְלֹ֥א הָלַ֖ךְ בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ יְהֹוָֽה׃


He forsook Adonoy God of his forefathers, and did not follow in the way of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 21:23

וַיִּקְשְׁר֥וּ עַבְדֵי־אָמ֖וֹן עָלָ֑יו וַיָּמִ֥יתוּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בְּבֵיתֽוֹ׃


Amon’s servants conspired against him and assassinated the king in his palace.


###### II Kings 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/21:24)


וַיַּךְ֙ עַם־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַקֹּשְׁרִ֖ים עַל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אָמ֑וֹן וַיַּמְלִ֧יכוּ עַם־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֶת־יֹאשִׁיָּ֥הוּ בְנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


The people of the land executed all the conspirators against king Amon; the people of the land appointed his son Yeshayohu in his place.

וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י אָמ֖וֹן אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


And the rest of Amon’s deeds that he did, are indeed written in the book of chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.
