He buried him in his grave in the garden of Uzza, and his son Yeshayohu reigned in his place.



## Chapter 22

###### II Kings 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:1)


בֶּן־שְׁמֹנֶ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ יֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וְאַחַת֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ יְדִידָ֥ה בַת־עֲדָיָ֖ה מִבָּֽצְקַֽת׃


Yoshiyahu was eight years old when he became king, and he reigned in Yerusholayim thirty-one years; his mother’s name was Yedidoh daughter of Adoyoh of Botzkas.


###### II Kings 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֵּ֗לֶךְ בְּכׇל־דֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ דָּוִ֣ד אָבִ֔יו וְלֹא־סָ֖ר יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹֽאול׃{פ}


He did what was right in the eyes of Adonoy, and he followed in all the way[s] of Dovid his father; he turned away neither right nor left.


###### II Kings 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:3)


וַיְהִ֗י בִּשְׁמֹנֶ֤ה עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ שָׁלַ֣ח הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ אֶת־שָׁפָ֨ן בֶּן־אֲצַלְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־מְשֻׁלָּם֙ הַסֹּפֵ֔ר בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it was in the eighteenth year of king Yoshiyahu, that the king sent Shophon son of Atzalyohu son of Meshullom, the scribe, to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, saying,


###### II Kings 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:4)


עֲלֵ֗ה אֶל־חִלְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ הַכֹּהֵ֣ן הַגָּד֔וֹל וְיַתֵּ֣ם אֶת־הַכֶּ֔סֶף הַמּוּבָ֖א בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָסְפ֛וּ שֹׁמְרֵ֥י הַסַּ֖ף מֵאֵ֥ת הָעָֽם׃


“Go up [and tell] Chilkiyohu the *kohein* *gadol* that he should gather all the silver that was brought to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, which the custodians of the utensils collected from the people.


###### II Kings 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:5)


(ויתנה) [וְיִתְּנוּ֗הוּ] עַל־יַד֙ עֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה הַמֻּפְקָדִ֖ים (בבית) [בֵּ֣ית] יְהֹוָ֑ה וְיִתְּנ֣וּ אֹת֗וֹ לְעֹשֵׂ֤י הַמְּלָאכָה֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה לְחַזֵּ֖ק בֶּ֥דֶק הַבָּֽיִת׃


Let them deliver it into the hands of the supervisors of the work, who are appointed in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and they should give it to the workers who are in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, to repair the damage of the Beis [Hamikdosh].


###### II Kings 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:6)


לֶחָ֣רָשִׁ֔ים וְלַבֹּנִ֖ים וְלַגֹּֽדְרִ֑ים וְלִקְנ֤וֹת עֵצִים֙ וְאַבְנֵ֣י מַחְצֵ֔ב לְחַזֵּ֖ק אֶת־הַבָּֽיִת׃


To the carpenters, to the builders, and to the masons; to buy wood and quarried stones to repair the Beis [Hamikdosh].


###### II Kings 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:7)


אַ֚ךְ לֹא־יֵחָשֵׁ֣ב אִתָּ֔ם הַכֶּ֖סֶף הַנִּתָּ֣ן עַל־יָדָ֑ם כִּ֥י בֶאֱמוּנָ֖ה הֵ֥ם עֹשִֽׂים׃


However, no reckoning should be made with them of the silver that is given into their hand, for they deal honestly.”


###### II Kings 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:8)


וַ֠יֹּ֠אמֶר חִלְקִיָּ֜הוּ הַכֹּהֵ֤ן הַגָּדוֹל֙ עַל־שָׁפָ֣ן הַסֹּפֵ֔ר סֵ֧פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֛ה מָצָ֖אתִי בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּתֵּ֨ן חִלְקִיָּ֧ה אֶת־הַסֵּ֛פֶר אֶל־שָׁפָ֖ן וַיִּקְרָאֵֽהוּ׃


Chilkiyohu the *kohein gadol* said to Shophon the scribe, “I have found the Torah Scroll in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy,” and Chilkiyoh gave the Scroll to Shophon, and he read it.


###### II Kings 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:9)


וַיָּבֹ֞א שָׁפָ֤ן הַסֹּפֵר֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַיָּ֥שֶׁב אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ דָּבָ֑ר וַיֹּ֗אמֶר הִתִּ֤יכוּ עֲבָדֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶת־הַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ הַנִּמְצָ֣א בַבַּ֔יִת וַֽיִּתְּנֻ֗הוּ עַל־יַד֙ עֹשֵׂ֣י הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה הַמֻּפְקָדִ֖ים בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Shophon the scribe came to the king and brought back word to the king, and said, “Your servants have melted the silver that was found in the Beis [Hamikdosh], and they have given it into the hand[s] of the supervisors of the work who were appointed over the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:10)


וַיַּגֵּ֞ד שָׁפָ֤ן הַסֹּפֵר֙ לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ לֵאמֹ֔ר סֵ֚פֶר נָ֣תַן לִ֔י חִלְקִיָּ֖ה הַכֹּהֵ֑ן וַיִּקְרָאֵ֥הוּ שָׁפָ֖ן לִפְנֵ֥י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


Shophon the scribe told the king, saying, “Chilkiyoh the kohein gave me a Scroll; and Shophon read it before the king.


###### II Kings 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:11)


וַֽיְהִי֙ כִּשְׁמֹ֣עַ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֖י סֵ֣פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֑ה וַיִּקְרַ֖ע אֶת־בְּגָדָֽיו׃


And it was when the king heard the words of the Torah Scroll, that he rent his garments.


###### II Kings 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:12)


וַיְצַ֣ו הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ אֶת־חִלְקִיָּ֣ה הַכֹּהֵ֡ן וְאֶת־אֲחִיקָ֣ם בֶּן־שָׁ֠פָ֠ן וְאֶת־עַכְבּ֨וֹר בֶּן־מִיכָיָ֜ה וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שָׁפָ֣ן הַסֹּפֵ֗ר וְאֵ֛ת עֲשָׂיָ֥ה עֶֽבֶד־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לֵאמֹֽר׃


The king commanded Chilkiyoh the *kohein*, Achikom son of Shophon, Achbor son of Michoyoh, Shophon the scribe, and Asoyoh the king’s servant, saying,


###### II Kings 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:13)


לְכוּ֩ דִרְשׁ֨וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֜ה בַּעֲדִ֣י וּבְעַד־הָעָ֗ם וּבְעַד֙ כׇּל־יְהוּדָ֔ה עַל־דִּבְרֵ֛י הַסֵּ֥פֶר הַנִּמְצָ֖א הַזֶּ֑ה כִּֽי־גְדוֹלָ֞ה חֲמַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁר־הִיא֙ נִצְּתָ֣ה בָ֔נוּ עַל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־שָׁמְע֜וּ אֲבֹתֵ֗ינוּ עַל־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַסֵּ֣פֶר הַזֶּ֔ה לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת כְּכׇל־הַכָּת֥וּב עָלֵֽינוּ׃


“Go, inquire of Adonoy on my behalf, on behalf of the people, and on behalf of all of Yehudah regarding the words of this Scroll which has been found; for great is the wrath of Adonoy which is kindled against us because our fathers did not observe the words of this Scroll, to do according to all that is written for us.”


###### II Kings 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:14)


וַיֵּ֣לֶךְ חִלְקִיָּ֣הוּ הַ֠כֹּהֵ֠ן וַאֲחִיקָ֨ם וְעַכְבּ֜וֹר וְשָׁפָ֣ן וַעֲשָׂיָ֗ה אֶל־חֻלְדָּ֨ה הַנְּבִיאָ֜ה אֵ֣שֶׁת**׀** שַׁלֻּ֣ם בֶּן־תִּקְוָ֗ה בֶּן־חַרְחַס֙ שֹׁמֵ֣ר הַבְּגָדִ֔ים וְהִ֛יא יֹשֶׁ֥בֶת בִּירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בַּמִּשְׁנֶ֑ה וַֽיְדַבְּר֖וּ אֵלֶֽיהָ׃


And they went— Chilkiyohu the *kohein*, Achikom, Achbor, Shophon and Asoyoh to Chuldoh the prophetess, the wife of Shallum son of Tikvoh son of Charchas, the keeper of the garments, and she was sitting in Yerusholayim in the study house, and they spoke to her.


###### II Kings 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:15)


וַתֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אִמְר֣וּ לָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־שָׁלַ֥ח אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֵלָֽי׃


She said to them, “So has Adonoy, God of Yisroel said, ‘Say to the man who sent you to me.


###### II Kings 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:16)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֨י מֵבִ֥יא רָעָ֛ה אֶל־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה וְעַל־יֹֽשְׁבָ֑יו אֵ֚ת כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֣י הַסֵּ֔פֶר אֲשֶׁ֥ר קָרָ֖א מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


So has Adonoy said, ‘Behold, I am bringing a calamity upon this place and upon its inhabitants, all the words of the Scroll that the king of Yehudah read.


###### II Kings 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:17)


תַּ֣חַת**׀** אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲזָב֗וּנִי וַֽיְקַטְּרוּ֙ לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים לְמַ֙עַן֙ הַכְעִיסֵ֔נִי בְּכֹ֖ל מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יְדֵיהֶ֑ם וְנִצְּתָ֧ה חֲמָתִ֛י בַּמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה וְלֹ֥א תִכְבֶּֽה׃


Because they have forsaken Me and have burned incense to the gods of others, in order to provoke Me with all their deeds, My wrath is kindled against this place, and it will not be extinguished.’


###### II Kings 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:18)


וְאֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֗ה הַשֹּׁלֵ֤חַ אֶתְכֶם֙ לִדְרֹ֣שׁ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְר֖וּ אֵלָ֑יו כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הַדְּבָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁמָֽעְתָּ׃


And concerning the king of Yehudah who has sent you to inquire of Adonoy, so shall you say to him, ‘So has Adonoy God of Yisroel said, “The words are what you heard.


###### II Kings 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:19)


יַ֠עַן רַךְ־לְבָ֨בְךָ֜ וַתִּכָּנַ֣ע**׀** מִפְּנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה בְּֽשׇׁמְעֲךָ֡ אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבַּ֩רְתִּי֩ עַל־הַמָּק֨וֹם הַזֶּ֜ה וְעַל־יֹשְׁבָ֗יו לִֽהְי֤וֹת לְשַׁמָּה֙ וְלִקְלָלָ֔ה וַתִּקְרַע֙ אֶת־בְּגָדֶ֔יךָ וַתִּבְכֶּ֖ה לְפָנָ֑י וְגַ֧ם אָנֹכִ֛י שָׁמַ֖עְתִּי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Because your heart has become soft, and you have humbled yourself before Adonoy when you heard what I spoke concerning this place and its inhabitants, to become a desolation and a curse, and you rent your garments and wept before Me; I, too, have heard,’ says Adonoy.


###### II Kings 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/22:20)


לָכֵן֩ הִנְנִ֨י אֹסִפְךָ֜ עַל־אֲבֹתֶ֗יךָ וְנֶאֱסַפְתָּ֣ אֶל־קִבְרֹתֶ֘יךָ֮ בְּשָׁלוֹם֒ וְלֹא־תִרְאֶ֣ינָה עֵינֶ֔יךָ בְּכֹל֙ הָרָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י מֵבִ֖יא עַל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וַיָּשִׁ֥בוּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ דָּבָֽר׃


Therefore, behold, I gather you in to your forefathers, and you will be gathered into your grave in peace; your eyes will not see any of the calamity that I am bringing upon this place.” And they brought back word to the king.



## Chapter 23

###### II Kings 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:1)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֖ח הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיַּאַסְפ֣וּ אֵלָ֔יו כׇּל־זִקְנֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


The king summoned, and they assembled before him all the elders of Yehudah and Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:2)


וַיַּ֣עַל הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֡ה וְכׇל־אִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָה֩ וְכׇל־יֹשְׁבֵ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם אִתּ֗וֹ וְהַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְהַנְּבִיאִ֔ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם לְמִקָּטֹ֣ן וְעַד־גָּד֑וֹל וַיִּקְרָ֣א בְאׇזְנֵיהֶ֗ם אֶת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵי֙ סֵ֣פֶר הַבְּרִ֔ית הַנִּמְצָ֖א בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


The king went up to the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, and all the people of Yehudah and all the inhabitants of Yerusholayim were with him, and the *kohanim* and the prophets, and all the people from small to great, and he read in their ears all the words of the Scroll of the Covenant that was found in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:3)


וַיַּעֲמֹ֣ד הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ עַֽל־הָ֨עַמּ֜וּד וַיִּכְרֹ֥ת אֶֽת־הַבְּרִ֣ית**׀** לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה לָלֶ֜כֶת אַחַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ וְלִשְׁמֹ֨ר מִצְוֺתָ֜יו וְאֶת־עֵדְוֺתָ֤יו וְאֶת־חֻקֹּתָיו֙ בְּכׇל־לֵ֣ב וּבְכׇל־נֶ֔פֶשׁ לְהָקִ֗ים אֶת־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֔את הַכְּתֻבִ֖ים עַל־הַסֵּ֣פֶר הַזֶּ֑ה וַיַּעֲמֹ֥ד כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם בַּבְּרִֽית׃


The king stood at his place, and enacted the Covenant before Adonoy, to follow Adonoy and to observe His commandments, His testimonies, and His statutes with all [their] heart and soul, to fulfill the words of this Covenant, which are written in this Scroll; and all the people accepted the Covenant.


###### II Kings 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:4)


וַיְצַ֣ו הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ אֶת־חִלְקִיָּ֩הוּ֩ הַכֹּהֵ֨ן הַגָּד֜וֹל וְאֶת־כֹּהֲנֵ֣י הַמִּשְׁנֶה֮ וְאֶת־שֹׁמְרֵ֣י הַסַּף֒ לְהוֹצִיא֙ מֵהֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֔ה אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַכֵּלִ֗ים הָֽעֲשׂוּיִם֙ לַבַּ֣עַל וְלָאֲשֵׁרָ֔ה וּלְכֹ֖ל צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַֽיִּשְׂרְפֵ֞ם מִח֤וּץ לִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ בְּשַׁדְמ֣וֹת קִדְר֔וֹן וְנָשָׂ֥א אֶת־עֲפָרָ֖ם בֵּֽית־אֵֽל׃


The king instructed Chilkiyohu the *kohein gadol*, the *kohanim*of the second rank and the gatekeepers, to remove from the Temple of Adonoy all the utensils that were made for the Baal, for the *asheiroh*, and for the entire host of the heavens; and he burned them outside Yerusholayim in the plains of Kidron, and he carried their ashes to Beis Eil.


###### II Kings 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:5)


וְהִשְׁבִּ֣ית אֶת־הַכְּמָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָֽתְנוּ֙ מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַיְקַטֵּ֤ר בַּבָּמוֹת֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וּמְסִבֵּ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְאֶת־הַֽמְקַטְּרִ֣ים לַבַּ֗עַל לַשֶּׁ֤מֶשׁ וְלַיָּרֵ֙חַ֙ וְלַמַּזָּל֔וֹת וּלְכֹ֖ל צְבָ֥א הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


He abolished the pagan priests who the kings of Yehudah had appointed and who had burned incense on the high places in the cities of Yehudah and the surrounding areas of Yerusholayim, and those who burned incense to the Baal, to the sun, to the moon, and to the constellations, and to all the host of the heavens.


###### II Kings 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:6)


וַיֹּצֵ֣א אֶת־הָאֲשֵׁרָה֩ מִבֵּ֨ית יְהֹוָ֜ה מִח֤וּץ לִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ אֶל־נַ֣חַל קִדְר֔וֹן וַיִּשְׂרֹ֥ף אֹתָ֛הּ בְּנַ֥חַל קִדְר֖וֹן וַיָּ֣דֶק לְעָפָ֑ר וַיַּשְׁלֵךְ֙ אֶת־עֲפָרָ֔הּ עַל־קֶ֖בֶר בְּנֵ֥י הָעָֽם׃


He removed the *asheiroh* from the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy to the Kidron Valley outside of Yerusholayim, and he burned it in the Kidron Valley and he ground it into dust; he threw its dust on the graves of the people.


###### II Kings 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:7)


וַיִּתֹּץ֙ אֶת־בָּתֵּ֣י הַקְּדֵשִׁ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר הַנָּשִׁ֗ים אֹרְג֥וֹת שָׁ֛ם בָּתִּ֖ים לָאֲשֵׁרָֽה׃


He demolished the houses devoted to pagan worship that were in the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, where the women would weave coverings for the *asheiroh*.


###### II Kings 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:8)


וַיָּבֵ֤א אֶת־כׇּל־הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ מֵעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַיְטַמֵּ֣א אֶת־הַבָּמ֗וֹת אֲשֶׁ֤ר קִטְּרֽוּ־שָׁ֙מָּה֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים מִגֶּ֖בַע עַד־בְּאֵ֣ר שָׁ֑בַע וְנָתַ֞ץ אֶת־בָּמ֣וֹת הַשְּׁעָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁר־פֶּ֜תַח שַׁ֤עַר יְהוֹשֻׁ֙עַ֙ שַׂר־הָעִ֔יר אֲשֶֽׁר־עַל־שְׂמֹ֥אול אִ֖ישׁ בְּשַׁ֥עַר הָעִֽיר׃


He brought all the priests from the towns of Yehudah, and he defiled the high places where the priests burned incense, from Geva to Be’er Sheva; he demolished the high places near the gates that were at the entrance of the gate of Yehoshua the governor of the city, which was on a person’s left [upon entering] the city gate.


###### II Kings 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:9)


אַ֗ךְ לֹ֤א יַעֲלוּ֙ כֹּהֲנֵ֣י הַבָּמ֔וֹת אֶל־מִזְבַּ֥ח יְהֹוָ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כִּ֛י אִם־אָכְל֥וּ מַצּ֖וֹת בְּת֥וֹךְ אֲחֵיהֶֽם׃


However, the priests of the high places were not [permitted] to ascend the altar of Adonoy in Yerusholayim; they would eat *matzohs* among their brethren.


###### II Kings 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:10)


וְטִמֵּ֣א אֶת־הַתֹּ֔פֶת אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּגֵ֣י (בני) [בֶן־]הִנֹּ֑ם לְבִלְתִּ֗י לְהַעֲבִ֨יר אִ֜ישׁ אֶת־בְּנ֧וֹ וְאֶת־בִּתּ֛וֹ בָּאֵ֖שׁ לַמֹּֽלֶךְ׃


He defiled the Tophes which was in the Valley of Ben Hinom, so that no man would pass his son or daughter through the fire to the Molech.


###### II Kings 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:11)


וַיַּשְׁבֵּ֣ת אֶת־הַסּוּסִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתְנוּ֩ מַלְכֵ֨י יְהוּדָ֤ה לַשֶּׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ מִבֹּ֣א בֵית־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־לִשְׁכַּת֙ נְתַן־מֶ֣לֶךְ הַסָּרִ֔יס אֲשֶׁ֖ר בַּפַּרְוָרִ֑ים וְאֶת־מַרְכְּב֥וֹת הַשֶּׁ֖מֶשׁ שָׂרַ֥ף בָּאֵֽשׁ׃


He abolished the horses that the kings of Yehudah had dedicated to the sun, from the entrance of the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy to the chamber of Neson Melech, the eunuch who was in the outskirts, and he burned the chariots of the sun in fire.


###### II Kings 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:12)


וְאֶת־הַֽמִּזְבְּח֡וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הַגָּג֩ עֲלִיַּ֨ת אָחָ֜ז אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֣וּ**׀** מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה וְאֶת־הַֽמִּזְבְּחוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֣ה מְנַשֶּׁ֗ה בִּשְׁתֵּ֛י חַצְר֥וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה נָתַ֣ץ הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיָּ֣רׇץ מִשָּׁ֔ם וְהִשְׁלִ֥יךְ אֶת־עֲפָרָ֖ם אֶל־נַ֥חַל קִדְרֽוֹן׃


The altars that were on the roof of the upper chamber of Achaz which the kings of Yehudah had made, and the altars that Menashe had made in the two courtyards of the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy, the king demolished; he quickly removed them from there and threw their dust into the Kidron Valley.


###### II Kings 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:13)


וְֽאֶת־הַבָּמ֞וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** עַל־פְּנֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם אֲשֶׁר֮ מִימִ֣ין לְהַר־הַמַּשְׁחִית֒ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּ֠נָ֠ה שְׁלֹמֹ֨ה מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל לְעַשְׁתֹּ֣רֶת**׀** שִׁקֻּ֣ץ צִידֹנִ֗ים וְלִכְמוֹשׁ֙ שִׁקֻּ֣ץ מוֹאָ֔ב וּלְמִלְכֹּ֖ם תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֑וֹן טִמֵּ֖א הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


And the high places that were facing Yerusholayim, that were on the right of Har Hamashchis, which Shlomo king of Yisroel had built for Ashtores the abomination of the Tzidonim, and for Chemosh the abomination of Moav, and for Milkom the abomination of the children of Amon, the king defiled.


###### II Kings 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:14)


וְשִׁבַּר֙ אֶת־הַמַּצֵּב֔וֹת וַיִּכְרֹ֖ת אֶת־הָאֲשֵׁרִ֑ים וַיְמַלֵּ֥א אֶת־מְקוֹמָ֖ם עַצְמ֥וֹת אָדָֽם׃


He smashed the monuments, and cut down the *asheirim*, and he filled their place with human bones.


###### II Kings 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:15)


וְגַ֨ם אֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֜חַ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּבֵֽית־אֵ֗ל הַבָּמָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה יָרׇבְעָ֤ם בֶּן־נְבָט֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֶחֱטִ֣יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל גַּ֣ם אֶת־הַמִּזְבֵּ֧חַ הַה֛וּא וְאֶת־הַבָּמָ֖ה נָתָ֑ץ וַיִּשְׂרֹ֧ף אֶת־הַבָּמָ֛ה הֵדַ֥ק לְעָפָ֖ר וְשָׂרַ֥ף אֲשֵׁרָֽה׃


Also, the altar that was in Beis Eil, the high place that Yorovom son of Nevot made, with which he caused Yisroel to sin, also that altar and the high place he demolished, and he burned the high place; he ground it into dust and burned the*asheiroh*.


###### II Kings 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:16)


וַיִּ֣פֶן יֹאשִׁיָּ֗הוּ וַיַּ֨רְא אֶת־הַקְּבָרִ֤ים אֲשֶׁר־שָׁם֙ בָּהָ֔ר וַיִּשְׁלַ֗ח וַיִּקַּ֤ח אֶת־הָֽעֲצָמוֹת֙ מִן־הַקְּבָרִ֔ים וַיִּשְׂרֹ֥ף עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּ֖חַ וַֽיְטַמְּאֵ֑הוּ כִּדְבַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר קָרָא֙ אִ֣ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר קָרָ֔א אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


Yoshiyahu turned and saw the graves that were there on the mountain, and he sent [people] and took the bones out of the graves, and he burned them on the altar and defiled it; according to the word of Adonoy that the man of God had proclaimed, who had foretold these events.


###### II Kings 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:17)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר מָ֚ה הַצִּיּ֣וּן הַלָּ֔ז אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֲנִ֣י רֹאֶ֑ה וַיֹּאמְר֨וּ אֵלָ֜יו אַנְשֵׁ֣י הָעִ֗יר הַקֶּ֤בֶר אִישׁ־הָֽאֱלֹהִים֙ אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣א מִֽיהוּדָ֔ה וַיִּקְרָ֗א אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔יתָ עַ֖ל הַמִּזְבַּ֥ח בֵּֽית־אֵֽל׃


And he said, “What is this marker that I see?” And the people of the city said to him, “It is the grave of the man of God who came from Yehudah and foretold things that you have done on the altar of Beis Eil.”


###### II Kings 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:18)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ הַנִּ֣יחוּ ל֔וֹ אִ֖ישׁ אַל־יָנַ֣ע עַצְמוֹתָ֑יו וַֽיְמַלְּטוּ֙ עַצְמוֹתָ֔יו אֵ֚ת עַצְמ֣וֹת הַנָּבִ֔יא אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֖א מִשֹּׁמְרֽוֹן׃


And he said, “Leave it alone. Let no one move his bones.” They spared his bones with the bones of the prophet who came from Shomron.


###### II Kings 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:19)


וְגַם֩ אֶת־כׇּל־בָּתֵּ֨י הַבָּמ֜וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** בְּעָרֵ֣י שֹׁמְר֗וֹן אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֜וּ מַלְכֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לְהַכְעִ֔יס הֵסִ֖יר יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ וַיַּ֣עַשׂ לָהֶ֔ם כְּכׇל־הַֽמַּעֲשִׂ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה בְּבֵֽית־אֵֽל׃


And also all the temples of the high places in the cities of Shomron, that Yisroel’s kings had made to anger [Adonoy], Yoshiyahu removed, and he did to them like all the deeds he had done in Beis Eil.


###### II Kings 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:20)


וַ֠יִּזְבַּ֠ח אֶת־כׇּל־כֹּהֲנֵ֨י הַבָּמ֤וֹת אֲשֶׁר־שָׁם֙ עַל־הַֽמִּזְבְּח֔וֹת וַיִּשְׂרֹ֛ף אֶת־עַצְמ֥וֹת אָדָ֖ם עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וַיָּ֖שׇׁב יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


He slaughtered all the priests of the high places who were there on the altars, and he burned human bones upon them, and then he returned to Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:21)


וַיְצַ֤ו הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָעָ֣ם לֵאמֹ֔ר עֲשׂ֣וּ פֶ֔סַח לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם כַּכָּת֕וּב עַ֛ל סֵ֥פֶר הַבְּרִ֖ית הַזֶּֽה׃


The king commanded all the people, saying, “Offer a Pesach sacrifice to Adonoy you God, as written in the Book of the Covenant.”


###### II Kings 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:22)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א נַֽעֲשָׂה֙ כַּפֶּ֣סַח הַזֶּ֔ה מִימֵי֙ הַשֹּׁ֣פְטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁפְט֖וּ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְכֹ֗ל יְמֵ֛י מַלְכֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וּמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


For such a Pesach sacrifice had not been offered since the time of the Judges who judged Yisroel, and all the days of the kings of Yisroel and the kings of Yehudah.


###### II Kings 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:23)


כִּ֗י אִם־בִּשְׁמֹנֶ֤ה עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ נַעֲשָׂ֞ה הַפֶּ֧סַח הַזֶּ֛ה לַיהֹוָ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Except in the eighteenth year of king Yoshiyahu, this Pesach sacrifice was offered to Adonoy, in Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:24)


וְגַ֣ם אֶת־הָאֹב֣וֹת וְאֶת־הַ֠יִּדְּעֹנִ֠ים וְאֶת־הַתְּרָפִ֨ים וְאֶת־הַגִּלֻּלִ֜ים וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַשִּׁקֻּצִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר נִרְאוּ֙ בְּאֶ֤רֶץ יְהוּדָה֙ וּבִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם בִּעֵ֖ר יֹאשִׁיָּ֑הוּ לְ֠מַ֠עַן הָקִ֞ים אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֤י הַתּוֹרָה֙ הַכְּתֻבִ֣ים עַל־הַסֵּ֔פֶר אֲשֶׁ֥ר מָצָ֛א חִלְקִיָּ֥הוּ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Also, the necromancers and those who divine by the Yidoa bone and the *teraphim* and the idols, and all the abominations that were seen in the land of Yehudah and in Yerusholayim, Yoshiyahu abolished, in order to fulfill the words of the Torah which were written in the Scroll that Chilkiyohu the *kohein* had found [in] the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy.


###### II Kings 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:25)


וְכָמֹ֩הוּ֩ לֹא־הָיָ֨ה לְפָנָ֜יו מֶ֗לֶךְ אֲשֶׁר־שָׁ֤ב אֶל־יְהֹוָה֙ בְּכׇל־לְבָב֤וֹ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשׁוֹ֙ וּבְכׇל־מְאֹד֔וֹ כְּכֹ֖ל תּוֹרַ֣ת מֹשֶׁ֑ה וְאַחֲרָ֖יו לֹא־קָ֥ם כָּמֹֽהוּ׃


Before him there was never a king like him, who returned to Adonoy with all his heart, with all his soul, and with all his possessions, according to the entire Torah of Moshe, and after him no one arose like him.


###### II Kings 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:26)


אַ֣ךְ**׀** לֹא־שָׁ֣ב יְהֹוָ֗ה מֵחֲר֤וֹן אַפּוֹ֙ הַגָּד֔וֹל אֲשֶׁר־חָרָ֥ה אַפּ֖וֹ בִּֽיהוּדָ֑ה עַ֚ל כׇּל־הַכְּעָסִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִכְעִיס֖וֹ מְנַשֶּֽׁה׃


However, Adonoy did not turn back from His great wrath, His wrath that was kindled against Yehudah, because of all the provocations with which Menashe had provoked Him.


###### II Kings 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:27)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֗ה גַּ֤ם אֶת־יְהוּדָה֙ אָסִיר֙ מֵעַ֣ל פָּנַ֔י כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר הֲסִרֹ֖תִי אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּ֠מָאַסְתִּ֠י אֶת־הָעִ֨יר הַזֹּ֤את אֲשֶׁר־בָּחַ֙רְתִּי֙ אֶת־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְאֶ֨ת־הַבַּ֔יִת אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָמַ֔רְתִּי יִֽהְיֶ֥ה שְׁמִ֖י שָֽׁם׃


Adonoy said, “Yehudah, too, I will remove from My Presence, as I have removed Yisroel, and I will reject this city which I have chosen, Yerusholayim, and the Beis [Hamikdosh] of which I said, ‘My Name will be there.’”


###### II Kings 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:28)


וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יֹאשִׁיָּ֖הוּ וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


The rest of the deeds of Yoshiyahu and all that he did, are written in the book of the chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.


###### II Kings 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:29)


בְּיָמָ֡יו עָלָה֩ פַרְעֹ֨ה נְכֹ֧ה מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֛יִם עַל־מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּׁ֖וּר עַל־נְהַר־פְּרָ֑ת וַיֵּ֨לֶךְ הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ יֹאשִׁיָּ֙הוּ֙ לִקְרָאת֔וֹ וַיְמִיתֵ֙הוּ֙ בִּמְגִדּ֔וֹ כִּרְאֹת֖וֹ אֹתֽוֹ׃


In his days, Paroh Neko king of Egypt went up against the king of Ashur by the Euphrates River; King Yoshiyahu went toward him, and he [Paroh Neko] killed him in Megiddo when he saw him.


###### II Kings 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:30)


וַיַּרְכִּבֻ֨הוּ עֲבָדָ֥יו מֵת֙ מִמְּגִדּ֔וֹ וַיְבִאֻ֙הוּ֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַֽיִּקְבְּרֻ֖הוּ בִּקְבֻרָת֑וֹ וַיִּקַּ֣ח עַם־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֶת־יְהֽוֹאָחָז֙ בֶּן־יֹ֣אשִׁיָּ֔הוּ וַיִּמְשְׁח֥וּ אֹת֛וֹ וַיַּמְלִ֥יכוּ אֹת֖וֹ תַּ֥חַת אָבִֽיו׃{פ}


His servants transported him; dead, from Megiddo, they brought him to Yerusholayim, and buried him in his grave. The people of the land took Yehoachaz son of Yoshiyahu, anointed him, and made him king in place of his father.


###### II Kings 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:31)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְשָׁלֹ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ יְהוֹאָחָ֣ז בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה חֳדָשִׁ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירֽוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ חֲמוּטַ֥ל בַּֽת־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ מִלִּבְנָֽה׃


Yehoachaz was twenty-three years old when he became king, and he reigned three months in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Chamutal daughter of Yirmiyahu of Livnoh.


###### II Kings 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:32)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֖וּ אֲבֹתָֽיו׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, like all that his forefathers had done.


###### II Kings 23:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:33)


וַיַּאַסְרֵ֩הוּ֩ פַרְעֹ֨ה נְכֹ֤ה בְרִבְלָה֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חֲמָ֔ת (במלך) [מִמְּלֹ֖ךְ] בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיִּתֶּן־עֹ֙נֶשׁ֙ עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ מֵאָ֥ה כִכַּר־כֶּ֖סֶף וְכִכַּ֥ר זָהָֽב׃


Paroh Neko imprisoned him in Rivloh in the land of Chamos, [to prevent him] from reigning in Yerusholayim; he imposed a fine on the land [consisting] of one hundred talents of silver and a talent of gold.


###### II Kings 23:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:34)


וַיַּמְלֵךְ֩ פַּרְעֹ֨ה נְכֹ֜ה אֶת־אֶלְיָקִ֣ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֗הוּ תַּ֚חַת יֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ אָבִ֔יו וַיַּסֵּ֥ב אֶת־שְׁמ֖וֹ יְהוֹיָקִ֑ים וְאֶת־יְהוֹאָחָ֣ז לָקַ֔ח וַיָּבֹ֥א מִצְרַ֖יִם וַיָּ֥מׇת שָֽׁם׃


Paroh Neko crowned Elyokim son of Yoshiyahu in place of his father Yoshiyahu, and he changed his name to Yehoyakim. He took Yehoachaz [captive], and came back to Egypt; and [Yehoachaz] died there.


###### II Kings 23:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:35)


וְהַכֶּ֣סֶף וְהַזָּהָ֗ב נָתַ֤ן יְהֽוֹיָקִים֙ לְפַרְעֹ֔ה אַ֚ךְ הֶעֱרִ֣יךְ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ לָתֵ֥ת אֶת־הַכֶּ֖סֶף עַל־פִּ֣י פַרְעֹ֑ה אִ֣ישׁ כְּעֶרְכּ֗וֹ נָגַ֞שׂ אֶת־הַכֶּ֤סֶף וְאֶת־הַזָּהָב֙ אֶת־עַ֣ם הָאָ֔רֶץ לָתֵ֖ת לְפַרְעֹ֥ה נְכֹֽה׃{ס}


Yehoyakim gave the silver and gold to Paroh, but he assessed the land in order to give the silver that Paroh had demanded; each one according to his assessment, he exacted the silver and gold from the people of the land to give to Paroh Neko.


###### II Kings 23:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:36)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְחָמֵ֤שׁ שָׁנָה֙ יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ (זבידה) [זְבוּדָּ֥ה] בַת־פְּדָיָ֖ה מִן־רוּמָֽה׃


Yehoyakim was twenty-five years old when he became king, and he reigned eleven years in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Zevudoh daughter of Pedoyoh of Rumoh.


###### II Kings 23:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/23:37)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֖וּ אֲבֹתָֽיו׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, like all that his forefathers had done.



## Chapter 24

###### II Kings 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:1)


בְּיָמָ֣יו עָלָ֔ה נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֖ר מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֑ל וַיְהִי־ל֨וֹ יְהוֹיָקִ֥ים עֶ֙בֶד֙ שָׁלֹ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֔ים וַיָּ֖שׇׁב וַיִּמְרׇד־בּֽוֹ׃


In his days, Nevuchadnetzar king of Bavel went up, and Yehoyakim became his vassal for three years, then he turned against him and rebelled.


###### II Kings 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:2)


וַיְשַׁלַּ֣ח יְהֹוָ֣ה׀בּ֡וֹ אֶת־גְּדוּדֵ֣י כַשְׂדִּים֩ וְאֶת־גְּדוּדֵ֨י אֲרָ֜ם וְאֵ֣ת**׀** גְּדוּדֵ֣י מוֹאָ֗ב וְאֵת֙ גְּדוּדֵ֣י בְנֵֽי־עַמּ֔וֹן וַיְשַׁלְּחֵ֥ם בִּיהוּדָ֖ה לְהַאֲבִיד֑וֹ כִּדְבַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּ֔ר בְּיַ֖ד עֲבָדָ֥יו הַנְּבִיאִֽים׃


Adonoy incited against him bands of Kasdim, bands of Aromeans, bands of Moavites, and bands of the children of Ammon, and he incited them against Yehudah to destroy it, according to the word of Adonoy which He had spoken through His servants, the prophets.


###### II Kings 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:3)


אַ֣ךְ**׀** עַל־פִּ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה הָֽיְתָה֙ בִּֽיהוּדָ֔ה לְהָסִ֖יר מֵעַ֣ל פָּנָ֑יו בְּחַטֹּ֣את מְנַשֶּׁ֔ה כְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָֽׂה׃


Indeed, it was by the order of Adonoy against Yehudah to remove [them] from His Presence, because of the sins of Menashe, according to all that he had done.


###### II Kings 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:4)


וְגַ֤ם דַּֽם־הַנָּקִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁפָ֔ךְ וַיְמַלֵּ֥א אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם דָּ֣ם נָקִ֑י וְלֹא־אָבָ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה לִסְלֹֽחַ׃


And also [because of] the innocent blood that he had shed, and he had filled Yerusholayim with innocent blood, and Adonoy did not want to forgive.


###### II Kings 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:5)


וְיֶ֛תֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י יְהוֹיָקִ֖ים וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֑ה הֲלֹא־הֵ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֗ים עַל־סֵ֛פֶר דִּבְרֵ֥י הַיָּמִ֖ים לְמַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


The rest of the deeds of Yehoyakim and all that he did are written in the book of the chronicles of Yehudah’s kings.


###### II Kings 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:6)


וַיִּשְׁכַּ֥ב יְהוֹיָקִ֖ים עִם־אֲבֹתָ֑יו וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ יְהוֹיָכִ֥ין בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


Yehoyakim lay with his forefathers, and Yehoyachin his son reigned in his place.


###### II Kings 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:7)


וְלֹא־הֹסִ֥יף עוֹד֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ מִצְרַ֔יִם לָצֵ֖את מֵֽאַרְצ֑וֹ כִּֽי־לָקַ֞ח מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֗ל מִנַּ֤חַל מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ עַד־נְהַר־פְּרָ֔ת כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָיְתָ֖ה לְמֶ֥לֶךְ מִצְרָֽיִם׃{פ}


The king of Egypt no longer left his land, for the king of Bavel had taken from the River of Egypt until the Euphrates River all [the territory] that belonged to the king of Egypt.


###### II Kings 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:8)


בֶּן־שְׁמֹנֶ֨ה עֶשְׂרֵ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ יְהוֹיָכִ֣ין בְּמׇלְכ֔וֹ וּשְׁלֹשָׁ֣ה חֳדָשִׁ֔ים מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירֽוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ נְחֻשְׁתָּ֥א בַת־אֶלְנָתָ֖ן מִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Yehoyachin was eighteen years old when he became king and he reigned three months in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Nechushta daughter of Elnoson of Yerusholayim.


###### II Kings 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:9)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה אָבִֽיו׃


And he did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy like all that his father had done.


###### II Kings 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:10)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔יא (עלה) [עָל֗וּ] עַבְדֵ֛י נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַתָּבֹ֥א הָעִ֖יר בַּמָּצֽוֹר׃


At that time, Nevuchadnetzar’s servants went up against Yerusholayim and the city came under siege.


###### II Kings 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:11)


וַיָּבֹ֛א נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל עַל־הָעִ֑יר וַעֲבָדָ֖יו צָרִ֥ים עָלֶֽיהָ׃


Nevuchadnetzar king of Bavel came to the city, while his servants were besieging it.


###### II Kings 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:12)


וַיֵּצֵ֞א יְהוֹיָכִ֤ין מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֙ עַל־מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל ה֣וּא וְאִמּ֔וֹ וַעֲבָדָ֖יו וְשָׂרָ֣יו וְסָרִיסָ֑יו וַיִּקַּ֤ח אֹתוֹ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל בִּשְׁנַ֥ת שְׁמֹנֶ֖ה לְמׇלְכֽוֹ׃


Yehoyachin, king of Yehudah came out to the king of Bavel, he, his mother his servants his officers and his warriors; and Bavel’s king took him [captive] in the eighth year of his reign.


###### II Kings 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:13)


וַיּוֹצֵ֣א מִשָּׁ֗ם אֶת־כׇּל־אֽוֹצְרוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאוֹצְר֖וֹת בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיְקַצֵּ֞ץ אֶת־כׇּל־כְּלֵ֣י הַזָּהָ֗ב אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה שְׁלֹמֹ֤ה מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ בְּהֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


He removed from there all the treasures of the Beis [Hamikdosh] of Adonoy and the treasures of the king’s palace; and he stripped off all the golden decorations that Shlomo king of Yisroel had made in the Temple of Adonoy, as Adonoy had spoken.


###### II Kings 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:14)


וְהִגְלָ֣ה אֶת־כׇּל־יְ֠רוּשָׁלַ֠͏ִם וְֽאֶת־כׇּל־הַשָּׂרִ֞ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־גִּבּוֹרֵ֣י הַחַ֗יִל (עשרה) [עֲשֶׂ֤רֶת] אֲלָפִים֙ גּוֹלֶ֔ה וְכׇל־הֶחָרָ֖שׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּ֑ר לֹ֣א נִשְׁאַ֔ר זוּלַ֖ת דַּלַּ֥ת עַם־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


He exiled all of Yerusholayim, all the officers, and all the mighty warriors, ten thousand exiles, and all the craftsmen and gatekeepers; no one remained except for the poorest of the people of the land.


###### II Kings 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:15)


וַיֶּ֥גֶל אֶת־יְהוֹיָכִ֖ין בָּבֶ֑לָה וְאֶת־אֵ֣ם הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ וְאֶת־נְשֵׁ֨י הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ וְאֶת־סָרִיסָ֗יו וְאֵת֙ (אולי) [אֵילֵ֣י] הָאָ֔רֶץ הוֹלִ֛יךְ גּוֹלָ֥ה מִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בָּבֶֽלָה׃


He exiled Yehoyachin to Bavel; and the king’s mother, the king’s wives, his officers and the dignitaries of the land, he led into exile from Yerusholayim to Bavel.


###### II Kings 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:16)


וְאֵת֩ כׇּל־אַנְשֵׁ֨י הַחַ֜יִל שִׁבְעַ֣ת אֲלָפִ֗ים וְהֶחָרָ֤שׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר֙ אֶ֔לֶף הַכֹּ֕ל גִּבּוֹרִ֖ים עֹשֵׂ֣י מִלְחָמָ֑ה וַיְבִיאֵ֧ם מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל גּוֹלָ֖ה בָּבֶֽלָה׃


All the military men, seven thousand, and the craftsmen and the gatekeepers, one thousand, all mighty warriors, the king of Bavel brought them into exile, to Bavel.


###### II Kings 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:17)


וַיַּמְלֵ֧ךְ מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל אֶת־מַתַּנְיָ֥ה דֹד֖וֹ תַּחְתָּ֑יו וַיַּסֵּ֥ב אֶת־שְׁמ֖וֹ צִדְקִיָּֽהוּ׃{פ}


The king of Bavel crowned Matanyoh his [Yehoyachin’s] uncle in his place, and changed his name to Tzidkiyohu.


###### II Kings 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:18)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְאַחַ֤ת שָׁנָה֙ צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ (חמיטל) [חֲמוּטַ֥ל] בַּֽת־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ מִלִּבְנָֽה׃


Tzidkiyohu was twenty-one years old when he became king, and he reigned twenty-one years in Yerusholayim; his mother’s name was Chamutal daughter of Yirmiyahu from Livnoh.


###### II Kings 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:19)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה יְהוֹיָקִֽים׃


He did what was evil in the eyes of Adonoy, like all that Yehoyakim had done.


###### II Kings 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/24:20)


כִּ֣י**׀** עַל־אַ֣ף יְהֹוָ֗ה הָיְתָ֤ה בִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וּבִ֣יהוּדָ֔ה עַד־הִשְׁלִכ֥וֹ אֹתָ֖ם מֵעַ֣ל פָּנָ֑יו וַיִּמְרֹ֥ד צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּמֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃{ס}


Indeed, the wrath of Adonoy was against Yerusholayim and Yehudah until He cast them away from before His Presence; Tzidkiyohu rebelled against the king of Bavel.



## Chapter 25

###### II Kings 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:1)


וַיְהִי֩ בִשְׁנַ֨ת הַתְּשִׁיעִ֜ית לְמׇלְכ֗וֹ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָעֲשִׂירִי֮ בֶּעָשׂ֣וֹר לַחֹ֒דֶשׁ֒ בָּ֠א נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֨ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֜ל ה֧וּא וְכׇל־חֵיל֛וֹ עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וַיִּ֣חַן עָלֶ֑יהָ וַיִּבְנ֥וּ עָלֶ֛יהָ דָּיֵ֖ק סָבִֽיב׃


It was in the ninth year of his [Tzidkiyohu’s] reign, in the tenth month, on the tenth of the month, that Nevuchadnetzar king of Bavel came, he and his entire army, against Yerusholayim and encamped near it, built siege towers around it.


###### II Kings 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:2)


וַתָּבֹ֥א הָעִ֖יר בַּמָּצ֑וֹר עַ֚ד עַשְׁתֵּ֣י עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ צִדְקִיָּֽהוּ׃


The city came under siege until the eleventh year of king Tzidkiyohu.


###### II Kings 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:3)


בְּתִשְׁעָ֣ה לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ וַיֶּחֱזַ֥ק הָרָעָ֖ב בָּעִ֑יר וְלֹא־הָ֥יָה לֶ֖חֶם לְעַ֥ם הָאָֽרֶץ׃


On the ninth of the [fourth] month, the famine in the city became severe, and the people of the land had no food.


###### II Kings 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:4)


וַתִּבָּקַ֣ע הָעִ֗יר וְכׇל־אַנְשֵׁ֨י הַמִּלְחָמָ֤ה׀הַלַּ֙יְלָה֙ דֶּ֜רֶךְ שַׁ֣עַר**׀** בֵּ֣ין הַחֹמֹתַ֗יִם אֲשֶׁר֙ עַל־גַּ֣ן הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְכַשְׂדִּ֥ים עַל־הָעִ֖יר סָבִ֑יב וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ דֶּ֥רֶךְ הָעֲרָבָֽה׃


The city [wall] was breached, and all the men of war [fled] at night by way of the gate between the two walls that was near the king’s garden; the Kasdim were surrounding the city, and he [Tzidkiyohu] went by way of the Arovoh.


###### II Kings 25:5

וַיְהִי֩ בִשְׁלֹשִׁ֨ים וָשֶׁ֜בַע שָׁנָ֗ה לְגָלוּת֙ יְהוֹיָכִ֣ין מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֔ה בִּשְׁנֵ֤ים עָשָׂר֙ חֹ֔דֶשׁ בְּעֶשְׂרִ֥ים וְשִׁבְעָ֖ה לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ נָשָׂ֡א אֱוִ֣יל מְרֹדַךְ֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ בָּבֶ֜ל בִּשְׁנַ֣ת מׇלְכ֗וֹ אֶת־רֹ֛אשׁ יְהוֹיָכִ֥ין מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֖ה מִבֵּ֥ית כֶּֽלֶא׃


And it was in the thirty-seventh year of the exile of Yehoyachin king of Yehudah, in the twelfth month, on the twenty-seventh day of the month, that Evil Merodach king of Bavel elevated in the year of his coronation Yehoyachin king of Yehudah [and released him] from prison.


###### II Kings 25:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:28)


וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר אִתּ֖וֹ טֹב֑וֹת וַיִּתֵּן֙ אֶת־כִּסְא֔וֹ מֵעַ֗ל כִּסֵּ֧א הַמְּלָכִ֛ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתּ֖וֹ בְּבָבֶֽל׃


He spoke kindly with him, and placed his throne above the throne of the kings who were with him in Bavel.


###### II Kings 25:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:29)


וְשִׁנָּ֕א אֵ֖ת בִּגְדֵ֣י כִלְא֑וֹ וְאָכַ֨ל לֶ֧חֶם תָּמִ֛יד לְפָנָ֖יו כׇּל־יְמֵ֥י חַיָּֽיו׃


He changed his prison clothing, and he ate meals before him regularly, for the rest of his life.


###### II Kings 25:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/II%20Kings/r/25:30)


וַאֲרֻחָת֗וֹ אֲרֻחַ֨ת תָּמִ֧יד נִתְּנָה־לּ֛וֹ מֵאֵ֥ת הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ דְּבַר־י֣וֹם בְּיוֹמ֑וֹ כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֥י חַיָּֽו׃


His food allowance was a regular allowance, given to him from the king, each day’s need on its day, for the rest of his life.
